# Consolidate Transcript Parsing

## Context

5 duplicate JSONL parsers across 3 packages. Consolidate into the shared `transcript` package using the `bufio.Reader` approach (no size limit).

## Plan

### Step 1: Add `ParseFromFileAtLine` to `transcript/parse.go`

Move `cli.parseTranscriptFromLine` implementation here. One function, no convenience wrappers.

```go
func ParseFromFileAtLine(path string, startLine int) ([]Line, int, error)
```

### Step 2: Delete duplicates and update callers

**Delete from `cli/transcript.go`:**
- `parseTranscript(path)` → callers use `transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, 0)`, ignore totalLines
- `parseTranscriptFromLine(path, startLine)` → callers use `transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)`
- `parseTranscriptFromBytes(content)` → callers use `transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)` (already exists)

**Delete from `claudecode/transcript.go`:**
- `ParseTranscript(data)` → callers use `transcript.ParseFromBytes(data)`
- `parseTranscriptFromLine(path, startLine)` → callers use `transcript.ParseFromFileAtLine(path, startLine)`
- `scannerBufferSize` constant → removed

### Step 3: Move parsing tests to `transcript/parse_test.go`

Move `TestParseTranscript_*` and `TestParseTranscriptFromLine_*` from `cli/transcript_test.go`. Keep extract/utility tests in place.

### Step 4: Clean up imports

## Key files

- `cmd/entire/cli/transcript/parse.go` — add `ParseFromFileAtLine`
- `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go` — delete 3 functions
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` — delete 2 functions + constant

    14→// ParseFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.
    15→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    16→func ParseFromBytes(content []byte) ([]Line, error) {
    17→	var lines []Line
    18→	reader := bufio.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(content))
    19→
    20→	for {
    21→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    22→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    23→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    24→		}
    25→
    26→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    27→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    28→			if err == io.EOF {
    29→				break
    30→			}
    31→			continue
    32→		}
    33→
    34→		var line Line
    35→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    36→			lines = append(lines, line)
    37→		}
    38→
    39→		if err == io.EOF {
    40→			break
    41→		}
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	return lines, nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// SliceFromLine returns the content starting from line number `startLine` (0-indexed).
    48→// This is used to extract only the checkpoint-specific portion of a cumulative transcript.
    49→// For example, if startLine is 2, lines 0 and 1 are skipped and the result starts at line 2.
    50→// Returns empty slice if startLine exceeds the number of lines.
    51→func SliceFromLine(content []byte, startLine int) []byte {
    52→	if len(content) == 0 || startLine <= 0 {
    53→		return content
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Find the byte offset where startLine begins
    57→	lineCount := 0
    58→	offset := 0
    59→	for i, b := range content {
    60→		if b == '\n' {
    61→			lineCount++
    62→			if lineCount == startLine {
    63→				offset = i + 1
    64→				break
    65→			}
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// If we didn't find enough lines, return empty
    70→	if lineCount < startLine {
    71→		return nil
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// If offset is beyond content, return empty
    75→	if offset >= len(content) {
    76→		return nil
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	return content[offset:]
    80→}
    81→
    82→// ExtractUserContent extracts user content from a raw message.
    83→// Handles both string and array content formats.
    84→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
    85→// Returns empty string if the message cannot be parsed or contains no text.
    86→func ExtractUserContent(message json.RawMessage) string {
    87→	var msg UserMessage
    88→	if err := json.Unmarshal(message, &msg); err != nil {
    89→		return ""
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Handle string content
    93→	if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
    94→		return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Handle array content (only if it contains text blocks)
    98→	if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
    99→		var texts []string
   100→		for _, item := range arr {
   101→			if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   102→				if m["type"] == ContentTypeText {
   103→					if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   104→						texts = append(texts, text)
   105→					}
   106→				}
   107→			}
   108→		}
   109→		if len(texts) > 0 {
   110→			return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return ""
   115→}
   116→

    25→// parseTranscript reads and parses a Claude Code transcript file.
    26→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    27→func parseTranscript(path string) ([]transcriptLine, error) {
    28→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
    31→	}
    32→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    33→
    34→	var lines []transcriptLine
    35→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
    36→
    37→	for {
    38→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    39→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    40→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    41→		}
    42→
    43→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    44→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    45→			if err == io.EOF {
    46→				break
    47→			}
    48→			continue
    49→		}
    50→
    51→		var line transcriptLine
    52→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    53→			lines = append(lines, line)
    54→		}
    55→
    56→		if err == io.EOF {
    57→			break
    58→		}
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return lines, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// parseTranscriptFromLine reads and parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
    65→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    66→// Returns:
    67→//   - lines: parsed transcript lines from startLine onwards (malformed lines skipped)
    68→//   - totalLines: total number of lines in the file (including malformed ones)
    69→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
    70→//
    71→// The startLine parameter is 0-indexed (startLine=0 reads from the beginning).
    72→// This is useful for incremental parsing when you've already processed some lines.
    73→func parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
    74→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path is a controlled transcript file path
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    79→
    80→	var lines []transcriptLine
    81→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
    82→
    83→	totalLines := 0
    84→	for {
    85→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    86→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    87→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    88→		}
    89→
    90→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    91→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    92→			if err == io.EOF {
    93→				break
    94→			}
    95→			continue
    96→		}
    97→
    98→		// Count all lines for totalLines, but only parse after startLine
    99→		if totalLines >= startLine {
   100→			var line transcriptLine
   101→			if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
   102→				lines = append(lines, line)
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→		totalLines++
   106→
   107→		if err == io.EOF {
   108→			break
   109→		}
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	return lines, totalLines, nil
   113→}
   114→
   115→// parseTranscriptFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.
   116→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
   117→func parseTranscriptFromBytes(content []byte) ([]transcriptLine, error) {
   118→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→	return lines, nil
   123→}
   124→
   125→// PromptResponsePair represents a user prompt and the assistant's responses.
   126→// Used for extracting all interactions from a transcript.
   127→type PromptResponsePair struct {
   128→	Prompt    string
   129→	Responses []string // Multiple responses can occur between tool calls
   130→	Files     []string
   131→}
   132→
   133→// ExtractAllPromptResponses extracts all user prompt and assistant response pairs from a transcript.
   134→// Returns pairs in chronological order (oldest first).
   135→// Each pair contains the user's prompt, assistant's text response, and files modified.
   136→func ExtractAllPromptResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []PromptResponsePair {
   137→	var pairs []PromptResponsePair
   138→
   139→	// Find all user prompts (messages with string content, not tool results)
   140→	var userIndices []int
   141→	for i, line := range transcript {
   142→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeUser {
   143→			var msg userMessage
   144→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   145→				continue
   146→			}
   147→			// Only count messages with string content (real user prompts)
   148→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   149→				userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
   150→			} else if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   151→				// Check if array has text blocks (also a real user prompt)
   152→				for _, item := range arr {
   153→					if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   154→						if m["type"] == contentTypeText {
   155→							userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
   156→							break
   157→						}
   158→					}
   159→				}
   160→			}
   161→		}
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	// For each user prompt, extract the prompt text, following assistant response, and files
   165→	for idx, userIdx := range userIndices {
   166→		// Determine the range for this prompt's conversation
   167→		endIdx := len(transcript)
   168→		if idx < len(userIndices)-1 {
   169→			endIdx = userIndices[idx+1]
   170→		}
   171→
   172→		// Extract prompt text
   173→		prompt := extractUserPromptAt(transcript, userIdx)
   174→		if prompt == "" {
   175→			continue
   176→		}
   177→
   178→		// Extract assistant responses and files from this range
   179→		slice := transcript[userIdx:endIdx]
   180→		responses := extractAssistantResponses(slice)
   181→		files := extractModifiedFiles(slice)
   182→
   183→		pairs = append(pairs, PromptResponsePair{
   184→			Prompt:    prompt,
   185→			Responses: responses,
   186→			Files:     files,
   187→		})
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	return pairs
   191→}
   192→
   193→// extractUserPromptAt extracts the user prompt at the given index.
   194→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
   195→func extractUserPromptAt(lines []transcriptLine, idx int) string {
   196→	if idx >= len(lines) || lines[idx].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   197→		return ""
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	return transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[idx].Message)
   201→}
   202→
   203→// extractAssistantResponses collects all assistant text blocks from the given transcript slice.
   204→// A single prompt can trigger multiple assistant responses interspersed with tool calls.
   205→func extractAssistantResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   206→	var texts []string
   207→	for _, line := range transcript {
   208→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   209→			var msg assistantMessage
   210→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   211→				continue
   212→			}
   213→
   214→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   215→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   216→					texts = append(texts, block.Text)
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→	return texts
   222→}
   223→
   224→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user messages from the transcript in order.
   225→// Only considers messages with string content (not tool results).
   226→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   227→func extractUserPrompts(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   228→	var prompts []string
   229→	for i := range transcript {
   230→		if transcript[i].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   231→			continue
   232→		}
   233→		var msg userMessage
   234→		if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   235→			continue
   236→		}
   237→
   238→		// Handle string content
   239→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   240→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str))
   241→			continue
   242→		}
   243→
   244→		// Handle array content (only if it contains text blocks)
   245→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   246→			var texts []string
   247→			for _, item := range arr {
   248→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   249→					if m["type"] == contentTypeText {
   250→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   251→							texts = append(texts, text)
   252→						}
   253→					}
   254→				}
   255→			}
   256→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   257→				prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")))
   258→			}
   259→		}
   260→	}
   261→	return prompts
   262→}
   263→
   264→// extractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from the transcript.
   265→// Only considers messages with string content (not tool results).
   266→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
   267→func extractLastUserPrompt(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   268→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   269→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   270→		return ""
   271→	}
   272→	return prompts[len(prompts)-1]
   273→}
   274→
   275→// extractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last text message from the assistant
   276→func extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   277→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   278→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   279→			var msg assistantMessage
   280→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   281→				continue
   282→			}
   283→
   284→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   285→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   286→					return block.Text
   287→				}
   288→			}
   289→		}
   290→	}
   291→	return ""
   292→}
   293→
   294→// findLastUserUUID finds the UUID of the last user message (that has string content)
   295→func findLastUserUUID(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   296→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   297→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeUser {
   298→			var msg userMessage
   299→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   300→				continue
   301→			}
   302→			// Only count messages with string content (not tool results)
   303→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   304→				return transcript[i].UUID
   305→			}
   306→		}
   307→	}
   308→	return ""
   309→}
   310→
   311→// filterTranscriptAfterUUID returns transcript lines after the given UUID
   312→func filterTranscriptAfterUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, uuid string) []transcriptLine {
   313→	if uuid == "" {
   314→		return transcript
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	foundIndex := -1
   318→	for i, line := range transcript {
   319→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   320→			foundIndex = i
   321→			break
   322→		}
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	if foundIndex == -1 || foundIndex == len(transcript)-1 {
   326→		return transcript
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	return transcript[foundIndex+1:]
   330→}
   331→
   332→// extractModifiedFiles extracts the list of files modified by tool calls
   333→func extractModifiedFiles(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   334→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   335→	var files []string
   336→
   337→	for _, line := range transcript {
   338→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   339→			var msg assistantMessage
   340→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   341→				continue
   342→			}
   343→
   344→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   345→				if block.Type == contentTypeToolUse {
   346→					isModifyTool := false
   347→					for _, name := range claudecode.FileModificationTools {
   348→						if block.Name == name {
   349→							isModifyTool = true
   350→							break
   351→						}
   352→					}
   353→
   354→					if isModifyTool {
   355→						var input toolInput
   356→						if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
   357→							continue
   358→						}
   359→
   360→						file := input.FilePath
   361→						if file == "" {
   362→							file = input.NotebookPath
   363→						}
   364→
   365→						if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   366→							fileSet[file] = true
   367→							files = append(files, file)
   368→						}
   369→					}
   370→				}
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files
   376→}
   377→
   378→// extractKeyActions extracts key actions from the transcript for context file
   379→func extractKeyActions(transcript []transcriptLine, maxActions int) []string {
   380→	var keyActions []string
   381→
   382→	for _, line := range transcript {
   383→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   384→			var msg assistantMessage
   385→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   386→				continue
   387→			}
   388→
   389→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   390→				if block.Type == contentTypeToolUse {
   391→					var input toolInput
   392→					_ = json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort parsing for display purposes
   393→
   394→					detail := input.Description
   395→					if detail == "" {
   396→						detail = input.Command
   397→					}
   398→					if detail == "" {
   399→						detail = input.FilePath
   400→					}
   401→					if detail == "" {
   402→						detail = input.Pattern
   403→					}
   404→
   405→					action := "- **" + block.Name + "**: " + detail
   406→					keyActions = append(keyActions, action)
   407→					if len(keyActions) >= maxActions {
   408→						return keyActions
   409→					}
   410→				}
   411→			}
   412→		}
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	return keyActions
   416→}
   417→
   418→// AgentTranscriptPath returns the path to a subagent's transcript file.
   419→// Subagent transcripts are stored as agent-{agentId}.jsonl in the same directory
   420→// as the main transcript.
   421→func AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, agentID string) string {
   422→	return filepath.Join(transcriptDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   423→}
   424→
   425→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   426→type toolResultBlock struct {
   427→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   428→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   429→}
   430→
   431→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   432→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   433→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   434→}
   435→
   436→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   437→// for the given tool_use_id. This is used to find the checkpoint point for
   438→// transcript truncation when rewinding to a task.
   439→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   440→func FindCheckpointUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   441→	for _, line := range transcript {
   442→		if line.Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   443→			continue
   444→		}
   445→
   446→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   447→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   448→			continue
   449→		}
   450→
   451→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   452→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   453→				return line.UUID, true
   454→			}
   455→		}
   456→	}
   457→	return "", false
   458→}
   459→
   460→// TruncateTranscriptAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the
   461→// line with the given UUID. If the UUID is not found or is empty, returns
   462→// the entire transcript.
   463→//
   464→//nolint:revive // Exported for testing purposes
   465→func TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, uuid string) []transcriptLine {
   466→	if uuid == "" {
   467→		return transcript
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	for i, line := range transcript {
   471→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   472→			return transcript[:i+1]
   473→		}
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   477→	return transcript
   478→}
   479→
   480→// writeTranscript writes transcript lines to a file in JSONL format.
   481→func writeTranscript(path string, transcript []transcriptLine) error {
   482→	file, err := os.Create(path) //nolint:gosec // Writing to controlled git metadata path
   483→	if err != nil {
   484→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create file: %w", err)
   485→	}
   486→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   487→
   488→	for _, line := range transcript {
   489→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
   490→		if err != nil {
   491→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
   492→		}
   493→		if _, err := file.Write(data); err != nil {
   494→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write line: %w", err)
   495→		}
   496→		if _, err := file.WriteString("\n"); err != nil {
   497→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write newline: %w", err)
   498→		}
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	return nil
   502→}
   503→
   504→// TranscriptPosition contains the position information for a transcript file.
   505→type TranscriptPosition struct {
   506→	LastUUID  string // Last non-empty UUID (from user/assistant messages)
   507→	LineCount int    // Total number of lines
   508→}
   509→
   510→// GetTranscriptPosition reads a transcript file and returns the last UUID and line count.
   511→// Returns empty position if file doesn't exist or is empty.
   512→// Only considers UUIDs from actual messages (user/assistant), not summary rows which use leafUuid.
   513→func GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (TranscriptPosition, error) {
   514→	if path == "" {
   515→		return TranscriptPosition{}, nil
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   521→			return TranscriptPosition{}, nil
   522→		}
   523→		return TranscriptPosition{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
   524→	}
   525→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   526→
   527→	var pos TranscriptPosition
   528→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   529→
   530→	for {
   531→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   532→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
   533→			return TranscriptPosition{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   534→		}
   535→
   536→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
   537→			if err == io.EOF {
   538→				break
   539→			}
   540→			continue
   541→		}
   542→
   543→		pos.LineCount++
   544→
   545→		// Parse line to extract UUID (only from user/assistant messages, not summaries)
   546→		var line transcriptLine
   547→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
   548→			if line.UUID != "" {
   549→				pos.LastUUID = line.UUID
   550→			}
   551→		}
   552→
   553→		if err == io.EOF {
   554→			break
   555→		}
   556→	}
   557→
   558→	return pos, nil
   559→}
   560→

    26→// Scanner buffer size for large transcript files (10MB)
    27→const scannerBufferSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024

    29→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSONL content into transcript lines
    30→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
    31→	var lines []TranscriptLine
    32→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(bytes.NewReader(data))
    33→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
    34→
    35→	for scanner.Scan() {
    36→		var line TranscriptLine
    37→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
    38→			continue // Skip malformed lines
    39→		}
    40→		lines = append(lines, line)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
    44→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→	return lines, nil
    47→}

    49→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes

   116→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript

   202→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript.
   203→// This is specific to Claude/Anthropic's API format where each assistant message
   204→// contains a usage object with input_tokens, output_tokens, and cache tokens.
   205→//
   206→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   207→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   208→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcript []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   209→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   210→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   211→
   212→	for _, line := range transcript {
   213→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   214→			continue
   215→		}
   216→
   217→		var msg messageWithUsage
   218→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   219→			continue
   220→		}
   221→
   222→		if msg.ID == "" {
   223→			continue
   224→		}
   225→
   226→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   227→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   228→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   229→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Sum up all unique messages
   234→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   235→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   236→	}
   237→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   238→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   239→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   240→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   241→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	return usage
   245→}
   246→
   247→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript file.
   248→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   249→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   250→	if path == "" {
   251→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(path, startLine)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return nil, err
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return CalculateTokenUsage(transcript), nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→// parseTranscriptFromLine parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
   263→func parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
   264→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   265→	if err != nil {
   266→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   267→	}
   268→	defer file.Close()
   269→
   270→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   271→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
   272→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
   273→
   274→	lineNum := 0
   275→	for scanner.Scan() {
   276→		if lineNum < startLine {
   277→			lineNum++
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→		lineNum++
   281→
   282→		var line TranscriptLine
   283→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
   284→			continue // Skip malformed lines
   285→		}
   286→		lines = append(lines, line)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
   290→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	return lines, nil
   294→}
   295→
   296→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   297→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   298→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   299→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   300→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   301→
   302→	for _, line := range transcript {
   303→		if line.Type != "user" {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→
   307→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   308→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   309→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   310→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   311→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   312→		}
   313→
   314→		var msg struct {
   315→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   316→		}
   317→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   318→			continue
   319→		}
   320→
   321→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   322→			continue
   323→		}
   324→
   325→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   326→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   327→				continue
   328→			}
   329→
   330→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   331→			var textContent string
   332→
   333→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   334→			var textBlocks []struct {
   335→				Type string `json:"type"`
   336→				Text string `json:"text"`
   337→			}
   338→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   339→				var textContentSb361 strings.Builder
   340→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   341→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   342→						textContentSb361.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   343→					}
   344→				}
   345→				textContent += textContentSb361.String()
   346→			} else {
   347→				// Try as plain string
   348→				var str string
   349→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   350→					textContent = str
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			// Look for agentId in the text
   355→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   356→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   357→			}
   358→		}
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	return agentIDs
   362→}
   363→
   364→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   365→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   366→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   367→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   368→	if idx == -1 {
   369→		return ""
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   373→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   374→	end := start
   375→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   376→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   377→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   378→		end++
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	if end > start {
   382→		return text[start:end]
   383→	}
   384→	return ""
   385→}
   386→
   387→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   388→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   389→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   390→func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   391→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   392→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	// Parse transcript ONCE
   396→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   402→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   403→
   404→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   405→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
   406→
   407→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   408→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   409→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   410→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   411→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   412→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   413→			if err != nil {
   414→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   415→				continue
   416→			}
   417→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   418→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   419→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   420→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   421→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   422→		}
   423→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   424→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   425→		}
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	return mainUsage, nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   432→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   433→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   434→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   435→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   436→func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   437→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   438→		return nil, nil
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Parse main transcript once
   442→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	// Collect modified files from main agent
   448→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   449→	var files []string
   450→	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(transcript) {
   451→		if !fileSet[f] {
   452→			fileSet[f] = true
   453→			files = append(files, f)
   454→		}
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   458→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
   459→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   460→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   461→		agentTranscript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(agentPath, 0)
   462→		if err != nil {
   463→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   464→			continue
   465→		}
   466→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentTranscript) {
   467→			if !fileSet[f] {
   468→				fileSet[f] = true
   469→				files = append(files, f)
   470→			}
   471→		}
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	return files, nil
   475→}

    29→
    30→// parseAndLogHookInput parses the hook input and sets up logging context.
    31→func parseAndLogHookInput() (*hookInputData, error) {
    32→	// Get the agent from the hook command context (e.g., "entire hooks claude-code ...")
    33→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
    39→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, os.Stdin)
    40→	if err != nil {
    41→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
    45→	logging.Info(logCtx, "user-prompt-submit",
    46→		slog.String("hook", "user-prompt-submit"),
    47→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
    48→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
    49→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
    50→	)
    51→
    52→	sessionID := input.SessionID
    53→	if sessionID == "" {
    54→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
    55→	}
    56→
    57→	// Get the Entire session ID, preferring the persisted value to handle midnight boundary
    58→	return &hookInputData{
    59→		agent:     ag,
    60→		input:     input,
    61→		sessionID: sessionID,
    62→	}, nil
    63→}
    64→
    65→// captureInitialState captures the initial state on user prompt submit.
    66→func captureInitialState() error {
    67→	// Parse hook input and setup logging
    68→	hookData, err := parseAndLogHookInput()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		return err
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// CLI captures state directly (including transcript position)
    74→	if err := CapturePrePromptState(hookData.sessionID, hookData.input.SessionRef); err != nil {
    75→		return err
    76→	}
    77→
    78→	// If strategy implements SessionInitializer, call it to initialize session state
    79→	strat := GetStrategy()
    80→
    81→	// Ensure strategy setup is in place (git hooks, gitignore, metadata branch).
    82→	// Done here at turn start so hooks are installed before any mid-turn commits.
    83→	if err := strat.EnsureSetup(); err != nil {
    84→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to ensure strategy setup: %v\n", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	if initializer, ok := strat.(strategy.SessionInitializer); ok {
    88→		agentType := hookData.agent.Type()
    89→		if err := initializer.InitializeSession(hookData.sessionID, agentType, hookData.input.SessionRef, hookData.input.UserPrompt); err != nil {
    90→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to initialize session state: %v\n", err)
    91→		}
    92→	}
    93→
    94→	return nil
    95→}
    96→
    97→// commitWithMetadata commits the session changes with metadata.
    98→func commitWithMetadata() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    99→	// Get the agent for hook input parsing and session ID transformation
   100→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Parse hook input using agent interface
   106→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookStop, os.Stdin)
   107→	if err != nil {
   108→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   109→	}
   110→
   111→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   112→	logging.Info(logCtx, "stop",
   113→		slog.String("hook", "stop"),
   114→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   115→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   116→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.SessionRef),
   117→	)
   118→
   119→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   120→	if sessionID == "" {
   121→		sessionID = unknownSessionID
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	transcriptPath := input.SessionRef
   125→	if transcriptPath == "" || !fileExists(transcriptPath) {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("transcript file not found or empty: %s", transcriptPath)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Early check: bail out quickly if the repo has no commits yet.
   130→	// Without this, the function does a lot of work (transcript copy, prompt extraction, etc.)
   131→	// before eventually failing deep in strategy.SaveChanges().
   132→	if repo, err := strategy.OpenRepository(); err == nil && strategy.IsEmptyRepository(repo) {
   133→		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping checkpoint. Will activate after first commit.")
   134→		return NewSilentError(strategy.ErrEmptyRepository)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Create session metadata folder using the entire session ID (preserves original date on resume)
   138→	// Use AbsPath to ensure we create at repo root, not relative to cwd
   139→	sessionDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   140→	sessionDirAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(sessionDir)
   141→	if err != nil {
   142→		sessionDirAbs = sessionDir // Fallback to relative
   143→	}
   144→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDirAbs, 0o750); err != nil {
   145→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	// Wait for Claude Code to flush the transcript file.
   149→	// The stop hook fires before the transcript is fully written to disk.
   150→	// We poll for our own hook_progress sentinel entry in the file tail,
   151→	// which guarantees all prior entries have been flushed.
   152→	waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath, time.Now())
   153→
   154→	// Copy transcript
   155→	logFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.TranscriptFileName)
   156→	if err := copyFile(transcriptPath, logFile); err != nil {
   157→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   158→	}
   159→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copied transcript to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.TranscriptFileName)
   160→
   161→	// Load pre-prompt state (captured on UserPromptSubmit)
   162→	// Needed for transcript offset and file change detection
   163→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   164→	if err != nil {
   165→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   166→	}
   167→
   168→	// Determine transcript offset: prefer pre-prompt state, fall back to session state.
   169→	// Pre-prompt state has the offset when the transcript path was available at prompt time.
   170→	// Session state has the offset updated after each successful checkpoint save (auto-commit).
   171→	var transcriptOffset int
   172→	if preState != nil && preState.StepTranscriptStart > 0 {
   173→		transcriptOffset = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   174→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   175→	} else {
   176→		// Fall back to session state (e.g., auto-commit strategy updates it after each save)
   177→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   178→		if loadErr != nil {
   179→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   180→		}
   181→		if sessionState != nil && sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   182→			transcriptOffset = sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   183→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session state found: parsing transcript from line %d\n", transcriptOffset)
   184→		}
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Parse transcript (optionally from offset for strategies that track transcript position)
   188→	// When transcriptOffset > 0, only parse NEW lines since the last checkpoint
   189→	var transcript []transcriptLine
   190→	var totalLines int
   191→	if transcriptOffset > 0 {
   192→		// Parse only NEW lines since last checkpoint
   193→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset)
   194→		if err != nil {
   195→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript from line %d: %w", transcriptOffset, err)
   196→		}
   197→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Parsed %d new transcript lines (total: %d)\n", len(transcript), totalLines)
   198→	} else {
   199→		// First prompt or no session state - parse entire transcript
   200→		// Use parseTranscriptFromLine with offset 0 to also get totalLines
   201→		transcript, totalLines, err = parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, 0)
   202→		if err != nil {
   203→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   204→		}
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	// Extract all prompts since last checkpoint for prompt file
   208→	allPrompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   209→	promptFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.PromptFileName)
   210→	promptContent := strings.Join(allPrompts, "\n\n---\n\n")
   211→	if err := os.WriteFile(promptFile, []byte(promptContent), 0o600); err != nil {
   212→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write prompt file: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted %d prompt(s) to: %s\n", len(allPrompts), sessionDir+"/"+paths.PromptFileName)
   215→
   216→	// Extract summary
   217→	summaryFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.SummaryFileName)
   218→	summary := extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript)
   219→	if err := os.WriteFile(summaryFile, []byte(summary), 0o600); err != nil {
   220→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write summary file: %w", err)
   221→	}
   222→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Extracted summary to: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.SummaryFileName)
   223→
   224→	// Get modified files from transcript (main agent + subagents).
   225→	// Subagent transcripts live in <transcriptDir>/<modelSessionID>/subagents/
   226→	subagentsDir := filepath.Join(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), input.SessionID, "subagents")
   227→	modifiedFiles, subagentErr := claudecode.ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, transcriptOffset, subagentsDir)
   228→	if subagentErr != nil {
   229→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to extract modified files with subagents: %v\n", subagentErr)
   230→		// Fall back to main transcript only
   231→		modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	// Generate commit message from last user prompt
   235→	lastPrompt := ""
   236→	if len(allPrompts) > 0 {
   237→		lastPrompt = allPrompts[len(allPrompts)-1]
   238→	}
   239→	commitMessage := generateCommitMessage(lastPrompt)
   240→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Using commit message: %s\n", commitMessage)
   241→
   242→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   243→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   244→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   245→	if err != nil {
   246→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	if preState != nil {
   250→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Pre-prompt state: %d pre-existing untracked files\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   251→	}
   252→
   253→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   254→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	// Filter and normalize all paths (CLI responsibility)
   260→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   261→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   262→	if changes != nil {
   263→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   264→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   265→	}
   266→
   267→	// Check if there are any changes to commit
   268→	totalChanges := len(relModifiedFiles) + len(relNewFiles) + len(relDeletedFiles)
   269→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   270→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No files were modified during this session\n")
   271→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipping commit\n")
   272→		// Still transition phase even when skipping commit — the turn is ending.
   273→		transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   274→		// Clean up state even when skipping
   275→		if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   276→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   277→		}
   278→		return nil
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files modified during session (%d):\n", len(relModifiedFiles))
   282→	for _, file := range relModifiedFiles {
   283→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   284→	}
   285→	if len(relNewFiles) > 0 {
   286→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "New files created (%d):\n", len(relNewFiles))
   287→		for _, file := range relNewFiles {
   288→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  + %s\n", file)
   289→		}
   290→	}
   291→	if len(relDeletedFiles) > 0 {
   292→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Files deleted (%d):\n", len(relDeletedFiles))
   293→		for _, file := range relDeletedFiles {
   294→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", file)
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Create context file before saving changes
   299→	contextFile := filepath.Join(sessionDirAbs, paths.ContextFileName)
   300→	if err := createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile, transcript); err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create context file: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Created context file: %s\n", sessionDir+"/"+paths.ContextFileName)
   304→
   305→	// Get git author from local/global config
   306→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→
   311→	// Get the configured strategy
   312→	strat := GetStrategy()
   313→
   314→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   315→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   316→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   317→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   318→	}
   319→
   320→	// Get transcript position from pre-prompt state (captured at step/turn start)
   321→	var transcriptIdentifierAtStart string
   322→	var transcriptLinesAtStart int
   323→	if preState != nil {
   324→		transcriptIdentifierAtStart = preState.LastTranscriptIdentifier
   325→		transcriptLinesAtStart = preState.StepTranscriptStart
   326→	}
   327→
   328→	// Calculate token usage for this checkpoint (Claude Code specific)
   329→	var tokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage
   330→	if transcriptPath != "" {
   331→		usage, tokenErr := claudecode.CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, transcriptLinesAtStart, subagentsDir)
   332→		if tokenErr != nil {
   333→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to calculate token usage: %v\n", tokenErr)
   334→		} else {
   335→			tokenUsage = usage
   336→		}
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// Build fully-populated save context and delegate to strategy
   340→	ctx := strategy.SaveContext{
   341→		SessionID:                sessionID,
   342→		ModifiedFiles:            relModifiedFiles,
   343→		NewFiles:                 relNewFiles,
   344→		DeletedFiles:             relDeletedFiles,
   345→		MetadataDir:              sessionDir,
   346→		MetadataDirAbs:           sessionDirAbs,
   347→		CommitMessage:            commitMessage,
   348→		TranscriptPath:           transcriptPath,
   349→		AuthorName:               author.Name,
   350→		AuthorEmail:              author.Email,
   351→		AgentType:                agentType,
   352→		StepTranscriptIdentifier: transcriptIdentifierAtStart,
   353→		StepTranscriptStart:      transcriptLinesAtStart,
   354→		TokenUsage:               tokenUsage,
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	if err := strat.SaveChanges(ctx); err != nil {
   358→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save changes: %w", err)
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	// Update session state with new transcript position for strategies that create
   362→	// commits on the active branch (auto-commit strategy). This prevents parsing old transcript
   363→	// lines on subsequent checkpoints.
   364→	// Note: Shadow strategy tracks transcript position per-step via StepTranscriptStart in
   365→	// pre-prompt state, but doesn't advance CheckpointTranscriptStart in session state because
   366→	// its checkpoints accumulate all files touched across the entire session.
   367→	if strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   368→		// Load session state for updating transcript position
   369→		sessionState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   370→		if loadErr != nil {
   371→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state: %v\n", loadErr)
   372→		}
   373→		// Create session state lazily if it doesn't exist (backward compat for resumed sessions
   374→		// or if InitializeSession was never called/failed)
   375→		if sessionState == nil {
   376→			sessionState = &strategy.SessionState{
   377→				SessionID: sessionID,
   378→			}
   379→		}
   380→		sessionState.CheckpointTranscriptStart = totalLines
   381→		sessionState.StepCount++
   382→		if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(sessionState); updateErr != nil {
   383→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session state: %v\n", updateErr)
   384→		} else {
   385→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Updated session state: transcript position=%d, checkpoint=%d\n",
   386→				totalLines, sessionState.StepCount)
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Fire EventTurnEnd to transition session phase (all strategies).
   391→	// This moves ACTIVE → IDLE.
   392→	transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID)
   393→
   394→	// Clean up pre-prompt state (CLI responsibility)
   395→	if err := CleanupPrePromptState(sessionID); err != nil {
   396→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to cleanup pre-prompt state: %v\n", err)
   397→	}
   398→
   399→	return nil
   400→}
   401→
   402→// handleClaudeCodePostTodo handles the PostToolUse[TodoWrite] hook for subagent checkpoints.
   403→// Creates a checkpoint if we're in a subagent context (active pre-task file exists).
   404→// Skips silently if not in subagent context (main agent).
   405→func handleClaudeCodePostTodo() error {
   406→	input, err := parseSubagentCheckpointHookInput(os.Stdin)
   407→	if err != nil {
   408→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[TodoWrite] input: %w", err)
   409→	}
   410→
   411→	// Get agent for logging context
   412→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   413→	if err != nil {
   414→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   415→	}
   416→
   417→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   418→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-todo",
   419→		slog.String("hook", "post-todo"),
   420→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   421→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   422→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   423→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   424→	)
   425→
   426→	// Check if we're in a subagent context by looking for an active pre-task file
   427→	taskToolUseID, found := FindActivePreTaskFile()
   428→	if !found {
   429→		// Not in subagent context - this is a main agent TodoWrite, skip
   430→		return nil
   431→	}
   432→
   433→	// Skip on default branch to avoid polluting main/master history
   434→	if skip, branchName := ShouldSkipOnDefaultBranch(); skip {
   435→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Entire: skipping incremental checkpoint on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   436→		return nil
   437→	}
   438→
   439→	// Detect file changes since last checkpoint
   440→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(nil)
   441→	if err != nil {
   442→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to detect changed files: %v\n", err)
   443→		return nil
   444→	}
   445→
   446→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   447→	if len(changes.Modified) == 0 && len(changes.New) == 0 && len(changes.Deleted) == 0 {
   448→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping incremental checkpoint\n")
   449→		return nil
   450→	}
   451→
   452→	// Get git author
   453→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   454→	if err != nil {
   455→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to get git author: %v\n", err)
   456→		return nil
   457→	}
   458→
   459→	// Get the active strategy
   460→	strat := GetStrategy()
   461→
   462→	// Get the session ID from the transcript path or input, then transform to Entire session ID
   463→	sessionID := input.SessionID
   464→	if sessionID == "" {
   465→		sessionID = paths.ExtractSessionIDFromTranscriptPath(input.TranscriptPath)
   466→	}
   467→
   468→	// Get next checkpoint sequence
   469→	seq := GetNextCheckpointSequence(sessionID, taskToolUseID)
   470→
   471→	// Extract the todo content from the tool_input.
   472→	// PostToolUse receives the NEW todo list where the just-completed work is
   473→	// marked as "completed". The last completed item is the work that was just done.
   474→	todoContent := ExtractLastCompletedTodoFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   475→	if todoContent == "" {
   476→		// No completed items - this is likely the first TodoWrite (planning phase).
   477→		// Check if there are any todos at all to avoid duplicate messages.
   478→		todoCount := CountTodosFromToolInput(input.ToolInput)
   479→		if todoCount > 0 {
   480→			// Use "Planning: N todos" format for the first TodoWrite
   481→			todoContent = fmt.Sprintf("Planning: %d todos", todoCount)
   482→		}
   483→		// If todoCount == 0, todoContent remains empty and FormatIncrementalMessage
   484→		// will fall back to "Checkpoint #N" format
   485→	}
   486→
   487→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   488→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   489→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   490→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   491→	}
   492→
   493→	// Build incremental checkpoint context
   494→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   495→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   496→		ToolUseID:           taskToolUseID,
   497→		ModifiedFiles:       changes.Modified,
   498→		NewFiles:            changes.New,
   499→		DeletedFiles:        changes.Deleted,
   500→		TranscriptPath:      input.TranscriptPath,
   501→		AuthorName:          author.Name,
   502→		AuthorEmail:         author.Email,
   503→		IsIncremental:       true,
   504→		IncrementalSequence: seq,
   505→		IncrementalType:     input.ToolName,
   506→		IncrementalData:     input.ToolInput,
   507→		TodoContent:         todoContent,
   508→		AgentType:           agentType,
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	// Save incremental checkpoint
   512→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   513→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to save incremental checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   514→		return nil
   515→	}
   516→
   517→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Created incremental checkpoint #%d for %s (task: %s)\n",
   518→		seq, input.ToolName, taskToolUseID[:min(12, len(taskToolUseID))])
   519→	return nil
   520→}
   521→
   522→// handleClaudeCodePreTask handles the PreToolUse[Task] hook
   523→func handleClaudeCodePreTask() error {
   524→	input, err := parseTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   525→	if err != nil {
   526→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PreToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   527→	}
   528→
   529→	// Get agent for logging context
   530→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   531→	if err != nil {
   532→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   533→	}
   534→
   535→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   536→	logging.Info(logCtx, "pre-task",
   537→		slog.String("hook", "pre-task"),
   538→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   539→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   540→		slog.String("transcript_path", input.TranscriptPath),
   541→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   542→	)
   543→
   544→	// Log context to stdout
   545→	logPreTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input)
   546→
   547→	// Capture pre-task state locally (for computing new files when task completes).
   548→	// We don't create a shadow branch commit here. Commits are created during
   549→	// task completion (handleClaudeCodePostTask/handleClaudeCodePostTodo) only if the task resulted
   550→	// in file changes.
   551→	if err := CapturePreTaskState(input.ToolUseID); err != nil {
   552→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to capture pre-task state: %w", err)
   553→	}
   554→
   555→	return nil
   556→}
   557→
   558→// handleClaudeCodePostTask handles the PostToolUse[Task] hook
   559→func handleClaudeCodePostTask() error {
   560→	input, err := parsePostTaskHookInput(os.Stdin)
   561→	if err != nil {
   562→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse PostToolUse[Task] input: %w", err)
   563→	}
   564→
   565→	// Extract subagent type from tool_input for logging
   566→	subagentType, taskDescription := ParseSubagentTypeAndDescription(input.ToolInput)
   567→
   568→	// Get agent for logging context
   569→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   570→	if err != nil {
   571→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   572→	}
   573→
   574→	// Log parsed input context
   575→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   576→	logging.Info(logCtx, "post-task",
   577→		slog.String("hook", "post-task"),
   578→		slog.String("hook_type", "subagent"),
   579→		slog.String("tool_use_id", input.ToolUseID),
   580→		slog.String("agent_id", input.AgentID),
   581→		slog.String("subagent_type", subagentType),
   582→	)
   583→
   584→	// Determine subagent transcript path
   585→	transcriptDir := filepath.Dir(input.TranscriptPath)
   586→	var subagentTranscriptPath string
   587→	if input.AgentID != "" {
   588→		subagentTranscriptPath = AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, input.AgentID)
   589→		if !fileExists(subagentTranscriptPath) {
   590→			subagentTranscriptPath = ""
   591→		}
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	// Log context to stdout
   595→	logPostTaskHookContext(os.Stdout, input, subagentTranscriptPath)
   596→
   597→	// Parse transcript to extract modified files
   598→	var modifiedFiles []string
   599→	if subagentTranscriptPath != "" {
   600→		// Use subagent transcript if available
   601→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(subagentTranscriptPath)
   602→		if err != nil {
   603→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse subagent transcript: %v\n", err)
   604→		} else {
   605→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   606→		}
   607→	} else {
   608→		// Fall back to main transcript
   609→		transcript, err := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath)
   610→		if err != nil {
   611→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to parse transcript: %v\n", err)
   612→		} else {
   613→			modifiedFiles = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   614→		}
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	// Load pre-task state and compute new files
   618→	preState, err := LoadPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID)
   619→	if err != nil {
   620→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load pre-task state: %v\n", err)
   621→	}
   622→
   623→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   624→	changes, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   625→	if err != nil {
   626→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to compute file changes: %v\n", err)
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	// Get repo root for path conversion (not cwd, since Claude may be in a subdirectory)
   630→	// Using cwd would filter out files in sibling directories (paths starting with ..)
   631→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   632→	if err != nil {
   633→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repo root: %w", err)
   634→	}
   635→
   636→	// Filter and normalize paths
   637→	relModifiedFiles := FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFiles, repoRoot)
   638→	var relNewFiles, relDeletedFiles []string
   639→	if changes != nil {
   640→		relNewFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.New, repoRoot)
   641→		relDeletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(changes.Deleted, repoRoot)
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	// If no file changes, skip creating a checkpoint
   645→	if len(relModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(relNewFiles) == 0 && len(relDeletedFiles) == 0 {
   646→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] No file changes detected, skipping task checkpoint\n")
   647→		// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   648→		_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   649→		return nil
   650→	}
   651→
   652→	// Find checkpoint UUID from main transcript (best-effort, ignore errors)
   653→	transcript, _ := parseTranscript(input.TranscriptPath) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort extraction
   654→	checkpointUUID, _ := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, input.ToolUseID)
   655→
   656→	// Get git author
   657→	author, err := GetGitAuthor()
   658→	if err != nil {
   659→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get git author: %w", err)
   660→	}
   661→
   662→	// Get the configured strategy
   663→	strat := GetStrategy()
   664→
   665→	// Get agent type from the currently executing hook agent (authoritative source)
   666→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   667→	if hookAgent, agentErr := GetCurrentHookAgent(); agentErr == nil {
   668→		agentType = hookAgent.Type()
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	// Build task checkpoint context - strategy handles metadata creation
   672→	// Note: Incremental checkpoints are now created during task execution via handleClaudeCodePostTodo,
   673→	// so we don't need to collect/cleanup staging area here.
   674→	ctx := strategy.TaskCheckpointContext{
   675→		SessionID:              input.SessionID,
   676→		ToolUseID:              input.ToolUseID,
   677→		AgentID:                input.AgentID,
   678→		ModifiedFiles:          relModifiedFiles,
   679→		NewFiles:               relNewFiles,
   680→		DeletedFiles:           relDeletedFiles,
   681→		TranscriptPath:         input.TranscriptPath,
   682→		SubagentTranscriptPath: subagentTranscriptPath,
   683→		CheckpointUUID:         checkpointUUID,
   684→		AuthorName:             author.Name,
   685→		AuthorEmail:            author.Email,
   686→		SubagentType:           subagentType,
   687→		TaskDescription:        taskDescription,
   688→		AgentType:              agentType,
   689→	}
   690→
   691→	// Call strategy to save task checkpoint - strategy handles all metadata creation
   692→	if err := strat.SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx); err != nil {
   693→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save task checkpoint: %w", err)
   694→	}
   695→
   696→	// Cleanup pre-task state (ignore error - cleanup is best-effort)
   697→	_ = CleanupPreTaskState(input.ToolUseID) //nolint:errcheck // best-effort cleanup
   698→
   699→	return nil
   700→}
   701→
   702→// handleClaudeCodeSessionStart handles the SessionStart hook for Claude Code.
   703→func handleClaudeCodeSessionStart() error {
   704→	return handleSessionStartCommon()
   705→}
   706→
   707→// handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd handles the SessionEnd hook for Claude Code.
   708→// This fires when the user explicitly closes the session.
   709→// Updates the session state with EndedAt timestamp.
   710→func handleClaudeCodeSessionEnd() error {
   711→	ag, err := GetCurrentHookAgent()
   712→	if err != nil {
   713→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   714→	}
   715→
   716→	input, err := ag.ParseHookInput(agent.HookSessionEnd, os.Stdin)
   717→	if err != nil {
   718→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hook input: %w", err)
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	logCtx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks"), ag.Name())
   722→	logging.Info(logCtx, "session-end",
   723→		slog.String("hook", "session-end"),
   724→		slog.String("hook_type", "agent"),
   725→		slog.String("model_session_id", input.SessionID),
   726→	)
   727→
   728→	if input.SessionID == "" {
   729→		return nil // No session to update
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	// Best-effort cleanup - don't block session closure on failure
   733→	if err := markSessionEnded(input.SessionID); err != nil {
   734→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to mark session ended: %v\n", err)
   735→	}
   736→	return nil
   737→}
   738→
   739→// transitionSessionTurnEnd fires EventTurnEnd to move the session from
   740→// ACTIVE → IDLE. Best-effort: logs warnings on failure rather than
   741→// returning errors.
   742→func transitionSessionTurnEnd(sessionID string) {
   743→	turnState, loadErr := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   744→	if loadErr != nil {
   745→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to load session state for turn end: %v\n", loadErr)
   746→		return
   747→	}
   748→	if turnState == nil {
   749→		return
   750→	}
   751→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(turnState, session.EventTurnEnd, session.TransitionContext{})
   752→
   753→	// Always dispatch to strategy for turn-end handling. The strategy reads
   754→	// work items from state (e.g. TurnCheckpointIDs), not the action list.
   755→	strat := GetStrategy()
   756→	if handler, ok := strat.(strategy.TurnEndHandler); ok {
   757→		if err := handler.HandleTurnEnd(turnState); err != nil {
   758→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: turn-end action dispatch failed: %v\n", err)
   759→		}
   760→	}
   761→
   762→	if updateErr := strategy.SaveSessionState(turnState); updateErr != nil {
   763→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to update session phase on turn end: %v\n", updateErr)
   764→	}
   765→}
   766→
   767→// markSessionEnded transitions the session to ENDED phase via the state machine.
   768→func markSessionEnded(sessionID string) error {
   769→	state, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   770→	if err != nil {
   771→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   772→	}
   773→	if state == nil {
   774→		return nil // No state file, nothing to update
   775→	}
   776→
   777→	strategy.TransitionAndLog(state, session.EventSessionStop, session.TransitionContext{})
   778→
   779→	now := time.Now()
   780→	state.EndedAt = &now
   781→
   782→	if err := strategy.SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→	return nil
   786→}
   787→
   788→// stopHookSentinel is the string that appears in Claude Code's hook_progress
   789→// transcript entry when it launches our stop hook. Used to detect that the
   790→// transcript file has been fully flushed before we copy it.
   791→const stopHookSentinel = "hooks claude-code stop"
   792→
   793→// waitForTranscriptFlush polls the transcript file tail for the hook_progress
   794→// sentinel entry that Claude Code writes when launching the stop hook.
   795→// Once this entry appears in the file, all prior entries (assistant replies,
   796→// tool results) are guaranteed to have been flushed.
   797→//
   798→// hookStartTime is the approximate time our process started, used to avoid
   799→// matching stale sentinel entries from previous stop hook invocations.
   800→//
   801→// Falls back silently after a timeout — the transcript copy will proceed
   802→// with whatever data is available.
   803→func waitForTranscriptFlush(transcriptPath string, hookStartTime time.Time) {
   804→	const (
   805→		maxWait      = 3 * time.Second
   806→		pollInterval = 50 * time.Millisecond
   807→		tailBytes    = 4096 // Read last 4KB — sentinel is near the end
   808→		maxSkew      = 2 * time.Second
   809→	)
   810→
   811→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "hooks")
   812→	deadline := time.Now().Add(maxWait)
   813→	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
   814→		if checkStopSentinel(transcriptPath, tailBytes, hookStartTime, maxSkew) {
   815→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel found",
   816→				slog.Duration("wait", time.Since(hookStartTime)),
   817→			)
   818→			return
   819→		}
   820→		time.Sleep(pollInterval)
   821→	}
   822→	// Timeout — proceed with whatever is on disk.
   823→	logging.Warn(logCtx, "transcript flush sentinel not found within timeout, proceeding",
   824→		slog.Duration("timeout", maxWait),
   825→	)
   826→}
   827→
   828→// checkStopSentinel reads the tail of the transcript file and looks for a
   829→// hook_progress entry containing the stop hook sentinel, with a timestamp
   830→// close to hookStartTime.
   831→func checkStopSentinel(path string, tailBytes int64, hookStartTime time.Time, maxSkew time.Duration) bool {
   832→	f, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path comes from agent hook input
   833→	if err != nil {
   834→		return false
   835→	}
   836→	defer f.Close()
   837→
   838→	// Seek to tail
   839→	info, err := f.Stat()
   840→	if err != nil {
   841→		return false
   842→	}
   843→	offset := info.Size() - tailBytes
   844→	if offset < 0 {
   845→		offset = 0
   846→	}
   847→	buf := make([]byte, info.Size()-offset)
   848→	if _, err := f.ReadAt(buf, offset); err != nil {
   849→		return false
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Scan lines from the tail for the sentinel
   853→	lines := strings.Split(string(buf), "\n")
   854→	for _, line := range lines {
   855→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   856→		if line == "" || !strings.Contains(line, stopHookSentinel) {
   857→			continue
   858→		}
   859→
   860→		// Parse timestamp to check recency
   861→		var entry struct {
   862→			Timestamp string `json:"timestamp"`
   863→		}
   864→		if json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry) != nil || entry.Timestamp == "" {
   865→			continue
   866→		}
   867→		ts, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339Nano, entry.Timestamp)
   868→		if err != nil {
   869→			ts, err = time.Parse(time.RFC3339, entry.Timestamp)
   870→			if err != nil {
   871→				continue
   872→			}
   873→		}
   874→
   875→		// Check timestamp is within skew window of our start time
   876→		if ts.After(hookStartTime.Add(-maxSkew)) && ts.Before(hookStartTime.Add(maxSkew)) {
   877→			return true
   878→		}
   879→	}
   880→	return false
   881→}
   882→
