    17→func newDebugCmd() *cobra.Command {
    18→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    19→		Use:    "debug",
    20→		Short:  "Debug commands for troubleshooting",
    21→		Hidden: true, // Hidden from help output
    22→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    23→			return cmd.Help()
    24→		},
    25→	}
    26→
    27→	cmd.AddCommand(newDebugAutoCommitCmd())
    28→
    29→	return cmd
    30→}
    31→
    32→func newDebugAutoCommitCmd() *cobra.Command {
    33→	var transcriptPath string
    34→
    35→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    36→		Use:   "auto-commit",
    37→		Short: "Show whether current state would trigger an auto-commit",
    38→		Long: `Analyzes the current session state and configuration to determine
    39→if the Stop hook would create an auto-commit.
    40→
    41→This simulates what the Stop hook checks:
    42→- Current session and pre-prompt state
    43→- Modified files from transcript (if --transcript provided)
    44→- New files (current untracked - pre-prompt untracked)
    45→- Deleted files (tracked files that were removed)
    46→
    47→Without --transcript, shows git status changes instead.`,
    48→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
    49→			return runDebugAutoCommit(cmd.OutOrStdout(), transcriptPath)
    50→		},
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	cmd.Flags().StringVarP(&transcriptPath, "transcript", "t", "", "Path to transcript file (.jsonl) to parse for modified files")
    54→
    55→	return cmd
    56→}
    57→
    58→func runDebugAutoCommit(w io.Writer, transcriptPath string) error {
    59→	// Check if we're in a git repository
    60→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Not in a git repository")
    63→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // not being in a git repo is expected, not an error for status check
    64→	}
    65→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Repository: %s\n\n", repoRoot)
    66→
    67→	// Print strategy info
    68→	strat := GetStrategy()
    69→	isAutoCommit := strat.Name() == strategy.StrategyNameAutoCommit
    70→	printStrategyInfo(w, strat, isAutoCommit)
    71→
    72→	// Print session state
    73→	currentSession := printSessionState(w)
    74→
    75→	// Auto-detect transcript if not provided
    76→	if transcriptPath == "" && currentSession != "" {
    77→		detected, detectErr := findTranscriptForSession(currentSession)
    78→		if detectErr != nil {
    79→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nCould not auto-detect transcript: %v\n", detectErr)
    80→		} else if detected != "" {
    81→			transcriptPath = detected
    82→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nAuto-detected transcript: %s\n", transcriptPath)
    83→		}
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Print file changes and get total
    87→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n=== File Changes ===")
    88→	var totalChanges int
    89→	if transcriptPath != "" {
    90→		totalChanges = printTranscriptChanges(w, transcriptPath, currentSession, repoRoot)
    91→	} else {
    92→		var err error
    93→		totalChanges, err = printGitStatusChanges(w)
    94→		if err != nil {
    95→			return err
    96→		}
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Print decision
   100→	printDecision(w, isAutoCommit, strat.Name(), totalChanges)
   101→
   102→	// Print transcript location help if we couldn't find one
   103→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   104→		printTranscriptHelp(w)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	return nil
   108→}
   109→
   110→func printStrategyInfo(w io.Writer, strat strategy.Strategy, isAutoCommit bool) {
   111→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Strategy: %s\n", strat.Name())
   112→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Auto-commit strategy: %v\n", isAutoCommit)
   113→
   114→	_, branchName, err := IsOnDefaultBranch()
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Branch: (unable to determine: %v)\n\n", err)
   117→	} else {
   118→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Branch: %s\n\n", branchName)
   119→	}
   120→}
   121→
   122→func printSessionState(w io.Writer) string {
   123→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "=== Session State ===")
   124→
   125→	currentSession := strategy.FindMostRecentSession()
   126→	if currentSession == "" {
   127→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Current session: (none - no active session)")
   128→		return ""
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "Current session: %s\n", currentSession)
   132→	printPrePromptState(w, currentSession)
   133→	return currentSession
   134→}
   135→
   136→func printPrePromptState(w io.Writer, sessionID string) {
   137→	preState, err := LoadPrePromptState(sessionID)
   138→	switch {
   139→	case err != nil:
   140→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Pre-prompt state: (error: %v)\n", err)
   141→	case preState != nil:
   142→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "Pre-prompt state: captured at %s\n", preState.Timestamp)
   143→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Pre-existing untracked files: %d\n", len(preState.UntrackedFiles))
   144→		printUntrackedFilesSummary(w, preState.UntrackedFiles)
   145→	default:
   146→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Pre-prompt state: (none captured)")
   147→	}
   148→}
   149→
   150→func printUntrackedFilesSummary(w io.Writer, files []string) {
   151→	if len(files) == 0 {
   152→		return
   153→	}
   154→	if len(files) <= 10 {
   155→		for _, f := range files {
   156→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    - %s\n", f)
   157→		}
   158→	} else {
   159→		for _, f := range files[:5] {
   160→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "    - %s\n", f)
   161→		}
   162→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "    ... and %d more\n", len(files)-5)
   163→	}
   164→}
   165→
   166→func printTranscriptChanges(w io.Writer, transcriptPath, currentSession, repoRoot string) int {
   167→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\nParsing transcript: %s\n", transcriptPath)
   168→
   169→	var modifiedFromTranscript, newFiles, deletedFiles []string
   170→
   171→	// Parse transcript
   172→	transcript, err := parseTranscript(transcriptPath)
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Error parsing transcript: %v\n", err)
   175→	} else {
   176→		modifiedFromTranscript = extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   177→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Found %d modified files in transcript\n", len(modifiedFromTranscript))
   178→	}
   179→	// Compute new and deleted files (single git status call)
   180→	// Load preState only if we have an active session (needed for new file detection)
   181→	var preState *PrePromptState
   182→	if currentSession != "" {
   183→		var loadErr error
   184→		preState, loadErr = LoadPrePromptState(currentSession)
   185→		if loadErr != nil {
   186→			fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Error loading pre-prompt state: %v\n", loadErr)
   187→		}
   188→	}
   189→	// Always call DetectFileChanges - deleted files don't depend on preState
   190→	fileChanges, err := DetectFileChanges(preState.PreUntrackedFiles())
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  Error computing file changes: %v\n", err)
   193→	}
   194→	if fileChanges != nil {
   195→		newFiles = fileChanges.New
   196→		deletedFiles = fileChanges.Deleted
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	// Filter and normalize paths
   200→	modifiedFromTranscript = FilterAndNormalizePaths(modifiedFromTranscript, repoRoot)
   201→	newFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(newFiles, repoRoot)
   202→	deletedFiles = FilterAndNormalizePaths(deletedFiles, repoRoot)
   203→
   204→	// Print files
   205→	printFileList(w, "Modified (from transcript)", "M", modifiedFromTranscript)
   206→	printFileList(w, "New files (created during session)", "+", newFiles)
   207→	printFileList(w, "Deleted files", "D", deletedFiles)
   208→
   209→	totalChanges := len(modifiedFromTranscript) + len(newFiles) + len(deletedFiles)
   210→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   211→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nNo changes detected from transcript")
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	return totalChanges
   215→}
   216→
   217→func printGitStatusChanges(w io.Writer) (int, error) {
   218→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n(No --transcript provided, showing git status instead)")
   219→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Note: Stop hook uses transcript parsing, not git status")
   220→
   221→	modifiedFiles, untrackedFiles, deletedFiles, stagedFiles, err := getFileChanges()
   222→	if err != nil {
   223→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get file changes: %w", err)
   224→	}
   225→
   226→	printFileList(w, "Staged files", "+", stagedFiles)
   227→	printFileList(w, "Modified files", "M", modifiedFiles)
   228→	printFileList(w, "Untracked files", "?", untrackedFiles)
   229→	printFileList(w, "Deleted files", "D", deletedFiles)
   230→
   231→	totalChanges := len(modifiedFiles) + len(untrackedFiles) + len(deletedFiles) + len(stagedFiles)
   232→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   233→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "\nNo changes detected in git status")
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	return totalChanges, nil
   237→}
   238→
   239→func printFileList(w io.Writer, label, prefix string, files []string) {
   240→	if len(files) == 0 {
   241→		return
   242→	}
   243→	fmt.Fprintf(w, "\n%s (%d):\n", label, len(files))
   244→	for _, f := range files {
   245→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s %s\n", prefix, f)
   246→	}
   247→}
   248→
   249→func printDecision(w io.Writer, isAutoCommit bool, stratName string, totalChanges int) {
   250→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n=== Auto-Commit Decision ===")
   251→
   252→	wouldCommit := isAutoCommit && totalChanges > 0
   253→
   254→	if wouldCommit {
   255→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "Result: YES - Auto-commit would be triggered")
   256→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %d file(s) would be committed\n", totalChanges)
   257→		return
   258→	}
   259→
   260→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Result: NO - Auto-commit would NOT be triggered")
   261→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Reasons:")
   262→	if !isAutoCommit {
   263→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  - Strategy is not auto-commit (using %s)\n", stratName)
   264→	}
   265→	if totalChanges == 0 {
   266→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  - No file changes to commit")
   267→	}
   268→}
   269→
   270→func printTranscriptHelp(w io.Writer) {
   271→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "\n=== Finding Transcript ===")
   272→	fmt.Fprintln(w, "Claude Code transcripts are typically at:")
   273→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   274→	if err != nil {
   275→		fmt.Fprintln(w, "  ~/.claude/projects/*/sessions/*.jsonl")
   276→	} else {
   277→		fmt.Fprintf(w, "  %s/.claude/projects/*/sessions/*.jsonl\n", homeDir)
   278→	}
   279→}
   280→
   281→// getFileChanges returns the current file changes from git status.
   282→// Returns (modifiedFiles, untrackedFiles, deletedFiles, stagedFiles, error)
   283→func getFileChanges() ([]string, []string, []string, []string, error) {
   284→	repo, err := openRepository()
   285→	if err != nil {
   286→		return nil, nil, nil, nil, err
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   290→	if err != nil {
   291→		return nil, nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("getting worktree: %w", err)
   292→	}
   293→
   294→	status, err := worktree.Status()
   295→	if err != nil {
   296→		return nil, nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("getting status: %w", err)
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	var modifiedFiles, untrackedFiles, deletedFiles, stagedFiles []string
   300→
   301→	for file, st := range status {
   302→		// Skip .entire directory
   303→		if paths.IsInfrastructurePath(file) {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→
   307→		// Check staging area first
   308→		switch st.Staging {
   309→		case git.Added, git.Modified:
   310→			stagedFiles = append(stagedFiles, file)
   311→			continue
   312→		case git.Deleted:
   313→			deletedFiles = append(deletedFiles, file)
   314→			continue
   315→		case git.Unmodified, git.Renamed, git.Copied, git.UpdatedButUnmerged, git.Untracked:
   316→			// Fall through to check worktree status
   317→		}
   318→
   319→		// Check worktree status
   320→		switch st.Worktree {
   321→		case git.Modified:
   322→			modifiedFiles = append(modifiedFiles, file)
   323→		case git.Untracked:
   324→			untrackedFiles = append(untrackedFiles, file)
   325→		case git.Deleted:
   326→			deletedFiles = append(deletedFiles, file)
   327→		case git.Unmodified, git.Added, git.Renamed, git.Copied, git.UpdatedButUnmerged:
   328→			// No action needed
   329→		}
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Sort for consistent output
   333→	sort.Strings(modifiedFiles)
   334→	sort.Strings(untrackedFiles)
   335→	sort.Strings(deletedFiles)
   336→	sort.Strings(stagedFiles)
   337→
   338→	return modifiedFiles, untrackedFiles, deletedFiles, stagedFiles, nil
   339→}
   340→
   341→// findTranscriptForSession attempts to find the transcript file for a session.
   342→// Returns the path if found, empty string if not found, or error on failure.
   343→func findTranscriptForSession(sessionID string) (string, error) {
   344→	// Try to get agent type from session state
   345→	sessionState, err := strategy.LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   346→	if err != nil {
   347→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   348→	}
   349→
   350→	var ag agent.Agent
   351→	if sessionState != nil && sessionState.AgentType != "" {
   352→		ag, err = agent.GetByAgentType(sessionState.AgentType)
   353→		if err != nil {
   354→			return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent for type %q: %w", sessionState.AgentType, err)
   355→		}
   356→	} else {
   357→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent from sessionID: %s", sessionID)
   358→	}
   359→
   360→	// Resolve transcript path (checks session state's transcript_path first,
   361→	// falls back to agent's GetSessionDir + ResolveSessionFile)
   362→	transcriptPath, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, ag)
   363→	if err != nil {
   364→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to resolve transcript path: %w", err)
   365→	}
   366→
   367→	// Check if it exists
   368→	if _, err := os.Stat(transcriptPath); err != nil {
   369→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   370→			return "", nil // Not found, but not an error
   371→		}
   372→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to stat transcript: %w", err)
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return transcriptPath, nil
   376→}
   377→

    79→func runRewindInteractive() error { //nolint:maintidx // already present in codebase
    80→	// Get the configured strategy
    81→	start := GetStrategy()
    82→
    83→	// Check for uncommitted changes first
    84→	canRewind, changeMsg, err := start.CanRewind()
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→	if !canRewind {
    89→		fmt.Println(changeMsg)
    90→		return nil
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	// Get rewind points from strategy
    94→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
    95→	if err != nil {
    96→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	if len(points) == 0 {
   100→		fmt.Println("No rewind points found.")
   101→		fmt.Println("Rewind points are created automatically when agent sessions end.")
   102→		return nil
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	// Check if there are multiple sessions (to show session identifier)
   106→	sessionIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   107→	for _, p := range points {
   108→		if p.SessionID != "" {
   109→			sessionIDs[p.SessionID] = true
   110→		}
   111→	}
   112→	hasMultipleSessions := len(sessionIDs) > 1
   113→
   114→	// Build options for the select menu
   115→	options := make([]huh.Option[string], 0, len(points)+1)
   116→	for _, p := range points {
   117→		var label string
   118→		timestamp := p.Date.Format("2006-01-02 15:04")
   119→
   120→		// Build session identifier for display when multiple sessions exist
   121→		sessionLabel := ""
   122→		if hasMultipleSessions && p.SessionPrompt != "" {
   123→			// Show truncated prompt to identify the session
   124→			sessionLabel = fmt.Sprintf(" [%s]", sanitizeForTerminal(p.SessionPrompt))
   125→		}
   126→
   127→		switch {
   128→		case p.IsLogsOnly:
   129→			// Committed checkpoint - show commit sha (this is the real user commit)
   130→			shortID := p.ID
   131→			if len(shortID) >= 7 {
   132→				shortID = shortID[:7]
   133→			}
   134→			label = fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s) %s%s", shortID, timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   135→		case p.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   136→			// Task checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown
   137→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) [Task] %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   138→		default:
   139→			// Shadow checkpoint (uncommitted) - no sha shown (internal commit)
   140→			label = fmt.Sprintf("        (%s) %s%s", timestamp, sanitizeForTerminal(p.Message), sessionLabel)
   141→		}
   142→		options = append(options, huh.NewOption(label, p.ID))
   143→	}
   144→	options = append(options, huh.NewOption("Cancel", "cancel"))
   145→
   146→	var selectedID string
   147→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   148→		huh.NewGroup(
   149→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   150→				Title("Select a checkpoint to restore").
   151→				Description("Your working directory will be restored to this checkpoint's state").
   152→				Options(options...).
   153→				Value(&selectedID),
   154→		),
   155→	)
   156→
   157→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   158→		return fmt.Errorf("selection cancelled: %w", err)
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	if selectedID == "cancel" {
   162→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   163→		return nil
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	// Find the selected point
   167→	var selectedPoint *strategy.RewindPoint
   168→	for _, p := range points {
   169→		if p.ID == selectedID {
   170→			pointCopy := p
   171→			selectedPoint = &pointCopy
   172→			break
   173→		}
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	if selectedPoint == nil {
   177→		return errors.New("rewind point not found")
   178→	}
   179→
   180→	shortID := selectedPoint.ID
   181→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
   182→		shortID = shortID[:7]
   183→	}
   184→
   185→	// Show what was selected
   186→	switch {
   187→	case selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly:
   188→		// Committed checkpoint - show sha
   189→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s %s\n", shortID, sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   190→	case selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint:
   191→		// Task checkpoint - no sha
   192→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: [Task] %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   193→	default:
   194→		// Shadow checkpoint - no sha
   195→		fmt.Printf("\nSelected: %s\n", sanitizeForTerminal(selectedPoint.Message))
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Handle logs-only points with a sub-choice menu
   199→	if selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly {
   200→		return handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive(start, *selectedPoint, shortID)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Preview rewind to show warnings about files that will be deleted
   204→	preview, previewErr := start.PreviewRewind(*selectedPoint)
   205→	if previewErr == nil && preview != nil && len(preview.FilesToDelete) > 0 {
   206→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: The following untracked files will be DELETED:\n")
   207→		for _, f := range preview.FilesToDelete {
   208→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", f)
   209→		}
   210→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n")
   211→	}
   212→
   213→	// Confirm rewind
   214→	var confirm bool
   215→	description := fmt.Sprintf("This will reset to: %s\nChanges after this point may be lost!", selectedPoint.Message)
   216→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   217→		huh.NewGroup(
   218→			huh.NewConfirm().
   219→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Reset to %s?", shortID)).
   220→				Description(description).
   221→				Value(&confirm),
   222→		),
   223→	)
   224→
   225→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
   226→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	if !confirm {
   230→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   231→		return nil
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   235→	agent, err := getAgent(selectedPoint.Agent)
   236→	if err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   241→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   242→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   243→
   244→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind started",
   245→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   246→		slog.String("session_id", selectedPoint.SessionID),
   247→		slog.Bool("is_task_checkpoint", selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint),
   248→	)
   249→
   250→	// Perform the rewind using strategy
   251→	if err := start.Rewind(*selectedPoint); err != nil {
   252→		logging.Error(ctx, "rewind failed",
   253→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   254→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   255→		)
   256→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind completed",
   260→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   261→	)
   262→
   263→	// Handle transcript restoration differently for task checkpoints
   264→	var sessionID string
   265→	var transcriptFile string
   266→
   267→	if selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   268→		// For task checkpoint: read checkpoint.json to get UUID and truncate transcript
   269→		checkpoint, err := start.GetTaskCheckpoint(*selectedPoint)
   270→		if err != nil {
   271→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read task checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   272→			return nil
   273→		}
   274→
   275→		sessionID = checkpoint.SessionID
   276→
   277→		if checkpoint.CheckpointUUID != "" {
   278→			// Truncate transcript at checkpoint UUID
   279→			if err := restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(start, *selectedPoint, sessionID, checkpoint.CheckpointUUID, agent); err != nil {
   280→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore truncated session transcript: %v\n", err)
   281→			} else {
   282→				fmt.Printf("Rewound to task checkpoint. %s\n", agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   283→			}
   284→			return nil
   285→		}
   286→	} else {
   287→		// For session checkpoint: restore full transcript
   288→		// Prefer SessionID from trailer (set by GetRewindPoints from Entire-Session trailer)
   289→		// over path-based extraction which is less reliable.
   290→		sessionID = selectedPoint.SessionID
   291→		if sessionID == "" {
   292→			sessionID = filepath.Base(selectedPoint.MetadataDir)
   293→		}
   294→		transcriptFile = filepath.Join(selectedPoint.MetadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Try to restore transcript using the appropriate method:
   298→	// 1. Checkpoint storage (committed checkpoints with valid checkpoint ID)
   299→	// 2. Shadow branch (uncommitted checkpoints with commit hash)
   300→	// 3. Local file (active sessions)
   301→	var restored bool
   302→	if !selectedPoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   303→		// Try checkpoint storage first for committed checkpoints
   304→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(selectedPoint.CheckpointID, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   305→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   306→			restored = true
   307→		}
   308→	}
   309→
   310→	if !restored && selectedPoint.MetadataDir != "" && len(selectedPoint.ID) == 40 {
   311→		// Try shadow branch for uncommitted checkpoints (ID is a 40-char commit hash)
   312→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(selectedPoint.ID, selectedPoint.MetadataDir, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   313→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   314→			restored = true
   315→		}
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	if !restored {
   319→		// Fall back to local file
   320→		if err := restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID, agent); err != nil {
   321→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore session transcript: %v\n", err)
   322→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Source: %s\n", transcriptFile)
   323→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session ID: %s\n", sessionID)
   324→		}
   325→	}
   326→
   327→	fmt.Printf("Rewound to %s. %s\n", shortID, agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   328→	return nil
   329→}
   330→
   331→func runRewindList() error {
   332→	start := GetStrategy()
   333→
   334→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
   335→	if err != nil {
   336→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   337→	}
   338→
   339→	// Output as JSON for programmatic use
   340→	type jsonPoint struct {
   341→		ID               string `json:"id"`
   342→		Message          string `json:"message"`
   343→		MetadataDir      string `json:"metadata_dir"`
   344→		Date             string `json:"date"`
   345→		IsTaskCheckpoint bool   `json:"is_task_checkpoint"`
   346→		ToolUseID        string `json:"tool_use_id,omitempty"`
   347→		IsLogsOnly       bool   `json:"is_logs_only"`
   348→		CondensationID   string `json:"condensation_id,omitempty"`
   349→		SessionID        string `json:"session_id,omitempty"`
   350→		SessionPrompt    string `json:"session_prompt,omitempty"`
   351→	}
   352→
   353→	output := make([]jsonPoint, len(points))
   354→	for i, p := range points {
   355→		output[i] = jsonPoint{
   356→			ID:               p.ID,
   357→			Message:          p.Message,
   358→			MetadataDir:      p.MetadataDir,
   359→			Date:             p.Date.Format(time.RFC3339),
   360→			IsTaskCheckpoint: p.IsTaskCheckpoint,
   361→			ToolUseID:        p.ToolUseID,
   362→			IsLogsOnly:       p.IsLogsOnly,
   363→			CondensationID:   p.CheckpointID.String(),
   364→			SessionID:        p.SessionID,
   365→			SessionPrompt:    p.SessionPrompt,
   366→		}
   367→	}
   368→
   369→	// Print as JSON
   370→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(output, "", "  ")
   371→	if err != nil {
   372→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   373→	}
   374→	fmt.Println(string(data))
   375→	return nil
   376→}
   377→
   378→func runRewindToWithOptions(commitID string, logsOnly bool, reset bool) error {
   379→	return runRewindToInternal(commitID, logsOnly, reset)
   380→}
   381→
   382→func runRewindToInternal(commitID string, logsOnly bool, reset bool) error {
   383→	start := GetStrategy()
   384→
   385→	// Check for uncommitted changes (skip for reset which handles this itself)
   386→	if !reset {
   387→		canRewind, changeMsg, err := start.CanRewind()
   388→		if err != nil {
   389→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
   390→		}
   391→		if !canRewind {
   392→			return fmt.Errorf("%s", changeMsg)
   393→		}
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	// Get rewind points
   397→	points, err := start.GetRewindPoints(20)
   398→	if err != nil {
   399→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to find rewind points: %w", err)
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	// Find the matching point (support both full and short commit IDs)
   403→	var selectedPoint *strategy.RewindPoint
   404→	for _, p := range points {
   405→		if p.ID == commitID || (len(commitID) >= 7 && len(p.ID) >= 7 && strings.HasPrefix(p.ID, commitID)) {
   406→			pointCopy := p
   407→			selectedPoint = &pointCopy
   408→			break
   409→		}
   410→	}
   411→
   412→	if selectedPoint == nil {
   413→		return fmt.Errorf("rewind point not found: %s", commitID)
   414→	}
   415→
   416→	// Handle reset mode (for logs-only points)
   417→	if reset {
   418→		return handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive(start, *selectedPoint)
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	// Handle logs-only restoration:
   422→	// 1. For logs-only points, always use logs-only restoration
   423→	// 2. If --logs-only flag is set, use logs-only restoration even for checkpoint points
   424→	if selectedPoint.IsLogsOnly || logsOnly {
   425→		return handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive(start, *selectedPoint)
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	// Preview rewind to show warnings about files that will be deleted
   429→	preview, previewErr := start.PreviewRewind(*selectedPoint)
   430→	if previewErr == nil && preview != nil && len(preview.FilesToDelete) > 0 {
   431→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: The following untracked files will be DELETED:\n")
   432→		for _, f := range preview.FilesToDelete {
   433→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  - %s\n", f)
   434→		}
   435→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\n")
   436→	}
   437→
   438→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   439→	agent, err := getAgent(selectedPoint.Agent)
   440→	if err != nil {
   441→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   442→	}
   443→
   444→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   445→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   446→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   447→
   448→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind started",
   449→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   450→		slog.String("session_id", selectedPoint.SessionID),
   451→		slog.Bool("is_task_checkpoint", selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint),
   452→	)
   453→
   454→	// Perform the rewind
   455→	if err := start.Rewind(*selectedPoint); err != nil {
   456→		logging.Error(ctx, "rewind failed",
   457→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   458→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   459→		)
   460→		return err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
   461→	}
   462→
   463→	logging.Debug(ctx, "rewind completed",
   464→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", selectedPoint.ID),
   465→	)
   466→
   467→	// Handle transcript restoration
   468→	var sessionID string
   469→	var transcriptFile string
   470→
   471→	if selectedPoint.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   472→		checkpoint, err := start.GetTaskCheckpoint(*selectedPoint)
   473→		if err != nil {
   474→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to read task checkpoint: %v\n", err)
   475→			return nil
   476→		}
   477→
   478→		sessionID = checkpoint.SessionID
   479→
   480→		if checkpoint.CheckpointUUID != "" {
   481→			// Use strategy-based transcript restoration for task checkpoints
   482→			if err := restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(start, *selectedPoint, sessionID, checkpoint.CheckpointUUID, agent); err != nil {
   483→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore truncated session transcript: %v\n", err)
   484→			} else {
   485→				fmt.Printf("Rewound to task checkpoint. %s\n", agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   486→			}
   487→			return nil
   488→		}
   489→	} else {
   490→		// Prefer SessionID from trailer over path-based extraction
   491→		sessionID = selectedPoint.SessionID
   492→		if sessionID == "" {
   493→			sessionID = filepath.Base(selectedPoint.MetadataDir)
   494→		}
   495→		transcriptFile = filepath.Join(selectedPoint.MetadataDir, paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy)
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	// Try to restore transcript using the appropriate method:
   499→	// 1. Checkpoint storage (committed checkpoints with valid checkpoint ID)
   500→	// 2. Shadow branch (uncommitted checkpoints with commit hash)
   501→	// 3. Local file (active sessions)
   502→	var restored bool
   503→	if !selectedPoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   504→		// Try checkpoint storage first for committed checkpoints
   505→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(selectedPoint.CheckpointID, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   506→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   507→			restored = true
   508→		}
   509→	}
   510→
   511→	if !restored && selectedPoint.MetadataDir != "" && len(selectedPoint.ID) == 40 {
   512→		// Try shadow branch for uncommitted checkpoints (ID is a 40-char commit hash)
   513→		if returnedSessionID, err := restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(selectedPoint.ID, selectedPoint.MetadataDir, sessionID, agent); err == nil {
   514→			sessionID = returnedSessionID
   515→			restored = true
   516→		}
   517→	}
   518→
   519→	if !restored {
   520→		// Fall back to local file
   521→		if err := restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID, agent); err != nil {
   522→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to restore session transcript: %v\n", err)
   523→		}
   524→	}
   525→
   526→	fmt.Printf("Rewound to %s. %s\n", selectedPoint.ID[:7], agent.FormatResumeCommand(sessionID))
   527→	return nil
   528→}
   529→
   530→// handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive handles logs-only rewind in non-interactive mode.
   531→// Defaults to restoring logs only (no checkout) for safety.
   532→func handleLogsOnlyRewindNonInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   533→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   534→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   535→	if err != nil {
   536→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   537→	}
   538→
   539→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   540→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   541→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   542→
   543→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only rewind started",
   544→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   545→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   546→	)
   547→
   548→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   549→	if !ok {
   550→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   551→	}
   552→
   553→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   554→	if err != nil {
   555→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only rewind failed",
   556→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   557→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   558→		)
   559→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only rewind completed",
   563→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   564→	)
   565→
   566→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   567→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   568→
   569→	fmt.Println("Note: Working directory unchanged. Use interactive mode for full checkout.")
   570→	return nil
   571→}
   572→
   573→// handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive handles reset in non-interactive mode.
   574→// This performs a git reset --hard to the target commit.
   575→func handleLogsOnlyResetNonInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   576→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   577→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   578→	if err != nil {
   579→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   583→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   584→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   585→
   586→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset started",
   587→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   588→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   589→	)
   590→
   591→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   592→	if !ok {
   593→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Get current HEAD before reset (for recovery message)
   597→	currentHead, headErr := getCurrentHeadHash()
   598→	if headErr != nil {
   599→		currentHead = ""
   600→	}
   601→
   602→	// Restore logs first
   603→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   604→	if err != nil {
   605→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during log restoration",
   606→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   607→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   608→		)
   609→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   610→	}
   611→
   612→	// Perform git reset --hard
   613→	if err := performGitResetHard(point.ID); err != nil {
   614→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during git reset",
   615→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   616→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   617→		)
   618→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset branch: %w", err)
   619→	}
   620→
   621→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset completed",
   622→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   623→	)
   624→
   625→	shortID := point.ID
   626→	if len(shortID) > 7 {
   627→		shortID = shortID[:7]
   628→	}
   629→
   630→	fmt.Printf("Reset branch to %s.\n", shortID)
   631→
   632→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   633→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   634→
   635→	// Show recovery instructions
   636→	if currentHead != "" && currentHead != point.ID {
   637→		currentShort := currentHead
   638→		if len(currentShort) > 7 {
   639→			currentShort = currentShort[:7]
   640→		}
   641→		fmt.Printf("\nTo undo this reset: git reset --hard %s\n", currentShort)
   642→	}
   643→
   644→	return nil
   645→}
   646→
   647→func restoreSessionTranscript(transcriptFile, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) error {
   648→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   649→	if err != nil {
   650→		return err
   651→	}
   652→
   653→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   654→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   655→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   656→	}
   657→
   658→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Copying transcript:\n  From: %s\n  To: %s\n", transcriptFile, sessionFile)
   659→	if err := copyFile(transcriptFile, sessionFile); err != nil {
   660→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy transcript: %w", err)
   661→	}
   662→
   663→	return nil
   664→}
   665→
   666→// restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy restores a session transcript from checkpoint storage.
   667→// This is used for strategies that store transcripts in git branches rather than local files.
   668→// Returns the session ID that was actually used (may differ from input if checkpoint provides one).
   669→func restoreSessionTranscriptFromStrategy(cpID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   670→	// Get transcript content from checkpoint storage
   671→	content, returnedSessionID, err := checkpoint.LookupSessionLog(cpID)
   672→	if err != nil {
   673→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Use session ID returned from checkpoint if available
   677→	// Otherwise fall back to the passed-in sessionID
   678→	if returnedSessionID != "" {
   679→		sessionID = returnedSessionID
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	return writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content, sessionID, agent)
   683→}
   684→
   685→// restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow restores a session transcript from a shadow branch commit.
   686→// This is used for uncommitted checkpoints where the transcript is stored in the shadow branch tree.
   687→func restoreSessionTranscriptFromShadow(commitHash, metadataDir, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   688→	// Open repository
   689→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
   690→	if err != nil {
   691→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	// Parse commit hash
   695→	hash := plumbing.NewHash(commitHash)
   696→	if hash.IsZero() {
   697→		return "", fmt.Errorf("invalid commit hash: %s", commitHash)
   698→	}
   699→
   700→	// Get transcript from shadow branch commit tree
   701→	store := checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
   702→	content, err := store.GetTranscriptFromCommit(hash, metadataDir, agent.Type())
   703→	if err != nil {
   704→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get transcript from shadow branch: %w", err)
   705→	}
   706→
   707→	return writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content, sessionID, agent)
   708→}
   709→
   710→// writeTranscriptToAgentSession writes transcript content to the agent's session storage.
   711→func writeTranscriptToAgentSession(content []byte, sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   712→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   713→	if err != nil {
   714→		return "", err
   715→	}
   716→
   717→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   718→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   719→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   720→	}
   721→
   722→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   723→	if err := os.WriteFile(sessionFile, content, 0o600); err != nil {
   724→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   725→	}
   726→
   727→	return sessionID, nil
   728→}
   729→
   730→// resolveTranscriptPath determines the correct file path for an agent's session transcript.
   731→// Delegates to strategy.ResolveSessionFilePath after computing the fallback session directory.
   732→func resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) (string, error) {
   733→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   734→	if err != nil {
   735→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   736→	}
   737→
   738→	agentSessionDir, err := agent.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   739→	if err != nil {
   740→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent session directory: %w", err)
   741→	}
   742→
   743→	return strategy.ResolveSessionFilePath(sessionID, agent, agentSessionDir), nil
   744→}
   745→
   746→// restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript restores a truncated transcript for a task checkpoint.
   747→// Uses GetTaskCheckpointTranscript to fetch the transcript from the strategy.
   748→//
   749→// NOTE: The transcript parsing/truncation/writing pipeline (parseTranscriptFromBytes,
   750→// TruncateTranscriptAtUUID, writeTranscript) assumes Claude's JSONL format.
   751→// This is acceptable because task checkpoints are currently only created by Claude Code's
   752→// PostToolUse hook. If other agents gain sub-agent support, this will need a
   753→// format-aware refactor (agent-specific parsing, truncation, and serialization).
   754→func restoreTaskCheckpointTranscript(strat strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, sessionID, checkpointUUID string, agent agentpkg.Agent) error {
   755→	// Get transcript content from strategy
   756→	content, err := strat.GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point)
   757→	if err != nil {
   758→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get task checkpoint transcript: %w", err)
   759→	}
   760→
   761→	// Parse the transcript
   762→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromBytes(content)
   763→	if err != nil {
   764→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   765→	}
   766→
   767→	// Truncate at checkpoint UUID
   768→	truncated := TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(transcript, checkpointUUID)
   769→
   770→	sessionFile, err := resolveTranscriptPath(sessionID, agent)
   771→	if err != nil {
   772→		return err
   773→	}
   774→
   775→	// Ensure parent directory exists
   776→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(sessionFile), 0o750); err != nil {
   777→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create agent session directory: %w", err)
   778→	}
   779→
   780→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing truncated transcript to: %s\n", sessionFile)
   781→
   782→	if err := writeTranscript(sessionFile, truncated); err != nil {
   783→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write truncated transcript: %w", err)
   784→	}
   785→
   786→	return nil
   787→}
   788→
   789→// createContextFileMinimal creates a context file without staged files info
   790→// (since the strategy will handle staging)
   791→func createContextFileMinimal(contextFile, commitMessage, sessionID, promptFile, summaryFile string, transcript []transcriptLine) error {
   792→	prompt, _ := os.ReadFile(promptFile)   //nolint:errcheck,gosec // Best-effort loading of optional context files
   793→	summary, _ := os.ReadFile(summaryFile) //nolint:errcheck,gosec // Best-effort loading of optional context files
   794→	keyActions := extractKeyActions(transcript, 10)
   795→
   796→	var content strings.Builder
   797→	content.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   798→	content.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("**Session ID:** %s\n\n", sessionID))
   799→	content.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("**Commit Message:** %s\n\n", commitMessage))
   800→	content.WriteString("## Prompt\n\n")
   801→	content.Write(prompt)
   802→	content.WriteString("\n\n## Summary\n\n")
   803→	content.Write(summary)
   804→	content.WriteString("\n\n## Key Actions\n\n")
   805→	for _, action := range keyActions {
   806→		content.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("- %s\n", action))
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	if err := os.WriteFile(contextFile, []byte(content.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   810→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write context file: %w", err)
   811→	}
   812→	return nil
   813→}
   814→
   815→// handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive handles rewind for logs-only points with a sub-choice menu.
   816→func handleLogsOnlyRewindInteractive(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   817→	var action string
   818→
   819→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   820→		huh.NewGroup(
   821→			huh.NewSelect[string]().
   822→				Title("Logs-only point: "+shortID).
   823→				Description("This commit has session logs but no checkpoint state. Choose an action:").
   824→				Options(
   825→					huh.NewOption("Restore logs only (keep current files)", "logs"),
   826→					huh.NewOption("Checkout commit (detached HEAD, for viewing)", "checkout"),
   827→					huh.NewOption("Reset branch to this commit (destructive!)", "reset"),
   828→					huh.NewOption("Cancel", "cancel"),
   829→				).
   830→				Value(&action),
   831→		),
   832→	)
   833→
   834→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   835→		return fmt.Errorf("action selection cancelled: %w", err)
   836→	}
   837→
   838→	switch action {
   839→	case "logs":
   840→		return handleLogsOnlyRestore(start, point)
   841→	case "checkout":
   842→		return handleLogsOnlyCheckout(start, point, shortID)
   843→	case "reset":
   844→		return handleLogsOnlyReset(start, point, shortID)
   845→	case "cancel":
   846→		fmt.Println("Rewind cancelled.")
   847→		return nil
   848→	}
   849→
   850→	return nil
   851→}
   852→
   853→// handleLogsOnlyRestore restores only the session logs without changing files.
   854→func handleLogsOnlyRestore(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint) error {
   855→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   856→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   857→	if err != nil {
   858→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   859→	}
   860→
   861→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   862→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   863→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   864→
   865→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only restore started",
   866→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   867→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   868→	)
   869→
   870→	// Check if strategy supports logs-only restoration
   871→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   872→	if !ok {
   873→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   874→	}
   875→
   876→	// Restore logs
   877→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   878→	if err != nil {
   879→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only restore failed",
   880→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   881→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   882→		)
   883→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   884→	}
   885→
   886→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only restore completed",
   887→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   888→	)
   889→
   890→	// Show resume commands for all sessions
   891→	fmt.Println("Restored session logs.")
   892→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   893→	return nil
   894→}
   895→
   896→// handleLogsOnlyCheckout restores logs and checks out the commit (detached HEAD).
   897→func handleLogsOnlyCheckout(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   898→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   899→	agent, err := getAgent(point.Agent)
   900→	if err != nil {
   901→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", err)
   902→	}
   903→
   904→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   905→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   906→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   907→
   908→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only checkout started",
   909→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   910→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   911→	)
   912→
   913→	// First, restore the logs
   914→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   915→	if !ok {
   916→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   917→	}
   918→
   919→	sessions, err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   920→	if err != nil {
   921→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only checkout failed during log restoration",
   922→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   923→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   924→		)
   925→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", err)
   926→	}
   927→
   928→	// Show warning about detached HEAD
   929→	var confirm bool
   930→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
   931→		huh.NewGroup(
   932→			huh.NewConfirm().
   933→				Title("Create detached HEAD?").
   934→				Description("This will checkout the commit directly. You'll be in 'detached HEAD' state.\nAny uncommitted changes will be lost!").
   935→				Value(&confirm),
   936→		),
   937→	)
   938→
   939→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
   940→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
   941→	}
   942→
   943→	if !confirm {
   944→		fmt.Println("Checkout cancelled. Session logs were still restored.")
   945→		printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   946→		return nil
   947→	}
   948→
   949→	// Perform git checkout
   950→	if err := CheckoutBranch(point.ID); err != nil {
   951→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only checkout failed during git checkout",
   952→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   953→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   954→		)
   955→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to checkout commit: %w", err)
   956→	}
   957→
   958→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only checkout completed",
   959→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   960→	)
   961→
   962→	fmt.Printf("Checked out %s (detached HEAD).\n", shortID)
   963→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
   964→	return nil
   965→}
   966→
   967→// handleLogsOnlyReset restores logs and resets the branch to the commit (destructive).
   968→func handleLogsOnlyReset(start strategy.Strategy, point strategy.RewindPoint, shortID string) error {
   969→	// Resolve agent once for use throughout
   970→	agent, agentErr := getAgent(point.Agent)
   971→	if agentErr != nil {
   972→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get agent: %w", agentErr)
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Initialize logging context with agent from checkpoint
   976→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "rewind")
   977→	ctx = logging.WithAgent(ctx, agent.Name())
   978→
   979→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset (interactive) started",
   980→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   981→		slog.String("session_id", point.SessionID),
   982→	)
   983→
   984→	// First, restore the logs
   985→	restorer, ok := start.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer)
   986→	if !ok {
   987→		return errors.New("strategy does not support logs-only restoration")
   988→	}
   989→
   990→	sessions, restoreErr := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, true) // force=true for explicit rewind
   991→	if restoreErr != nil {
   992→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during log restoration",
   993→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
   994→			slog.String("error", restoreErr.Error()),
   995→		)
   996→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to restore logs: %w", restoreErr)
   997→	}
   998→
   999→	// Get current HEAD before reset (for recovery message)
  1000→	currentHead, err := getCurrentHeadHash()
  1001→	if err != nil {
  1002→		// Non-fatal - just won't show recovery message
  1003→		currentHead = ""
  1004→	}
  1005→
  1006→	// Get detailed uncommitted changes warning from strategy
  1007→	var uncommittedWarning string
  1008→	if _, warn, err := start.CanRewind(); err == nil {
  1009→		uncommittedWarning = warn
  1010→	}
  1011→
  1012→	// Check for safety issues
  1013→	warnings, err := checkResetSafety(point.ID, uncommittedWarning)
  1014→	if err != nil {
  1015→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check reset safety: %w", err)
  1016→	}
  1017→
  1018→	// Build confirmation message based on warnings
  1019→	var confirmTitle, confirmDesc string
  1020→	if len(warnings) > 0 {
  1021→		confirmTitle = "⚠️  Reset branch with warnings?"
  1022→		confirmDesc = "WARNING - the following issues were detected:\n" +
  1023→			strings.Join(warnings, "\n") +
  1024→			"\n\nThis will move your branch to " + shortID + " and DISCARD commits after it!"
  1025→	} else {
  1026→		confirmTitle = "Reset branch to " + shortID + "?"
  1027→		confirmDesc = "This will move your branch pointer to this commit.\nCommits after this point will be orphaned (but recoverable via reflog)."
  1028→	}
  1029→
  1030→	var confirm bool
  1031→	confirmForm := NewAccessibleForm(
  1032→		huh.NewGroup(
  1033→			huh.NewConfirm().
  1034→				Title(confirmTitle).
  1035→				Description(confirmDesc).
  1036→				Value(&confirm),
  1037→		),
  1038→	)
  1039→
  1040→	if err := confirmForm.Run(); err != nil {
  1041→		return fmt.Errorf("confirmation cancelled: %w", err)
  1042→	}
  1043→
  1044→	if !confirm {
  1045→		fmt.Println("Reset cancelled. Session logs were still restored.")
  1046→		printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
  1047→		return nil
  1048→	}
  1049→
  1050→	// Perform git reset --hard
  1051→	if err := performGitResetHard(point.ID); err != nil {
  1052→		logging.Error(ctx, "logs-only reset failed during git reset",
  1053→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
  1054→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1055→		)
  1056→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to reset branch: %w", err)
  1057→	}
  1058→
  1059→	logging.Debug(ctx, "logs-only reset (interactive) completed",
  1060→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", point.ID),
  1061→	)
  1062→
  1063→	fmt.Printf("Reset branch to %s.\n", shortID)
  1064→	printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions)
  1065→
  1066→	// Show recovery instructions
  1067→	if currentHead != "" && currentHead != point.ID {
  1068→		currentShort := currentHead
  1069→		if len(currentShort) > 7 {
  1070→			currentShort = currentShort[:7]
  1071→		}
  1072→		fmt.Printf("\nTo undo this reset: git reset --hard %s\n", currentShort)
  1073→	}
  1074→
  1075→	return nil
  1076→}
  1077→
  1078→// getCurrentHeadHash returns the current HEAD commit hash.
  1079→func getCurrentHeadHash() (string, error) {
  1080→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1081→	if err != nil {
  1082→		return "", err
  1083→	}
  1084→
  1085→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1086→	if err != nil {
  1087→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
  1088→	}
  1089→
  1090→	return head.Hash().String(), nil
  1091→}
  1092→
  1093→// checkResetSafety checks for potential issues before a git reset --hard.
  1094→// Returns a list of warning messages (empty if safe to proceed without warnings).
  1095→// If uncommittedChangesWarning is provided, it will be used instead of a generic warning.
  1096→func checkResetSafety(targetCommitHash string, uncommittedChangesWarning string) ([]string, error) {
  1097→	var warnings []string
  1098→
  1099→	repo, err := openRepository()
  1100→	if err != nil {
  1101→		return nil, err
  1102→	}
  1103→
  1104→	// Check for uncommitted changes
  1105→	if uncommittedChangesWarning != "" {
  1106→		// Use the detailed warning from strategy's CanRewind()
  1107→		warnings = append(warnings, uncommittedChangesWarning)
  1108→	} else {
  1109→		// Fall back to generic check
  1110→		worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
  1111→		if err != nil {
  1112→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
  1113→		}
  1114→
  1115→		status, err := worktree.Status()
  1116→		if err != nil {
  1117→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get status: %w", err)
  1118→		}
  1119→
  1120→		if !status.IsClean() {
  1121→			warnings = append(warnings, "• You have uncommitted changes that will be LOST")
  1122→		}
  1123→	}
  1124→
  1125→	// Check if current HEAD is ahead of target (we'd be discarding commits)
  1126→	head, err := repo.Head()
  1127→	if err != nil {
  1128→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
  1129→	}
  1130→
  1131→	targetHash := plumbing.NewHash(targetCommitHash)
  1132→
  1133→	// Count commits between target and HEAD
  1134→	commitsAhead, err := countCommitsBetween(repo, targetHash, head.Hash())
  1135→	if err != nil {
  1136→		// Non-fatal - just can't show commit count
  1137→		commitsAhead = -1
  1138→	}
  1139→
  1140→	if commitsAhead > 0 {
  1141→		warnings = append(warnings, fmt.Sprintf("• %d commit(s) after this point will be orphaned", commitsAhead))
  1142→	}
  1143→
  1144→	return warnings, nil
  1145→}
  1146→
  1147→// countCommitsBetween counts commits between ancestor and descendant.
  1148→// Returns 0 if ancestor == descendant, -1 on error.
  1149→func countCommitsBetween(repo *git.Repository, ancestor, descendant plumbing.Hash) (int, error) {
  1150→	if ancestor == descendant {
  1151→		return 0, nil
  1152→	}
  1153→
  1154→	// Walk from descendant back to ancestor
  1155→	count := 0
  1156→	current := descendant
  1157→
  1158→	for count < 1000 { // Safety limit
  1159→		if current == ancestor {
  1160→			return count, nil
  1161→		}
  1162→
  1163→		commit, err := repo.CommitObject(current)
  1164→		if err != nil {
  1165→			return -1, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
  1166→		}
  1167→
  1168→		if commit.NumParents() == 0 {
  1169→			// Reached root without finding ancestor - ancestor not in history
  1170→			return -1, nil
  1171→		}
  1172→
  1173→		count++
  1174→		current = commit.ParentHashes[0] // Follow first parent
  1175→	}
  1176→
  1177→	return -1, nil
  1178→}
  1179→
  1180→// performGitResetHard performs a git reset --hard to the specified commit.
  1181→// Uses the git CLI instead of go-git because go-git's HardReset incorrectly
  1182→// deletes untracked directories (like .entire/) even when they're in .gitignore.
  1183→func performGitResetHard(commitHash string) error {
  1184→	ctx := context.Background()
  1185→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "reset", "--hard", commitHash)
  1186→	if output, err := cmd.CombinedOutput(); err != nil {
  1187→		return fmt.Errorf("reset failed: %s: %w", strings.TrimSpace(string(output)), err)
  1188→	}
  1189→	return nil
  1190→}
  1191→
  1192→// sanitizeForTerminal removes or replaces characters that cause rendering issues
  1193→// in terminal UI components. This includes emojis with skin-tone modifiers and
  1194→// other multi-codepoint characters that confuse width calculations.
  1195→func sanitizeForTerminal(s string) string {
  1196→	var result strings.Builder
  1197→	result.Grow(len(s))
  1198→
  1199→	for _, r := range s {
  1200→		// Skip emoji skin tone modifiers (U+1F3FB to U+1F3FF)
  1201→		if r >= 0x1F3FB && r <= 0x1F3FF {
  1202→			continue
  1203→		}
  1204→		// Skip zero-width joiners used in emoji sequences
  1205→		if r == 0x200D {
  1206→			continue
  1207→		}
  1208→		// Skip variation selectors (U+FE00 to U+FE0F)
  1209→		if r >= 0xFE00 && r <= 0xFE0F {
  1210→			continue
  1211→		}
  1212→		// Keep printable characters and common whitespace
  1213→		if unicode.IsPrint(r) || r == '\t' || r == '\n' {
  1214→			result.WriteRune(r)
  1215→		}
  1216→	}
  1217→
  1218→	return result.String()
  1219→}
  1220→
  1221→// printMultiSessionResumeCommands prints resume commands for restored sessions.
  1222→// Each session may have a different agent, so per-session agent resolution is used.
  1223→func printMultiSessionResumeCommands(sessions []strategy.RestoredSession) {
  1224→	if len(sessions) == 0 {
  1225→		return
  1226→	}
  1227→
  1228→	if len(sessions) > 1 {
  1229→		fmt.Printf("\nRestored %d sessions. Resume with:\n", len(sessions))
  1230→	}
  1231→
  1232→	for i, sess := range sessions {
  1233→		ag, err := strategy.ResolveAgentForRewind(sess.Agent)
  1234→		if err != nil {
  1235→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: could not resolve agent %q for session %s, skipping\n", sess.Agent, sess.SessionID)
  1236→			continue
  1237→		}
  1238→
  1239→		cmd := ag.FormatResumeCommand(sess.SessionID)
  1240→
  1241→		if len(sessions) > 1 {
  1242→			// Add "(most recent)" label to the last session
  1243→			if i == len(sessions)-1 {
  1244→				if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1245→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # %s (most recent)\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1246→				} else {
  1247→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # (most recent)\n", cmd)
  1248→				}
  1249→			} else {
  1250→				if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1251→					fmt.Printf("  %s  # %s\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1252→				} else {
  1253→					fmt.Printf("  %s\n", cmd)
  1254→				}
  1255→			}
  1256→		} else {
  1257→			if sess.Prompt != "" {
  1258→				fmt.Printf("%s  # %s\n", cmd, sess.Prompt)
  1259→			} else {
  1260→				fmt.Printf("%s\n", cmd)
  1261→			}
  1262→		}
  1263→	}
  1264→}
  1265→

    49→// DetectPresence checks if Claude Code is configured in the repository.
    50→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) DetectPresence() (bool, error) {
    51→	// Get repo root to check for .claude directory
    52→	// This is needed because the CLI may be run from a subdirectory
    53→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    54→	if err != nil {
    55→		// Not in a git repo, fall back to CWD-relative check
    56→		repoRoot = "."
    57→	}
    58→
    59→	// Check for .claude directory
    60→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude")
    61→	if _, err := os.Stat(claudeDir); err == nil {
    62→		return true, nil
    63→	}
    64→	// Check for .claude/settings.json
    65→	settingsFile := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", "settings.json")
    66→	if _, err := os.Stat(settingsFile); err == nil {
    67→		return true, nil
    68→	}
    69→	return false, nil
    70→}

    82→// ParseHookInput parses Claude Code hook input from stdin.
    83→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ParseHookInput(hookType agent.HookType, reader io.Reader) (*agent.HookInput, error) {
    84→	data, err := io.ReadAll(reader)
    85→	if err != nil {
    86→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read input: %w", err)
    87→	}
    88→
    89→	if len(data) == 0 {
    90→		return nil, errors.New("empty input")
    91→	}
    92→
    93→	input := &agent.HookInput{
    94→		HookType:  hookType,
    95→		Timestamp: time.Now(),
    96→		RawData:   make(map[string]interface{}),
    97→	}
    98→
    99→	// Parse based on hook type
   100→	switch hookType {
   101→	case agent.HookUserPromptSubmit:
   102→		var raw userPromptSubmitRaw
   103→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   104→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse user prompt submit: %w", err)
   105→		}
   106→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   107→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   108→		input.UserPrompt = raw.Prompt
   109→
   110→	case agent.HookSessionStart, agent.HookSessionEnd, agent.HookStop:
   111→		var raw sessionInfoRaw
   112→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   113→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse session info: %w", err)
   114→		}
   115→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   116→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   117→
   118→	case agent.HookPreToolUse:
   119→		var raw taskHookInputRaw
   120→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   121→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse pre-tool input: %w", err)
   122→		}
   123→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   124→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   125→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   126→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   127→
   128→	case agent.HookPostToolUse:
   129→		var raw postToolHookInputRaw
   130→		if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   131→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse post-tool input: %w", err)
   132→		}
   133→		input.SessionID = raw.SessionID
   134→		input.SessionRef = raw.TranscriptPath
   135→		input.ToolUseID = raw.ToolUseID
   136→		input.ToolInput = raw.ToolInput
   137→		// Store agent ID in raw data for Task tool results
   138→		if raw.ToolResponse.AgentID != "" {
   139→			input.RawData["agent_id"] = raw.ToolResponse.AgentID
   140→		}
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	return input, nil
   144→}

   160→// GetSessionDir returns the directory where Claude stores session transcripts.
   161→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error) {
   162→	// Check for test environment override
   163→	if override := os.Getenv("ENTIRE_TEST_CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR"); override != "" {
   164→		return override, nil
   165→	}
   166→
   167→	homeDir, err := os.UserHomeDir()
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get home directory: %w", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	projectDir := SanitizePathForClaude(repoPath)
   173→	return filepath.Join(homeDir, ".claude", "projects", projectDir), nil
   174→}

   176→// ReadSession reads a session from Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   177→// The session data is stored in NativeData as raw JSONL bytes.
   178→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   179→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   180→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   181→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   185→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   191→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   197→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   198→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   199→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   200→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   201→		NativeData:    data,
   202→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   203→	}, nil
   204→}
   205→
   206→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   207→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   208→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   209→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   210→	if session == nil {
   211→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   215→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   216→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   220→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   224→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   228→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   229→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	return nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   236→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   237→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   238→}
   239→
   240→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   241→
   242→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   243→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   244→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   245→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   246→		return ""
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return ""
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   255→}
   256→
   257→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   258→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   259→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   260→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   261→	if session == nil {
   262→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   266→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	if uuid == "" {
   270→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   271→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   272→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   273→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   274→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   275→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   276→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   277→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   278→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   279→		}, nil
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   283→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   284→	if err != nil {
   285→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   289→
   290→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   291→	if err != nil {
   292→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   296→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   297→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   298→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   299→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   300→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   301→		NativeData:    newData,
   302→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   303→	}, nil
   304→}
   305→
   306→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   307→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   308→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   309→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   310→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   311→		return "", false
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return "", false
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   320→}
   321→
   322→// ReadSessionFromPath is a convenience method that reads a session directly from a file path.
   323→// This is useful when you have the path but not a HookInput.
   324→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSessionFromPath(transcriptPath, sessionID string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   325→	return c.ReadSession(&agent.HookInput{
   326→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   327→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,
   328→	})
   329→}
   330→
   331→// SanitizePathForClaude converts a path to Claude's project directory format.
   332→// Claude replaces any non-alphanumeric character with a dash.
   333→var nonAlphanumericRegex = regexp.MustCompile(`[^a-zA-Z0-9]`)
   334→
   335→func SanitizePathForClaude(path string) string {
   336→	return nonAlphanumericRegex.ReplaceAllString(path, "-")
   337→}
   338→
   339→// TranscriptAnalyzer interface implementation
   340→
   341→// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current line count of a Claude Code transcript.
   342→// Claude Code uses JSONL format, so position is the number of lines.
   343→// This is a lightweight operation that only counts lines without parsing JSON.
   344→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   345→// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   346→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error) {
   347→	if path == "" {
   348→		return 0, nil
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   352→	if err != nil {
   353→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   354→			return 0, nil
   355→		}
   356→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→	defer file.Close()
   359→
   360→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   361→	lineCount := 0
   362→
   363→	for {
   364→		_, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   365→		if err != nil {
   366→			if err == io.EOF {
   367→				break
   368→			}
   369→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   370→		}
   371→		lineCount++
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	return lineCount, nil
   375→}
   376→
   377→// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given line number.
   378→// For Claude Code (JSONL format), offset is the starting line number.
   379→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
   380→// Returns:
   381→//   - files: list of file paths modified by Claude (from Write/Edit tools)
   382→//   - currentPosition: total number of lines in the file
   383→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   384→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error) {
   385→	if path == "" {
   386→		return nil, 0, nil
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	file, openErr := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   390→	if openErr != nil {
   391→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", openErr)
   392→	}
   393→	defer file.Close()
   394→
   395→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   396→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   397→	lineNum := 0
   398→
   399→	for {
   400→		lineData, readErr := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   401→		if readErr != nil && readErr != io.EOF {
   402→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", readErr)
   403→		}
   404→
   405→		if len(lineData) > 0 {
   406→			lineNum++
   407→			if lineNum > startOffset {
   408→				var line TranscriptLine
   409→				if parseErr := json.Unmarshal(lineData, &line); parseErr == nil {
   410→					lines = append(lines, line)
   411→				}
   412→				// Skip malformed lines silently
   413→			}
   414→		}
   415→
   416→		if readErr == io.EOF {
   417→			break
   418→		}
   419→	}
   420→
   421→	return ExtractModifiedFiles(lines), lineNum, nil
   422→}
   423→
   424→// TranscriptChunker interface implementation
   425→
   426→// ChunkTranscript splits a JSONL transcript at line boundaries.
   427→// Claude Code uses JSONL format (one JSON object per line), so chunking
   428→// is done at newline boundaries to preserve message integrity.
   429→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error) {
   430→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkJSONL(content, maxSize)
   431→	if err != nil {
   432→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk JSONL transcript: %w", err)
   433→	}
   434→	return chunks, nil
   435→}
   436→
   437→// ReassembleTranscript concatenates JSONL chunks with newlines.
   438→//
   439→//nolint:unparam // error return is required by interface, kept for consistency
   440→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error) {
   441→	return agent.ReassembleJSONL(chunks), nil
   442→}
   443→
