    29→
    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   108→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   109→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   110→//
   111→// For mid-session commits (no Stop/SaveChanges called yet), the shadow branch may not exist.
   112→// In this case, data is extracted from the live transcript instead.
   113→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState, committedFiles map[string]struct{}) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   114→	// Get shadow branch (may not exist for mid-session commits)
   115→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   116→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   117→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   118→	hasShadowBranch := err == nil
   119→
   120→	var sessionData *ExtractedSessionData
   121→	if hasShadowBranch {
   122→		// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   123→		// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   124→		// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   125→		// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   126→		// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveChanges may have been skipped if the
   127→		// last turn had no code changes).
   128→		// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   129→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   130→		if err != nil {
   131→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   132→		}
   133→	} else {
   134→		// No shadow branch: mid-session commit before Stop/SaveChanges.
   135→		// Extract data directly from live transcript.
   136→		if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   137→			return nil, errors.New("shadow branch not found and no live transcript available")
   138→		}
   139→		sessionData, err = s.extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state)
   140→		if err != nil {
   141→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data from live transcript: %w", err)
   142→		}
   143→	}
   144→
   145→	// For 1:1 checkpoint model: filter files_touched to only include files actually
   146→	// committed in this specific commit. This ensures each checkpoint represents
   147→	// exactly the files in that commit, not all files mentioned in the transcript.
   148→	if len(committedFiles) > 0 {
   149→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   150→			// Filter to intersection of transcript-extracted files and committed files
   151→			filtered := make([]string, 0, len(sessionData.FilesTouched))
   152→			for _, f := range sessionData.FilesTouched {
   153→				if _, ok := committedFiles[f]; ok {
   154→					filtered = append(filtered, f)
   155→				}
   156→			}
   157→			sessionData.FilesTouched = filtered
   158→		}
   159→
   160→		// If extraction failed or returned empty, use committedFiles as fallback.
   161→		// This handles mid-session commits where transcript parsing may not find files
   162→		// but we know what was committed.
   163→		if len(sessionData.FilesTouched) == 0 {
   164→			sessionData.FilesTouched = make([]string, 0, len(committedFiles))
   165→			for f := range committedFiles {
   166→				sessionData.FilesTouched = append(sessionData.FilesTouched, f)
   167→			}
   168→		}
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	// Get checkpoint store
   172→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   173→	if err != nil {
   174→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	// Get author info
   178→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   179→	// Attribution calculation requires shadow branch reference; skip if mid-session commit
   180→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   181→	if hasShadowBranch {
   182→		attribution = calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   183→	}
   184→	// Get current branch name
   185→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   186→
   187→	// Generate summary if enabled
   188→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   189→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   190→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   191→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   192→
   193→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   194→		// For Claude Code (JSONL), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset.
   195→		// For Gemini (JSON), CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index.
   196→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   197→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   198→			scopedTranscript = geminicli.SliceFromMessage(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   199→		} else {
   200→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   201→		}
   202→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   203→			var err error
   204→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, nil)
   205→			if err != nil {
   206→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   207→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   208→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   209→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   210→			} else {
   211→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   212→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   213→			}
   214→		}
   215→	}
   216→
   217→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   218→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   219→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   220→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   221→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   222→		Branch:                      branchName,
   223→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   224→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   225→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   226→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   227→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   228→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   229→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   230→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   231→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   232→		TurnID:                      state.TurnID,
   233→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   234→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   235→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   236→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   237→		Summary:                     summary,
   238→		SessionTranscriptPath:       homeRelativePath(state.TranscriptPath),
   239→	}); err != nil {
   240→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	return &CondenseResult{
   244→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   245→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   246→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   247→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   248→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   249→	}, nil
   250→}
   251→
   252→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   253→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   254→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   255→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   256→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   257→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   258→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   259→	if headErr != nil {
   260→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   261→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   262→	} else {
   263→		headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   264→		if commitErr != nil {
   265→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   266→				slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   267→		} else {
   268→			headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   269→			if treeErr != nil {
   270→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   271→					slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   272→			} else {
   273→				// Get shadow branch tree (checkpoint tree - what the agent wrote)
   274→				shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   275→				if shadowErr != nil {
   276→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   277→						slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   278→						slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   279→				} else {
   280→					shadowTree, shadowTreeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   281→					if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   282→						logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   283→							slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   284→					} else {
   285→						// Get base tree (state before session started)
   286→						var baseTree *object.Tree
   287→						attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   288→						if attrBase == "" {
   289→							attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   290→						}
   291→						if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   292→							if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   293→								baseTree = tree
   294→							} else {
   295→								logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   296→									slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   297→							}
   298→						} else {
   299→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   300→								slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   301→								slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   302→						}
   303→
   304→						// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   305→						var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   306→						for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   307→							totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   308→							totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   309→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   310→								slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   311→								slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   312→								slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   313→								slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   314→								slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   315→								slog.Int("index", i))
   316→						}
   317→
   318→						attribution = CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   319→							baseTree,
   320→							shadowTree,
   321→							headTree,
   322→							sessionData.FilesTouched,
   323→							state.PromptAttributions,
   324→						)
   325→
   326→						if attribution != nil {
   327→							logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   328→								slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   329→								slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   330→								slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   331→								slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   332→								slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   333→								slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   334→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   335→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   336→								slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   337→						}
   338→					}
   339→				}
   340→			}
   341→		}
   342→	}
   343→	return attribution
   344→}
   345→
   346→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   347→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   348→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   349→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   350→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   351→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   352→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   353→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   354→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   355→	if err != nil {
   356→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   357→	}
   358→
   359→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   360→	if err != nil {
   361→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   362→	}
   363→
   364→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   365→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   366→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   367→
   368→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   369→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   370→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   371→	var fullTranscript string
   372→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   373→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   374→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   375→		}
   376→	}
   377→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   378→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   379→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   380→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   381→				fullTranscript = content
   382→			}
   383→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   384→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   385→				fullTranscript = content
   386→			}
   387→		}
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   391→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   392→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   393→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   394→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   395→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   396→	}
   397→
   398→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   399→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   400→
   401→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   402→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   403→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   404→	}
   405→
   406→	return data, nil
   407→}
   408→
   409→// extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript extracts session data directly from the live transcript file.
   410→// This is used for mid-session commits where no shadow branch exists yet.
   411→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionDataFromLiveTranscript(state *SessionState) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   412→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   413→
   414→	// Read the live transcript
   415→	if state.TranscriptPath == "" {
   416→		return nil, errors.New("no transcript path in session state")
   417→	}
   418→
   419→	liveData, err := os.ReadFile(state.TranscriptPath)
   420→	if err != nil {
   421→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read live transcript: %w", err)
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	if len(liveData) == 0 {
   425→		return nil, errors.New("live transcript is empty")
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	fullTranscript := string(liveData)
   429→	data.Transcript = liveData
   430→	data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   431→	data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(state.AgentType, fullTranscript)
   432→	data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   433→
   434→	// Extract files from transcript since state.FilesTouched may be empty for mid-session commits
   435→	// (no SaveChanges/Stop has been called yet to populate it)
   436→	if len(state.FilesTouched) > 0 {
   437→		data.FilesTouched = state.FilesTouched
   438→	} else {
   439→		// Use the shared helper which includes subagent transcripts
   440→		data.FilesTouched = s.extractModifiedFilesFromLiveTranscript(state, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   441→	}
   442→
   443→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   444→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   445→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(state.AgentType, data.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   446→	}
   447→
   448→	return data, nil
   449→}
   450→
   451→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   452→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   453→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   454→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   455→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   456→	if content == "" {
   457→		return 0
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   461→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   462→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   463→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   464→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   465→		}
   466→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   467→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   468→			return 0
   469→		}
   470→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   471→	}
   472→
   473→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   474→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   475→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   476→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   477→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   478→	}
   479→	return len(allLines)
   480→}
   481→
   482→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   483→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   484→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   485→	if content == "" {
   486→		return nil
   487→	}
   488→
   489→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   490→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   491→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   492→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   493→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   494→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   495→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   496→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   497→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   498→				}
   499→			}
   500→			return cleaned
   501→		}
   502→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   503→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   504→			return nil
   505→		}
   506→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   510→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   511→}
   512→
   513→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   514→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   515→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   516→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   517→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   518→	if len(data) == 0 {
   519→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   520→	}
   521→
   522→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   523→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   524→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   525→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   526→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   527→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   528→		}
   529→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   530→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   531→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   532→		}
   533→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   537→	lines, err := claudecode.ParseTranscript(data)
   538→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   539→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   540→	}
   541→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   542→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   543→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   544→	}
   545→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   546→}
   547→
   548→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   549→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   550→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   551→	var prompts []string
   552→	for _, line := range lines {
   553→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   554→		if line == "" {
   555→			continue
   556→		}
   557→
   558→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   559→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   560→			continue
   561→		}
   562→
   563→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   564→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   565→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   566→			continue
   567→		}
   568→
   569→		// Extract message content
   570→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   571→		if !ok {
   572→			continue
   573→		}
   574→
   575→		// Handle string content
   576→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   577→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   578→			if cleaned != "" {
   579→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   580→			}
   581→			continue
   582→		}
   583→
   584→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   585→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   586→			var texts []string
   587→			for _, item := range arr {
   588→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   589→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   590→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   591→							texts = append(texts, text)
   592→						}
   593→					}
   594→				}
   595→			}
   596→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   597→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   598→				if cleaned != "" {
   599→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   600→				}
   601→			}
   602→		}
   603→	}
   604→	return prompts
   605→}
   606→
   607→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   608→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   609→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   610→		return nil
   611→	}
   612→
   613→	var buf strings.Builder
   614→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   615→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   616→
   617→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   618→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   619→		displayPrompt := prompt
   620→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   621→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   622→		}
   623→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   624→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   625→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   626→	}
   627→
   628→	return []byte(buf.String())
   629→}
   630→
   631→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   632→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   633→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   634→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   635→
   636→	// Load session state
   637→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   638→	if err != nil {
   639→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   640→	}
   641→	if state == nil {
   642→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   643→	}
   644→
   645→	// Open repository
   646→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   647→	if err != nil {
   648→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   649→	}
   650→
   651→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   652→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   653→	if err != nil {
   654→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   655→	}
   656→
   657→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   658→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   659→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   660→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   661→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   662→
   663→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   664→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   665→		// Just clean up the state file.
   666→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   667→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   668→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   669→		)
   670→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   671→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   672→		}
   673→		return nil
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	// Condense the session
   677→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state, nil)
   678→	if err != nil {
   679→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   683→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   684→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   685→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   686→	)
   687→
   688→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   689→	state.StepCount = 0
   690→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   691→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   692→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   693→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   694→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   695→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   696→
   697→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   698→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   699→	}
   700→
   701→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   702→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   703→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   704→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   705→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   706→		)
   707→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   708→	}
   709→
   710→	return nil
   711→}
   712→
   713→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   714→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   715→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   716→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   717→	if err != nil {
   718→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	for _, state := range allStates {
   722→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   723→			continue
   724→		}
   725→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   726→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   727→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   728→			return nil
   729→		}
   730→	}
   731→
   732→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   733→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   734→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   735→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   736→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   737→			return nil
   738→		}
   739→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   740→	}
   741→	return nil
   742→}
   743→

    74→func TestParseTranscript_LineExceedsScannerBuffer(t *testing.T) {
    75→	// Create a line larger than the 10MB ScannerBufferSize limit.
    76→	// This simulates a transcript with a very large tool output (e.g., reading a huge file).
    77→	// The current implementation using bufio.Scanner will fail with "token too long".
    78→	largeContent := strings.Repeat("x", 11*1024*1024) // 11MB - exceeds 10MB limit
    79→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"` + largeContent + `"}}`
    80→
    81→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)
    82→
    83→	lines, err := parseTranscript(tmpFile)
    84→	if err != nil {
    85→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error parsing line exceeding buffer: %v", err)
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	if len(lines) != 1 {
    89→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 line, got %d", len(lines))
    90→	}
    91→}
    92→
    93→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_LineExceedsScannerBuffer(t *testing.T) {
    94→	// Same test for parseTranscriptFromLine - should handle lines > 10MB
    95→	largeContent := strings.Repeat("x", 11*1024*1024) // 11MB
    96→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"` + largeContent + `"}}`
    97→
    98→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)
    99→
   100→	lines, totalLines, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(tmpFile, 0)
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error parsing line exceeding buffer: %v", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	if totalLines != 1 {
   106→		t.Errorf("expected totalLines=1, got %d", totalLines)
   107→	}
   108→	if len(lines) != 1 {
   109→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 parsed line, got %d", len(lines))
   110→	}
   111→}

   176→func TestExtractModifiedFiles_AllToolTypes(t *testing.T) {
   177→	transcript := []transcriptLine{
   178→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/path/write.go"}}]}`)},
   179→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Edit","input":{"file_path":"/path/edit.go"}}]}`)},
   180→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"mcp__acp__Write","input":{"file_path":"/path/mcp_write.go"}}]}`)},
   181→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"mcp__acp__Edit","input":{"file_path":"/path/mcp_edit.go"}}]}`)},
   182→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"NotebookEdit","input":{"notebook_path":"/path/notebook.ipynb"}}]}`)},
   183→		{Type: "assistant", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/path/read.go"}}]}`)},
   184→	}
   185→
   186→	files := extractModifiedFiles(transcript)
   187→
   188→	expected := []string{"/path/write.go", "/path/edit.go", "/path/mcp_write.go", "/path/mcp_edit.go", "/path/notebook.ipynb"}
   189→	if len(files) != len(expected) {
   190→		t.Fatalf("expected %d files, got %d: %v", len(expected), len(files), files)
   191→	}
   192→
   193→	for i, exp := range expected {
   194→		if files[i] != exp {
   195→			t.Errorf("file %d: expected %s, got %s", i, exp, files[i])
   196→		}
   197→	}
   198→}

   239→func createTempTranscript(t *testing.T, content string) string {
   240→	t.Helper()
   241→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   242→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   243→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   244→		t.Fatalf("failed to create temp file: %v", err)
   245→	}
   246→	return tmpFile
   247→}

   249→func TestAgentTranscriptPath(t *testing.T) {

   253→		agentID       string
   254→		expected      string
   255→	}{

   259→			agentID:       "agent_abc123",

   261→		},

   264→			transcriptDir: "/path/to/transcripts",

   266→			expected:      "/path/to/transcripts/agent-.jsonl",

   268→	}
   269→
   270→	for _, tt := range tests {

   275→			}
   276→		})
   277→	}
   278→}

   280→func TestFindCheckpointUUID(t *testing.T) {
   281→	transcript := []transcriptLine{
   282→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u1", Message: []byte(`{"content":"First prompt"}`)},
   283→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a1", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_task1","name":"Task","input":{}}]}`)},
   284→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_task1","content":"Task completed"}]}`)},
   285→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done"}]}`)},
   286→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a3", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_task2","name":"Task","input":{}}]}`)},
   287→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u3", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_task2","content":"Second task done"}]}`)},
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	tests := []struct {
   291→		name      string
   292→		toolUseID string
   293→		wantUUID  string
   294→		wantFound bool
   295→	}{
   296→		{
   297→			name:      "find first task result",
   298→			toolUseID: "toolu_task1",
   299→			wantUUID:  "u2",
   300→			wantFound: true,
   301→		},
   302→		{
   303→			name:      "find second task result",
   304→			toolUseID: "toolu_task2",
   305→			wantUUID:  "u3",
   306→			wantFound: true,
   307→		},
   308→		{
   309→			name:      "non-existent tool use ID",
   310→			toolUseID: "toolu_nonexistent",
   311→			wantUUID:  "",
   312→			wantFound: false,
   313→		},
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	for _, tt := range tests {
   317→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   318→			gotUUID, gotFound := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, tt.toolUseID)
   319→			if gotFound != tt.wantFound {
   320→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID() found = %v, want %v", gotFound, tt.wantFound)
   321→			}
   322→			if gotUUID != tt.wantUUID {
   323→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID() uuid = %v, want %v", gotUUID, tt.wantUUID)
   324→			}
   325→		})
   326→	}
   327→}

   329→func TestTruncateTranscriptAtUUID(t *testing.T) {
   330→	transcript := []transcriptLine{
   331→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u1", Message: []byte(`{"content":"First"}`)},
   332→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a1", Message: []byte(`{"content":[]}`)},
   333→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u2", Message: []byte(`{"content":"Second"}`)},
   334→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[]}`)},
   335→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u3", Message: []byte(`{"content":"Third"}`)},
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	tests := []struct {
   339→		name         string
   340→		uuid         string
   341→		expectedLen  int
   342→		expectedLast string
   343→	}{
   344→		{
   345→			name:         "truncate at u2",
   346→			uuid:         "u2",
   347→			expectedLen:  3,
   348→			expectedLast: "u2",
   349→		},
   350→		{
   351→			name:         "truncate at first",
   352→			uuid:         "u1",
   353→			expectedLen:  1,
   354→			expectedLast: "u1",
   355→		},
   356→		{
   357→			name:         "truncate at last",
   358→			uuid:         "u3",
   359→			expectedLen:  5,
   360→			expectedLast: "u3",
   361→		},
   362→		{
   363→			name:         "uuid not found - return all",
   364→			uuid:         "nonexistent",
   365→			expectedLen:  5,
   366→			expectedLast: "u3",
   367→		},
   368→		{
   369→			name:         "empty uuid - return all",
   370→			uuid:         "",
   371→			expectedLen:  5,
   372→			expectedLast: "u3",
   373→		},
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	for _, tt := range tests {
   377→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   378→			result := TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(transcript, tt.uuid)
   379→			if len(result) != tt.expectedLen {
   380→				t.Errorf("TruncateTranscriptAtUUID() len = %d, want %d", len(result), tt.expectedLen)
   381→			}
   382→			if len(result) > 0 && result[len(result)-1].UUID != tt.expectedLast {
   383→				t.Errorf("TruncateTranscriptAtUUID() last UUID = %s, want %s", result[len(result)-1].UUID, tt.expectedLast)
   384→			}
   385→		})
   386→	}
   387→}

   415→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_EntireFile(t *testing.T) {

   417→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Hi"}]}}

   436→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_Offset(t *testing.T) {
   437→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"Line1"}}
   438→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Line2"}]}}
   439→{"type":"user","uuid":"user-2","message":{"content":"Line3"}}
   440→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-2","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Line4"}]}}`
   441→
   442→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)
   443→
   444→	// Parse from line 2 (0-indexed, so skip first 2 lines)
   445→	lines, totalLines, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(tmpFile, 2)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	if totalLines != 4 {
   451→		t.Errorf("totalLines = %d, want 4", totalLines)
   452→	}
   453→	if len(lines) != 2 {
   454→		t.Errorf("len(lines) = %d, want 2 (lines after offset)", len(lines))
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Verify we got the correct lines (user-2 and asst-2)
   458→	if len(lines) >= 1 && lines[0].UUID != "user-2" {
   459→		t.Errorf("first line UUID = %q, want 'user-2'", lines[0].UUID)
   460→	}
   461→	if len(lines) >= 2 && lines[1].UUID != "asst-2" {
   462→		t.Errorf("second line UUID = %q, want 'asst-2'", lines[1].UUID)
   463→	}
   464→}

   486→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_SkipsMalformedLines(t *testing.T) {
   487→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"Hello"}}
   488→invalid json line
   489→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Hi"}]}}
   490→{"type":"user","uuid":"user-2","message":{"content":"Bye"}}`
   491→
   492→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)
   493→

   495→	lines, totalLines, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(tmpFile, 1)
   496→	if err != nil {
   497→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   498→	}
   499→
   500→	// Total lines counts ALL lines including malformed (they're still lines in the file)
   501→	if totalLines != 4 {
   502→		t.Errorf("totalLines = %d, want 4", totalLines)
   503→	}
   504→	// But parsed lines excludes malformed
   505→	if len(lines) != 2 {
   506→		t.Errorf("len(lines) = %d, want 2 (valid lines after offset)", len(lines))
   507→	}
   508→}

   553→func TestGetTranscriptPosition_EmptyFile(t *testing.T) {
   554→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, "")
   555→
   556→	pos, err := GetTranscriptPosition(tmpFile)
   557→	if err != nil {
   558→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	if pos.LineCount != 0 {
   562→		t.Errorf("LineCount = %d, want 0", pos.LineCount)

     1→// Package transcript provides shared types for parsing Claude Code transcripts.
     2→// This package contains only data structures and constants, not parsing logic.
     3→package transcript
     4→
     5→import "encoding/json"
     6→
     7→// Message type constants for transcript lines.
     8→const (
     9→	TypeUser      = "user"
    10→	TypeAssistant = "assistant"
    11→)
    12→
    13→// Content type constants for content blocks within messages.
    14→const (
    15→	ContentTypeText    = "text"
    16→	ContentTypeToolUse = "tool_use"
    17→)
    18→
    19→// Line represents a single line in a Claude Code JSONL transcript.
    20→type Line struct {
    21→	Type    string          `json:"type"`
    22→	UUID    string          `json:"uuid"`
    23→	Message json.RawMessage `json:"message"`
    24→}
    25→
    26→// UserMessage represents a user message in the transcript.
    27→type UserMessage struct {
    28→	Content interface{} `json:"content"`
    29→}
    30→
    31→// AssistantMessage represents an assistant message in the transcript.
    32→type AssistantMessage struct {
    33→	Content []ContentBlock `json:"content"`
    34→}
    35→
    36→// ContentBlock represents a block within an assistant message.
    37→type ContentBlock struct {
    38→	Type  string          `json:"type"`
    39→	Text  string          `json:"text,omitempty"`
    40→	Name  string          `json:"name,omitempty"`
    41→	Input json.RawMessage `json:"input,omitempty"`
    42→}
    43→
    44→// ToolInput represents the input to various tools.
    45→// Used to extract file paths and descriptions from tool calls.
    46→type ToolInput struct {
    47→	FilePath     string `json:"file_path,omitempty"`
    48→	NotebookPath string `json:"notebook_path,omitempty"`
    49→	Description  string `json:"description,omitempty"`
    50→	Command      string `json:"command,omitempty"`
    51→	Pattern      string `json:"pattern,omitempty"`
    52→	// Skill tool fields
    53→	Skill string `json:"skill,omitempty"`
    54→	// WebFetch tool fields
    55→	URL    string `json:"url,omitempty"`
    56→	Prompt string `json:"prompt,omitempty"`
    57→}
    58→
```

     1→package transcript
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// ParseFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.
    15→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    16→func ParseFromBytes(content []byte) ([]Line, error) {
    17→	var lines []Line
    18→	reader := bufio.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(content))
    19→
    20→	for {
    21→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    22→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    23→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    24→		}
    25→
    26→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    27→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    28→			if err == io.EOF {
    29→				break
    30→			}
    31→			continue
    32→		}
    33→
    34→		var line Line
    35→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    36→			lines = append(lines, line)
    37→		}
    38→
    39→		if err == io.EOF {
    40→			break
    41→		}
    42→	}
    43→
    44→	return lines, nil
    45→}
    46→
    47→// SliceFromLine returns the content starting from line number `startLine` (0-indexed).
    48→// This is used to extract only the checkpoint-specific portion of a cumulative transcript.
    49→// For example, if startLine is 2, lines 0 and 1 are skipped and the result starts at line 2.
    50→// Returns empty slice if startLine exceeds the number of lines.
    51→func SliceFromLine(content []byte, startLine int) []byte {
    52→	if len(content) == 0 || startLine <= 0 {
    53→		return content
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	// Find the byte offset where startLine begins
    57→	lineCount := 0
    58→	offset := 0
    59→	for i, b := range content {
    60→		if b == '\n' {
    61→			lineCount++
    62→			if lineCount == startLine {
    63→				offset = i + 1
    64→				break
    65→			}
    66→		}
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// If we didn't find enough lines, return empty
    70→	if lineCount < startLine {
    71→		return nil
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	// If offset is beyond content, return empty
    75→	if offset >= len(content) {
    76→		return nil
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	return content[offset:]
    80→}
    81→
    82→// ExtractUserContent extracts user content from a raw message.
    83→// Handles both string and array content formats.
    84→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
    85→// Returns empty string if the message cannot be parsed or contains no text.
    86→func ExtractUserContent(message json.RawMessage) string {
    87→	var msg UserMessage
    88→	if err := json.Unmarshal(message, &msg); err != nil {
    89→		return ""
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	// Handle string content
    93→	if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
    94→		return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str)
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	// Handle array content (only if it contains text blocks)
    98→	if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
    99→		var texts []string
   100→		for _, item := range arr {
   101→			if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   102→				if m["type"] == ContentTypeText {
   103→					if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   104→						texts = append(texts, text)
   105→					}
   106→				}
   107→			}
   108→		}
   109→		if len(texts) > 0 {
   110→			return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   111→		}
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	return ""
   115→}
   116→

    22→	contentTypeToolUse      = transcript.ContentTypeToolUse

    25→// parseTranscript reads and parses a Claude Code transcript file.
    26→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    27→func parseTranscript(path string) ([]transcriptLine, error) {
    28→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path
    29→	if err != nil {
    30→		return nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase
    31→	}
    32→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    33→
    34→	var lines []transcriptLine
    35→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
    36→
    37→	for {
    38→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    39→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    40→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    41→		}
    42→
    43→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    44→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    45→			if err == io.EOF {
    46→				break
    47→			}
    48→			continue
    49→		}
    50→
    51→		var line transcriptLine
    52→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    53→			lines = append(lines, line)
    54→		}
    55→
    56→		if err == io.EOF {
    57→			break
    58→		}
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	return lines, nil
    62→}
    63→
    64→// parseTranscriptFromLine reads and parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
    65→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    66→// Returns:
    67→//   - lines: parsed transcript lines from startLine onwards (malformed lines skipped)
    68→//   - totalLines: total number of lines in the file (including malformed ones)
    69→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
    70→//
    71→// The startLine parameter is 0-indexed (startLine=0 reads from the beginning).
    72→// This is useful for incremental parsing when you've already processed some lines.
    73→func parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {
    74→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path is a controlled transcript file path
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
    77→	}
    78→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    79→
    80→	var lines []transcriptLine
    81→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
    82→
    83→	totalLines := 0
    84→	for {
    85→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    86→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    87→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    88→		}
    89→
    90→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    91→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    92→			if err == io.EOF {
    93→				break
    94→			}
    95→			continue
    96→		}
    97→
    98→		// Count all lines for totalLines, but only parse after startLine
    99→		if totalLines >= startLine {
   100→			var line transcriptLine
   101→			if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
   102→				lines = append(lines, line)
   103→			}
   104→		}
   105→		totalLines++
   106→
   107→		if err == io.EOF {
   108→			break
   109→		}
   110→	}
   111→
   112→	return lines, totalLines, nil
   113→}
   114→
   115→// parseTranscriptFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.
   116→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
   117→func parseTranscriptFromBytes(content []byte) ([]transcriptLine, error) {
   118→	lines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)
   119→	if err != nil {
   120→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   121→	}
   122→	return lines, nil
   123→}
   124→
   125→// PromptResponsePair represents a user prompt and the assistant's responses.
   126→// Used for extracting all interactions from a transcript.
   127→type PromptResponsePair struct {
   128→	Prompt    string
   129→	Responses []string // Multiple responses can occur between tool calls
   130→	Files     []string
   131→}
   132→
   133→// ExtractAllPromptResponses extracts all user prompt and assistant response pairs from a transcript.
   134→// Returns pairs in chronological order (oldest first).
   135→// Each pair contains the user's prompt, assistant's text response, and files modified.
   136→func ExtractAllPromptResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []PromptResponsePair {
   137→	var pairs []PromptResponsePair
   138→
   139→	// Find all user prompts (messages with string content, not tool results)
   140→	var userIndices []int
   141→	for i, line := range transcript {
   142→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeUser {
   143→			var msg userMessage
   144→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   145→				continue
   146→			}
   147→			// Only count messages with string content (real user prompts)
   148→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   149→				userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
   150→			} else if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   151→				// Check if array has text blocks (also a real user prompt)
   152→				for _, item := range arr {
   153→					if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   154→						if m["type"] == contentTypeText {
   155→							userIndices = append(userIndices, i)
   156→							break
   157→						}
   158→					}
   159→				}
   160→			}
   161→		}
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	// For each user prompt, extract the prompt text, following assistant response, and files
   165→	for idx, userIdx := range userIndices {
   166→		// Determine the range for this prompt's conversation
   167→		endIdx := len(transcript)
   168→		if idx < len(userIndices)-1 {
   169→			endIdx = userIndices[idx+1]
   170→		}
   171→
   172→		// Extract prompt text
   173→		prompt := extractUserPromptAt(transcript, userIdx)
   174→		if prompt == "" {
   175→			continue
   176→		}
   177→
   178→		// Extract assistant responses and files from this range
   179→		slice := transcript[userIdx:endIdx]
   180→		responses := extractAssistantResponses(slice)
   181→		files := extractModifiedFiles(slice)
   182→
   183→		pairs = append(pairs, PromptResponsePair{
   184→			Prompt:    prompt,
   185→			Responses: responses,
   186→			Files:     files,
   187→		})
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	return pairs
   191→}
   192→
   193→// extractUserPromptAt extracts the user prompt at the given index.
   194→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
   195→func extractUserPromptAt(lines []transcriptLine, idx int) string {
   196→	if idx >= len(lines) || lines[idx].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   197→		return ""
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	return transcript.ExtractUserContent(lines[idx].Message)
   201→}
   202→
   203→// extractAssistantResponses collects all assistant text blocks from the given transcript slice.
   204→// A single prompt can trigger multiple assistant responses interspersed with tool calls.
   205→func extractAssistantResponses(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   206→	var texts []string
   207→	for _, line := range transcript {
   208→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   209→			var msg assistantMessage
   210→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   211→				continue
   212→			}
   213→
   214→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   215→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   216→					texts = append(texts, block.Text)
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→	return texts
   222→}
   223→
   224→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user messages from the transcript in order.
   225→// Only considers messages with string content (not tool results).
   226→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   227→func extractUserPrompts(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   228→	var prompts []string
   229→	for i := range transcript {
   230→		if transcript[i].Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   231→			continue
   232→		}
   233→		var msg userMessage
   234→		if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   235→			continue
   236→		}
   237→
   238→		// Handle string content
   239→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   240→			prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str))
   241→			continue
   242→		}
   243→
   244→		// Handle array content (only if it contains text blocks)
   245→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   246→			var texts []string
   247→			for _, item := range arr {
   248→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   249→					if m["type"] == contentTypeText {
   250→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   251→							texts = append(texts, text)
   252→						}
   253→					}
   254→				}
   255→			}
   256→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   257→				prompts = append(prompts, textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")))
   258→			}
   259→		}
   260→	}
   261→	return prompts
   262→}
   263→
   264→// extractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from the transcript.
   265→// Only considers messages with string content (not tool results).
   266→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
   267→func extractLastUserPrompt(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   268→	prompts := extractUserPrompts(transcript)
   269→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   270→		return ""
   271→	}
   272→	return prompts[len(prompts)-1]
   273→}
   274→
   275→// extractLastAssistantMessage extracts the last text message from the assistant
   276→func extractLastAssistantMessage(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   277→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   278→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   279→			var msg assistantMessage
   280→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   281→				continue
   282→			}
   283→
   284→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   285→				if block.Type == contentTypeText && block.Text != "" {
   286→					return block.Text
   287→				}
   288→			}
   289→		}
   290→	}
   291→	return ""
   292→}
   293→
   294→// findLastUserUUID finds the UUID of the last user message (that has string content)
   295→func findLastUserUUID(transcript []transcriptLine) string {
   296→	for i := len(transcript) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   297→		if transcript[i].Type == transcriptTypeUser {
   298→			var msg userMessage
   299→			if err := json.Unmarshal(transcript[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   300→				continue
   301→			}
   302→			// Only count messages with string content (not tool results)
   303→			if _, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   304→				return transcript[i].UUID
   305→			}
   306→		}
   307→	}
   308→	return ""
   309→}
   310→
   311→// filterTranscriptAfterUUID returns transcript lines after the given UUID
   312→func filterTranscriptAfterUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, uuid string) []transcriptLine {
   313→	if uuid == "" {
   314→		return transcript
   315→	}
   316→
   317→	foundIndex := -1
   318→	for i, line := range transcript {
   319→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   320→			foundIndex = i
   321→			break
   322→		}
   323→	}
   324→
   325→	if foundIndex == -1 || foundIndex == len(transcript)-1 {
   326→		return transcript
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	return transcript[foundIndex+1:]
   330→}
   331→
   332→// extractModifiedFiles extracts the list of files modified by tool calls
   333→func extractModifiedFiles(transcript []transcriptLine) []string {
   334→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   335→	var files []string
   336→
   337→	for _, line := range transcript {
   338→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   339→			var msg assistantMessage
   340→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   341→				continue
   342→			}
   343→
   344→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   345→				if block.Type == contentTypeToolUse {
   346→					isModifyTool := false
   347→					for _, name := range claudecode.FileModificationTools {
   348→						if block.Name == name {
   349→							isModifyTool = true
   350→							break
   351→						}
   352→					}
   353→
   354→					if isModifyTool {
   355→						var input toolInput
   356→						if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
   357→							continue
   358→						}
   359→
   360→						file := input.FilePath
   361→						if file == "" {
   362→							file = input.NotebookPath
   363→						}
   364→
   365→						if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   366→							fileSet[file] = true
   367→							files = append(files, file)
   368→						}
   369→					}
   370→				}
   371→			}
   372→		}
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	return files
   376→}
   377→
   378→// extractKeyActions extracts key actions from the transcript for context file
   379→func extractKeyActions(transcript []transcriptLine, maxActions int) []string {
   380→	var keyActions []string
   381→
   382→	for _, line := range transcript {
   383→		if line.Type == transcriptTypeAssistant {
   384→			var msg assistantMessage
   385→			if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   386→				continue
   387→			}
   388→
   389→			for _, block := range msg.Content {
   390→				if block.Type == contentTypeToolUse {
   391→					var input toolInput
   392→					_ = json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort parsing for display purposes
   393→
   394→					detail := input.Description
   395→					if detail == "" {
   396→						detail = input.Command
   397→					}
   398→					if detail == "" {
   399→						detail = input.FilePath
   400→					}
   401→					if detail == "" {
   402→						detail = input.Pattern
   403→					}
   404→
   405→					action := "- **" + block.Name + "**: " + detail
   406→					keyActions = append(keyActions, action)
   407→					if len(keyActions) >= maxActions {
   408→						return keyActions
   409→					}
   410→				}
   411→			}
   412→		}
   413→	}
   414→
   415→	return keyActions
   416→}
   417→
   418→// AgentTranscriptPath returns the path to a subagent's transcript file.
   419→// Subagent transcripts are stored as agent-{agentId}.jsonl in the same directory
   420→// as the main transcript.
   421→func AgentTranscriptPath(transcriptDir, agentID string) string {
   422→	return filepath.Join(transcriptDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   423→}
   424→
   425→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   426→type toolResultBlock struct {
   427→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   428→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   429→}
   430→
   431→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   432→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   433→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   434→}
   435→
   436→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   437→// for the given tool_use_id. This is used to find the checkpoint point for
   438→// transcript truncation when rewinding to a task.
   439→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   440→func FindCheckpointUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   441→	for _, line := range transcript {
   442→		if line.Type != transcriptTypeUser {
   443→			continue
   444→		}
   445→
   446→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   447→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   448→			continue
   449→		}
   450→
   451→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   452→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   453→				return line.UUID, true
   454→			}
   455→		}
   456→	}
   457→	return "", false
   458→}
   459→
   460→// TruncateTranscriptAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the
   461→// line with the given UUID. If the UUID is not found or is empty, returns
   462→// the entire transcript.
   463→//
   464→//nolint:revive // Exported for testing purposes
   465→func TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(transcript []transcriptLine, uuid string) []transcriptLine {
   466→	if uuid == "" {
   467→		return transcript
   468→	}
   469→
   470→	for i, line := range transcript {
   471→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   472→			return transcript[:i+1]
   473→		}
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   477→	return transcript
   478→}
   479→
   480→// writeTranscript writes transcript lines to a file in JSONL format.
   481→func writeTranscript(path string, transcript []transcriptLine) error {
   482→	file, err := os.Create(path) //nolint:gosec // Writing to controlled git metadata path
   483→	if err != nil {
   484→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create file: %w", err)
   485→	}
   486→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   487→
   488→	for _, line := range transcript {
   489→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
   490→		if err != nil {
   491→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
   492→		}
   493→		if _, err := file.Write(data); err != nil {
   494→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write line: %w", err)
   495→		}
   496→		if _, err := file.WriteString("\n"); err != nil {
   497→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write newline: %w", err)
   498→		}
   499→	}
   500→
   501→	return nil
   502→}
   503→
   504→// TranscriptPosition contains the position information for a transcript file.
   505→type TranscriptPosition struct {
   506→	LastUUID  string // Last non-empty UUID (from user/assistant messages)
   507→	LineCount int    // Total number of lines
   508→}
   509→
   510→// GetTranscriptPosition reads a transcript file and returns the last UUID and line count.
   511→// Returns empty position if file doesn't exist or is empty.
   512→// Only considers UUIDs from actual messages (user/assistant), not summary rows which use leafUuid.
   513→func GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (TranscriptPosition, error) {
   514→	if path == "" {
   515→		return TranscriptPosition{}, nil
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled transcript path
   519→	if err != nil {
   520→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   521→			return TranscriptPosition{}, nil
   522→		}
   523→		return TranscriptPosition{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
   524→	}
   525→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
   526→
   527→	var pos TranscriptPosition
   528→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
   529→
   530→	for {
   531→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
   532→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
   533→			return TranscriptPosition{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   534→		}
   535→
   536→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
   537→			if err == io.EOF {
   538→				break
   539→			}
   540→			continue
   541→		}
   542→
   543→		pos.LineCount++
   544→
   545→		// Parse line to extract UUID (only from user/assistant messages, not summaries)
   546→		var line transcriptLine
   547→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
   548→			if line.UUID != "" {
   549→				pos.LastUUID = line.UUID
   550→			}
   551→		}
   552→
   553→		if err == io.EOF {
   554→			break
   555→		}
   556→	}
   557→
   558→	return pos, nil
   559→}
   560→
```

  "old_string": "// parseTranscript reads and parses a Claude Code transcript file.\n// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.\nfunc parseTranscript(path string) ([]transcriptLine, error) {\n\tfile, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Reading from controlled git metadata path\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, err //nolint:wrapcheck // already present in codebase\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = file.Close() }()\n\n\tvar lines []transcriptLine\n\treader := bufio.NewReader(file)\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tlineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif err != nil && err != io.EOF {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Handle empty line or EOF without content\n\t\tif len(lineBytes) == 0 {\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tvar line transcriptLine\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {\n\t\t\tlines = append(lines, line)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn lines, nil\n}\n\n// parseTranscriptFromLine reads and parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.\n// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.\n// Returns:\n//   - lines: parsed transcript lines from startLine onwards (malformed lines skipped)\n//   - totalLines: total number of lines in the file (including malformed ones)\n//   - error: any error encountered during reading\n//\n// The startLine parameter is 0-indexed (startLine=0 reads from the beginning).\n// This is useful for incremental parsing when you've already processed some lines.\nfunc parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]transcriptLine, int, error) {\n\tfile, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path is a controlled transcript file path\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to open transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tdefer func() { _ = file.Close() }()\n\n\tvar lines []transcriptLine\n\treader := bufio.NewReader(file)\n\n\ttotalLines := 0\n\tfor {\n\t\tlineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\\n')\n\t\tif err != nil && err != io.EOF {\n\t\t\treturn nil, 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read transcript: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Handle empty line or EOF without content\n\t\tif len(lineBytes) == 0 {\n\t\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Count all lines for totalLines, but only parse after startLine\n\t\tif totalLines >= startLine {\n\t\t\tvar line transcriptLine\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\tlines = append(lines, line)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\ttotalLines++\n\n\t\tif err == io.EOF {\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn lines, totalLines, nil\n}\n\n// parseTranscriptFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.\n// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.\nfunc parseTranscriptFromBytes(content []byte) ([]transcriptLine, error) {\n\tlines, err := transcript.ParseFromBytes(content)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse transcript: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn lines, nil\n}\n\n// PromptResponsePair",

}
