     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"os"
     9→	"path/filepath"
    10→	"strings"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// TranscriptLine is an alias to the shared transcript.Line type.
    17→type TranscriptLine = transcript.Line
    18→
    19→// Type aliases for internal use.
    20→type (
    21→	userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
    22→	assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
    23→	toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
    24→)
    25→
    26→// Scanner buffer size for large transcript files (10MB)
    27→const scannerBufferSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024
    28→
    29→// ParseTranscript parses raw JSONL content into transcript lines
    30→func ParseTranscript(data []byte) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
    31→	var lines []TranscriptLine
    32→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(bytes.NewReader(data))
    33→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
    34→
    35→	for scanner.Scan() {
    36→		var line TranscriptLine
    37→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
    38→			continue // Skip malformed lines
    39→		}
    40→		lines = append(lines, line)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
    44→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
    45→	}
    46→	return lines, nil
    47→}
    48→
    49→// SerializeTranscript converts transcript lines back to JSONL bytes
    50→func SerializeTranscript(lines []TranscriptLine) ([]byte, error) {
    51→	var buf bytes.Buffer
    52→	for _, line := range lines {
    53→		data, err := json.Marshal(line)
    54→		if err != nil {
    55→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal line: %w", err)
    56→		}
    57→		buf.Write(data)
    58→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
    59→	}
    60→	return buf.Bytes(), nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// ExtractModifiedFiles extracts files modified by tool calls from transcript
    64→func ExtractModifiedFiles(lines []TranscriptLine) []string {
    65→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
    66→	var files []string
    67→
    68→	for _, line := range lines {
    69→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
    70→			continue
    71→		}
    72→
    73→		var msg assistantMessage
    74→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
    75→			continue
    76→		}
    77→
    78→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
    79→			if block.Type != "tool_use" {
    80→				continue
    81→			}
    82→
    83→			// Check if it's a file modification tool
    84→			isModifyTool := false
    85→			for _, name := range FileModificationTools {
    86→				if block.Name == name {
    87→					isModifyTool = true
    88→					break
    89→				}
    90→			}
    91→
    92→			if !isModifyTool {
    93→				continue
    94→			}
    95→
    96→			var input toolInput
    97→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Input, &input); err != nil {
    98→				continue
    99→			}
   100→
   101→			file := input.FilePath
   102→			if file == "" {
   103→				file = input.NotebookPath
   104→			}
   105→
   106→			if file != "" && !fileSet[file] {
   107→				fileSet[file] = true
   108→				files = append(files, file)
   109→			}
   110→		}
   111→	}
   112→
   113→	return files
   114→}
   115→
   116→// ExtractLastUserPrompt extracts the last user message from transcript
   117→func ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines []TranscriptLine) string {
   118→	for i := len(lines) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
   119→		if lines[i].Type != "user" { //nolint:goconst // already present in codebase
   120→			continue
   121→		}
   122→
   123→		var msg userMessage
   124→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lines[i].Message, &msg); err != nil {
   125→			continue
   126→		}
   127→
   128→		// Handle string content
   129→		if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   130→			return str
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		// Handle array content (text blocks)
   134→		if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   135→			var texts []string
   136→			for _, item := range arr {
   137→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   138→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   139→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   140→							texts = append(texts, text)
   141→						}
   142→					}
   143→				}
   144→			}
   145→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   146→				return strings.Join(texts, "\n\n")
   147→			}
   148→		}
   149→	}
   150→	return ""
   151→}
   152→
   153→// TruncateAtUUID returns transcript lines up to and including the line with given UUID
   154→func TruncateAtUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, uuid string) []TranscriptLine {
   155→	if uuid == "" {
   156→		return lines
   157→	}
   158→
   159→	for i, line := range lines {
   160→		if line.UUID == uuid {
   161→			return lines[:i+1]
   162→		}
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// UUID not found, return full transcript
   166→	return lines
   167→}
   168→
   169→// toolResultBlock represents a tool_result in a user message
   170→type toolResultBlock struct {
   171→	Type      string `json:"type"`
   172→	ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   173→}
   174→
   175→// userMessageWithToolResults represents a user message that may contain tool results
   176→type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
   177→	Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
   178→}
   179→
   180→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   181→// for the given tool_use_id
   182→func FindCheckpointUUID(lines []TranscriptLine, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   183→	for _, line := range lines {
   184→		if line.Type != "user" {
   185→			continue
   186→		}
   187→
   188→		var msg userMessageWithToolResults
   189→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   190→			continue
   191→		}
   192→
   193→		for _, block := range msg.Content {
   194→			if block.Type == "tool_result" && block.ToolUseID == toolUseID {
   195→				return line.UUID, true
   196→			}
   197→		}
   198→	}
   199→	return "", false
   200→}
   201→
   202→// CalculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript.
   203→// This is specific to Claude/Anthropic's API format where each assistant message
   204→// contains a usage object with input_tokens, output_tokens, and cache tokens.
   205→//
   206→// Due to streaming, multiple transcript rows may share the same message.id.
   207→// We deduplicate by taking the row with the highest output_tokens for each message.id.
   208→func CalculateTokenUsage(transcript []TranscriptLine) *agent.TokenUsage {
   209→	// Map from message.id to the usage with highest output_tokens
   210→	usageByMessageID := make(map[string]messageUsage)
   211→
   212→	for _, line := range transcript {
   213→		if line.Type != "assistant" {
   214→			continue
   215→		}
   216→
   217→		var msg messageWithUsage
   218→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   219→			continue
   220→		}
   221→
   222→		if msg.ID == "" {
   223→			continue
   224→		}
   225→
   226→		// Keep the entry with highest output_tokens (final streaming state)
   227→		existing, exists := usageByMessageID[msg.ID]
   228→		if !exists || msg.Usage.OutputTokens > existing.OutputTokens {
   229→			usageByMessageID[msg.ID] = msg.Usage
   230→		}
   231→	}
   232→
   233→	// Sum up all unique messages
   234→	usage := &agent.TokenUsage{
   235→		APICallCount: len(usageByMessageID),
   236→	}
   237→	for _, u := range usageByMessageID {
   238→		usage.InputTokens += u.InputTokens
   239→		usage.CacheCreationTokens += u.CacheCreationInputTokens
   240→		usage.CacheReadTokens += u.CacheReadInputTokens
   241→		usage.OutputTokens += u.OutputTokens
   242→	}
   243→
   244→	return usage
   245→}
   246→
   247→// CalculateTokenUsageFromFile calculates token usage from a Claude Code transcript file.
   248→// If startLine > 0, only considers lines from startLine onwards.
   249→func CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(path string, startLine int) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   250→	if path == "" {
   251→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(path, startLine)
   255→	if err != nil {
   256→		return nil, err
   257→	}
   258→
   259→	return CalculateTokenUsage(transcript), nil
   260→}
   261→
   262→// parseTranscriptFromLine parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
   263→func parseTranscriptFromLine(path string, startLine int) ([]TranscriptLine, error) {
   264→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // Path comes from Claude Code transcript location
   265→	if err != nil {
   266→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript file: %w", err)
   267→	}
   268→	defer file.Close()
   269→
   270→	var lines []TranscriptLine
   271→	scanner := bufio.NewScanner(file)
   272→	scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 0, scannerBufferSize), scannerBufferSize)
   273→
   274→	lineNum := 0
   275→	for scanner.Scan() {
   276→		if lineNum < startLine {
   277→			lineNum++
   278→			continue
   279→		}
   280→		lineNum++
   281→
   282→		var line TranscriptLine
   283→		if err := json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &line); err != nil {
   284→			continue // Skip malformed lines
   285→		}
   286→		lines = append(lines, line)
   287→	}
   288→
   289→	if err := scanner.Err(); err != nil {
   290→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan transcript: %w", err)
   291→	}
   292→
   293→	return lines, nil
   294→}
   295→
   296→// ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs extracts agent IDs from Task tool results in a transcript.
   297→// When a Task tool completes, the tool_result contains "agentId: <id>" in its content.
   298→// Returns a map of agentID -> toolUseID for all spawned agents.
   299→func ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript []TranscriptLine) map[string]string {
   300→	agentIDs := make(map[string]string)
   301→
   302→	for _, line := range transcript {
   303→		if line.Type != "user" {
   304→			continue
   305→		}
   306→
   307→		// Parse as array of content blocks (tool results)
   308→		var contentBlocks []struct {
   309→			Type      string          `json:"type"`
   310→			ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   311→			Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   312→		}
   313→
   314→		var msg struct {
   315→			Content json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
   316→		}
   317→		if err := json.Unmarshal(line.Message, &msg); err != nil {
   318→			continue
   319→		}
   320→
   321→		if err := json.Unmarshal(msg.Content, &contentBlocks); err != nil {
   322→			continue
   323→		}
   324→
   325→		for _, block := range contentBlocks {
   326→			if block.Type != "tool_result" {
   327→				continue
   328→			}
   329→
   330→			// Content can be a string or array of text blocks
   331→			var textContent string
   332→
   333→			// Try as array of text blocks first
   334→			var textBlocks []struct {
   335→				Type string `json:"type"`
   336→				Text string `json:"text"`
   337→			}
   338→			if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &textBlocks); err == nil {
   339→				var textContentSb361 strings.Builder
   340→				for _, tb := range textBlocks {
   341→					if tb.Type == "text" {
   342→						textContentSb361.WriteString(tb.Text + "\n")
   343→					}
   344→				}
   345→				textContent += textContentSb361.String()
   346→			} else {
   347→				// Try as plain string
   348→				var str string
   349→				if err := json.Unmarshal(block.Content, &str); err == nil {
   350→					textContent = str
   351→				}
   352→			}
   353→
   354→			// Look for agentId in the text
   355→			if agentID := extractAgentIDFromText(textContent); agentID != "" {
   356→				agentIDs[agentID] = block.ToolUseID
   357→			}
   358→		}
   359→	}
   360→
   361→	return agentIDs
   362→}
   363→
   364→// extractAgentIDFromText extracts an agent ID from text containing "agentId: <id>".
   365→func extractAgentIDFromText(text string) string {
   366→	const prefix = "agentId: "
   367→	idx := strings.Index(text, prefix)
   368→	if idx == -1 {
   369→		return ""
   370→	}
   371→
   372→	// Extract the ID (alphanumeric characters after the prefix)
   373→	start := idx + len(prefix)
   374→	end := start
   375→	for end < len(text) && (text[end] >= 'a' && text[end] <= 'z' ||
   376→		text[end] >= 'A' && text[end] <= 'Z' ||
   377→		text[end] >= '0' && text[end] <= '9') {
   378→		end++
   379→	}
   380→
   381→	if end > start {
   382→		return text[start:end]
   383→	}
   384→	return ""
   385→}
   386→
   387→// CalculateTotalTokenUsage calculates token usage for a turn, including subagents.
   388→// It parses the main transcript from startLine, extracts spawned agent IDs,
   389→// and calculates their token usage from transcripts in subagentsDir.
   390→func CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) (*agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   391→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   392→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}, nil
   393→	}
   394→
   395→	// Parse transcript ONCE
   396→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	// Calculate token usage from parsed transcript
   402→	mainUsage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   403→
   404→	// Extract spawned agent IDs from the same parsed transcript
   405→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
   406→
   407→	// Calculate subagent token usage
   408→	if len(agentIDs) > 0 {
   409→		subagentUsage := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   410→		for agentID := range agentIDs {
   411→			agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   412→			agentUsage, err := CalculateTokenUsageFromFile(agentPath, 0)
   413→			if err != nil {
   414→				// Agent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   415→				continue
   416→			}
   417→			subagentUsage.InputTokens += agentUsage.InputTokens
   418→			subagentUsage.CacheCreationTokens += agentUsage.CacheCreationTokens
   419→			subagentUsage.CacheReadTokens += agentUsage.CacheReadTokens
   420→			subagentUsage.OutputTokens += agentUsage.OutputTokens
   421→			subagentUsage.APICallCount += agentUsage.APICallCount
   422→		}
   423→		if subagentUsage.APICallCount > 0 {
   424→			mainUsage.SubagentTokens = subagentUsage
   425→		}
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	return mainUsage, nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// ExtractAllModifiedFiles extracts files modified by both the main agent and
   432→// any subagents spawned via the Task tool. It parses the main transcript from
   433→// startLine, collects modified files from the main agent, then reads each
   434→// subagent's transcript from subagentsDir to collect their modified files too.
   435→// The result is a deduplicated list of all modified file paths.
   436→func ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath string, startLine int, subagentsDir string) ([]string, error) {
   437→	if transcriptPath == "" {
   438→		return nil, nil
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Parse main transcript once
   442→	transcript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(transcriptPath, startLine)
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→
   447→	// Collect modified files from main agent
   448→	fileSet := make(map[string]bool)
   449→	var files []string
   450→	for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(transcript) {
   451→		if !fileSet[f] {
   452→			fileSet[f] = true
   453→			files = append(files, f)
   454→		}
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Find spawned subagents and collect their modified files
   458→	agentIDs := ExtractSpawnedAgentIDs(transcript)
   459→	for agentID := range agentIDs {
   460→		agentPath := filepath.Join(subagentsDir, fmt.Sprintf("agent-%s.jsonl", agentID))
   461→		agentTranscript, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(agentPath, 0)
   462→		if err != nil {
   463→			// Subagent transcript may not exist yet or may have been cleaned up
   464→			continue
   465→		}
   466→		for _, f := range ExtractModifiedFiles(agentTranscript) {
   467→			if !fileSet[f] {
   468→				fileSet[f] = true
   469→				files = append(files, f)
   470→			}
   471→		}
   472→	}
   473→
   474→	return files, nil
   475→}
   476→
```

   178→// ModifiedFiles is computed by parsing the transcript.
   179→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) ReadSession(input *agent.HookInput) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   180→	if input.SessionRef == "" {
   181→		return nil, errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   182→	}
   183→
   184→	// Read the raw JSONL file
   185→	data, err := os.ReadFile(input.SessionRef)
   186→	if err != nil {
   187→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   188→	}
   189→
   190→	// Parse to extract computed fields
   191→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   197→		SessionID:     input.SessionID,
   198→		AgentName:     c.Name(),
   199→		SessionRef:    input.SessionRef,
   200→		StartTime:     time.Now(),
   201→		NativeData:    data,
   202→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(lines),
   203→	}, nil
   204→}
   205→
   206→// WriteSession writes a session to Claude's storage (JSONL transcript file).
   207→// Uses the NativeData field which contains raw JSONL bytes.
   208→// The session must have been created by Claude Code (AgentName check).
   209→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) WriteSession(session *agent.AgentSession) error {
   210→	if session == nil {
   211→		return errors.New("session is nil")
   212→	}
   213→
   214→	// Verify this session belongs to Claude Code
   215→	if session.AgentName != "" && session.AgentName != c.Name() {
   216→		return fmt.Errorf("session belongs to agent %q, not %q", session.AgentName, c.Name())
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	if session.SessionRef == "" {
   220→		return errors.New("session reference (transcript path) is required")
   221→	}
   222→
   223→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   224→		return errors.New("session has no native data to write")
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	// Write the raw JSONL data
   228→	if err := os.WriteFile(session.SessionRef, session.NativeData, 0o600); err != nil {
   229→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", err)
   230→	}
   231→
   232→	return nil
   233→}
   234→
   235→// FormatResumeCommand returns the command to resume a Claude Code session.
   236→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string {
   237→	return "claude -r " + sessionID
   238→}
   239→
   240→// Session helper methods - work on AgentSession with Claude's native JSONL data
   241→
   242→// GetLastUserPrompt extracts the last user prompt from the session.
   243→// Requires NativeData to be populated (call ReadSession first).
   244→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetLastUserPrompt(session *agent.AgentSession) string {
   245→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   246→		return ""
   247→	}
   248→
   249→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   250→	if err != nil {
   251→		return ""
   252→	}
   253→
   254→	return ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   255→}
   256→
   257→// TruncateAtUUID returns a new session truncated at the given UUID (inclusive).
   258→// This is used for rewind operations to restore transcript state.
   259→// Requires NativeData to be populated.
   260→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) TruncateAtUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, uuid string) (*agent.AgentSession, error) {
   261→	if session == nil {
   262→		return nil, errors.New("session is nil")
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	if len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   266→		return nil, errors.New("session has no native data")
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	if uuid == "" {
   270→		// No truncation needed, return copy
   271→		return &agent.AgentSession{
   272→			SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   273→			AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   274→			RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   275→			SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   276→			StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   277→			NativeData:    session.NativeData,
   278→			ModifiedFiles: session.ModifiedFiles,
   279→		}, nil
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	// Parse, truncate, re-serialize
   283→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   284→	if err != nil {
   285→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse transcript: %w", err)
   286→	}
   287→
   288→	truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, uuid)
   289→
   290→	newData, err := SerializeTranscript(truncated)
   291→	if err != nil {
   292→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to serialize truncated transcript: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→
   295→	return &agent.AgentSession{
   296→		SessionID:     session.SessionID,
   297→		AgentName:     session.AgentName,
   298→		RepoPath:      session.RepoPath,
   299→		SessionRef:    session.SessionRef,
   300→		StartTime:     session.StartTime,
   301→		NativeData:    newData,
   302→		ModifiedFiles: ExtractModifiedFiles(truncated),
   303→	}, nil
   304→}
   305→
   306→// FindCheckpointUUID finds the UUID of the message containing the tool_result
   307→// for the given tool use ID. Used for task checkpoint rewind.
   308→// Returns the UUID and true if found, empty string and false otherwise.
   309→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) FindCheckpointUUID(session *agent.AgentSession, toolUseID string) (string, bool) {
   310→	if session == nil || len(session.NativeData) == 0 {
   311→		return "", false
   312→	}
   313→
   314→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(session.NativeData)
   315→	if err != nil {
   316→		return "", false
   317→	}
   318→
   319→	return FindCheckpointUUID(lines, toolUseID)
   320→}

   326→		SessionID:  sessionID,
   327→		SessionRef: transcriptPath,

   117→func TestExtractLastUserPrompt(t *testing.T) {
   118→	t.Parallel()
   119→
   120→	tests := []struct {
   121→		name string
   122→		data string
   123→		want string
   124→	}{
   125→		{
   126→			name: "string content",
   127→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first"}}
   128→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[]}}
   129→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second"}}`,
   130→			want: "second",
   131→		},
   132→		{
   133→			name: "array content with text block",
   134→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"hello world"}]}}`,
   135→			want: "hello world",
   136→		},
   137→		{
   138→			name: "empty transcript",
   139→			data: ``,
   140→			want: "",
   141→		},
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	for _, tt := range tests {
   145→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   146→			t.Parallel()
   147→			lines, err := ParseTranscript([]byte(tt.data))
   148→			if err != nil && tt.data != "" {
   149→				t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
   150→			}
   151→			got := ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   152→			if got != tt.want {
   153→				t.Errorf("ExtractLastUserPrompt() = %q, want %q", got, tt.want)
   154→			}
   155→		})
   156→	}
   157→}
   158→
   159→func TestTruncateAtUUID(t *testing.T) {
   160→	t.Parallel()
   161→
   162→	data := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{}}
   163→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{}}
   164→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{}}
   165→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{}}
   166→`)
   167→
   168→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	tests := []struct {
   174→		name     string
   175→		uuid     string
   176→		wantLen  int
   177→		lastUUID string
   178→	}{
   179→		{"truncate at u1", "u1", 1, "u1"},
   180→		{"truncate at a1", "a1", 2, "a1"},
   181→		{"truncate at u2", "u2", 3, "u2"},
   182→		{"truncate at a2", "a2", 4, "a2"},
   183→		{"empty uuid returns all", "", 4, "a2"},
   184→		{"unknown uuid returns all", "unknown", 4, "a2"},
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	for _, tt := range tests {
   188→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   189→			t.Parallel()
   190→			truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, tt.uuid)
   191→			if len(truncated) != tt.wantLen {
   192→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) got %d lines, want %d", tt.uuid, len(truncated), tt.wantLen)
   193→			}
   194→			if len(truncated) > 0 && truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID != tt.lastUUID {
   195→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) last UUID = %q, want %q", tt.uuid, truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID, tt.lastUUID)
   196→			}
   197→		})
   198→	}
   199→}

   201→func TestFindCheckpointUUID(t *testing.T) {
   202→	t.Parallel()
   203→
   204→	data := []byte(`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool1"}]}}
   205→{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"tool1"}]}}
   206→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool2"}]}}
   207→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"tool2"}]}}
   208→`)
   209→
   210→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	tests := []struct {
   216→		toolUseID string
   217→		wantUUID  string
   218→		wantFound bool
   219→	}{
   220→		{"tool1", "u1", true},
   221→		{"tool2", "u2", true},
   222→		{"unknown", "", false},
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	for _, tt := range tests {
   226→		t.Run(tt.toolUseID, func(t *testing.T) {
   227→			t.Parallel()
   228→			uuid, found := FindCheckpointUUID(lines, tt.toolUseID)
   229→			if found != tt.wantFound {
   230→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID(%q) found = %v, want %v", tt.toolUseID, found, tt.wantFound)
   231→			}
   232→			if uuid != tt.wantUUID {
   233→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID(%q) uuid = %q, want %q", tt.toolUseID, uuid, tt.wantUUID)
   234→			}
   235→		})
   236→	}
   237→}

   239→// Token calculation tests - Claude Code specific token format

   241→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_BasicMessages(t *testing.T) {
   242→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   243→		{
   244→			Type: "assistant",
   245→			UUID: "asst-1",
   246→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   247→				"id": "msg_001",
   248→				"usage": map[string]int{
   249→					"input_tokens":                10,
   250→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   251→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   252→					"output_tokens":               20,
   253→				},
   254→			}),
   255→		},
   256→		{
   257→			Type: "assistant",
   258→			UUID: "asst-2",
   259→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   260→				"id": "msg_002",
   261→				"usage": map[string]int{
   262→					"input_tokens":                5,
   263→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 200,
   264→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     0,
   265→					"output_tokens":               30,
   266→				},
   267→			}),
   268→		},
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   272→
   273→	if usage.APICallCount != 2 {
   274→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 2", usage.APICallCount)
   275→	}
   276→	if usage.InputTokens != 15 {
   277→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 15", usage.InputTokens)
   278→	}
   279→	if usage.CacheCreationTokens != 300 {
   280→		t.Errorf("CacheCreationTokens = %d, want 300", usage.CacheCreationTokens)
   281→	}
   282→	if usage.CacheReadTokens != 50 {
   283→		t.Errorf("CacheReadTokens = %d, want 50", usage.CacheReadTokens)
   284→	}
   285→	if usage.OutputTokens != 50 {
   286→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 50", usage.OutputTokens)
   287→	}
   288→}

   290→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_StreamingDeduplication(t *testing.T) {
   291→	// Simulate streaming: multiple rows with same message ID, increasing output_tokens
   292→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   293→		{
   294→			Type: "assistant",
   295→			UUID: "asst-1",
   296→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   297→				"id": "msg_001",
   298→				"usage": map[string]int{
   299→					"input_tokens":                10,
   300→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   301→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   302→					"output_tokens":               1, // First streaming chunk
   303→				},
   304→			}),
   305→		},
   306→		{
   307→			Type: "assistant",
   308→			UUID: "asst-2",
   309→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   310→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   311→				"usage": map[string]int{
   312→					"input_tokens":                10,
   313→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   314→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   315→					"output_tokens":               5, // More output
   316→				},
   317→			}),
   318→		},
   319→		{
   320→			Type: "assistant",
   321→			UUID: "asst-3",
   322→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   323→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   324→				"usage": map[string]int{
   325→					"input_tokens":                10,
   326→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   327→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   328→					"output_tokens":               20, // Final output
   329→				},
   330→			}),
   331→		},
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   335→
   336→	// Should deduplicate to 1 API call with the highest output_tokens
   337→	if usage.APICallCount != 1 {
   338→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 1 (should deduplicate by message ID)", usage.APICallCount)
   339→	}
   340→	if usage.OutputTokens != 20 {
   341→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 20 (should take highest)", usage.OutputTokens)
   342→	}
   343→	// Input/cache tokens should not be duplicated
   344→	if usage.InputTokens != 10 {
   345→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 10", usage.InputTokens)
   346→	}
   347→}

   494→func TestExtractAgentIDFromText(t *testing.T) {
   495→	tests := []struct {
   496→		name     string
   497→		text     string
   498→		expected string
   499→	}{
   500→		{
   501→			name:     "standard format",
   502→			text:     "agentId: ac66d4b (for resuming)",
   503→			expected: "ac66d4b",
   504→		},
   505→		{
   506→			name:     "at end of text",
   507→			text:     "Result text\n\nagentId: abc1234",
   508→			expected: "abc1234",
   509→		},
   510→		{
   511→			name:     "no agent ID",
   512→			text:     "Some text without agent ID",
   513→			expected: "",
   514→		},
   515→		{
   516→			name:     "empty text",
   517→			text:     "",
   518→			expected: "",
   519→		},
   520→		{
   521→			name:     "agent ID with newline after",
   522→			text:     "agentId: xyz9999\nMore text",
   523→			expected: "xyz9999",
   524→		},
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	for _, tt := range tests {
   528→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   529→			got := extractAgentIDFromText(tt.text)
   530→			if got != tt.expected {
   531→				t.Errorf("extractAgentIDFromText(%q) = %q, want %q", tt.text, got, tt.expected)
   532→			}
   533→		})
   534→	}
   535→}

   537→// mustMarshal is a test helper that marshals a value to JSON or fails the test
   538→func mustMarshal(t *testing.T, v interface{}) []byte {
   539→	t.Helper()
   540→	data, err := json.Marshal(v)
   541→	if err != nil {
   542→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)
   543→	}
   544→	return data
   545→}
   546→
   547→// TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint verifies token usage
   548→// is calculated per-checkpoint, not from the full conversation.
   549→// This tests the core CalculateTotalTokenUsage function which should:
   550→// - From line 0: count all turns
   551→// - From line N: count only turns from line N onwards
   552→func TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint(t *testing.T) {
   553→	t.Parallel()
   554→
   555→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   556→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   557→
   558→	// Build transcript with 3 turns:
   559→	// Turn 1: user + assistant (100 input, 50 output)
   560→	// Turn 2: user + assistant (200 input, 100 output)
   561→	// Turn 3: user + assistant (300 input, 150 output)
   562→	//
   563→	// Lines:
   564→	// 0: user message 1
   565→	// 1: assistant response 1 (100/50 tokens)
   566→	// 2: user message 2
   567→	// 3: assistant response 2 (200/100 tokens)
   568→	// 4: user message 3
   569→	// 5: assistant response 3 (300/150 tokens)
   570→
   571→	transcriptContent := []byte(
   572→		`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   573→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"id":"m1","usage":{"input_tokens":100,"output_tokens":50}}}` + "\n" +
   574→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   575→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"id":"m2","usage":{"input_tokens":200,"output_tokens":100}}}` + "\n" +
   576→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u3","message":{"content":"third prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   577→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a3","message":{"id":"m3","usage":{"input_tokens":300,"output_tokens":150}}}` + "\n",
   578→	)
   579→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, transcriptContent, 0o600); err != nil {
   580→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Test 1: From line 0 - all 3 turns = 600 input, 300 output
   584→	usage1, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 0, "")
   585→	if err != nil {
   586→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(0) error: %v", err)
   587→	}
   588→	if usage1.InputTokens != 600 || usage1.OutputTokens != 300 {
   589→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got input=%d output=%d, want input=600 output=300",
   590→			usage1.InputTokens, usage1.OutputTokens)
   591→	}
   592→	if usage1.APICallCount != 3 {
   593→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got APICallCount=%d, want 3", usage1.APICallCount)
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Test 2: From line 2 (after turn 1) - turns 2+3 only = 500 input, 250 output
   597→	usage2, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 2, "")
   598→	if err != nil {
   599→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(2) error: %v", err)
   600→	}
   601→	if usage2.InputTokens != 500 || usage2.OutputTokens != 250 {
   602→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got input=%d output=%d, want input=500 output=250",
   603→			usage2.InputTokens, usage2.OutputTokens)
   604→	}
   605→	if usage2.APICallCount != 2 {
   606→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got APICallCount=%d, want 2", usage2.APICallCount)
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Test 3: From line 4 (after turns 1+2) - turn 3 only = 300 input, 150 output
   610→	usage3, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 4, "")
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(4) error: %v", err)
   613→	}
   614→	if usage3.InputTokens != 300 || usage3.OutputTokens != 150 {
   615→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got input=%d output=%d, want input=300 output=150",
   616→			usage3.InputTokens, usage3.OutputTokens)
   617→	}
   618→	if usage3.APICallCount != 1 {
   619→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got APICallCount=%d, want 1", usage3.APICallCount)
   620→	}
   621→}
   622→
   623→// writeJSONLFile is a test helper that writes JSONL transcript lines to a file.
   624→func writeJSONLFile(t *testing.T, path string, lines ...string) {
   625→	t.Helper()
   626→	var buf strings.Builder
   627→	for _, line := range lines {
   628→		buf.WriteString(line)
   629→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
   630→	}
   631→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(buf.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   632→		t.Fatalf("failed to write JSONL file %s: %v", path, err)
   633→	}
   634→}
   635→
   636→// makeWriteToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Write tool_use for the given file.
   637→func makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
   638→	t.Helper()
   639→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   640→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   641→			{
   642→				"type":  "tool_use",
   643→				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
   644→				"name":  "Write",
   645→				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
   646→			},
   647→		},
   648→	})
   649→	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   650→		"type":    "assistant",
   651→		"uuid":    uuid,
   652→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   653→	})
   654→	return string(line)
   655→}

   699→// makeTaskResultLine returns a JSONL user line with a tool_result containing agentId.

   719→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_IncludesSubagentFiles(t *testing.T) {
   720→	t.Parallel()
   721→
   722→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   723→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   724→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   725→
   726→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   727→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   728→	}
   729→
   730→	// Main transcript: Write to main.go + Task call spawning subagent "sub1"
   731→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   732→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/main.go"),
   733→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   734→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   735→	)
   736→
   737→	// Subagent transcript: Write to helper.go + Edit to utils.go
   738→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   739→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/helper.go"),
   740→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/utils.go"),
   741→	)
   742→
   743→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   744→	if err != nil {
   745→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   746→	}
   747→
   748→	if len(files) != 3 {
   749→		t.Errorf("expected 3 files, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
   753→		"/repo/main.go":   true,
   754→		"/repo/helper.go": true,
   755→		"/repo/utils.go":  true,
   756→	}
   757→	for _, f := range files {
   758→		if !wantFiles[f] {
   759→			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
   760→		}
   761→		delete(wantFiles, f)
   762→	}
   763→	for f := range wantFiles {
   764→		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
   765→	}
   766→}

   768→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_DeduplicatesAcrossAgents(t *testing.T) {
   769→	t.Parallel()
   770→
   771→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   772→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   773→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   774→
   775→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   776→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   777→	}
   778→
   779→	// Main transcript: Write to shared.go + Task call
   780→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   781→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   782→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   783→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   784→	)
   785→
   786→	// Subagent transcript: Also modifies shared.go (same file as main)
   787→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   788→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   789→	)
   790→
   791→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	if len(files) != 1 {
   797→		t.Errorf("expected 1 file (deduplicated), got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   798→	}
   799→	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/shared.go" {
   800→		t.Errorf("expected /repo/shared.go, got %q", files[0])
   801→	}
   802→}
   803→
   804→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_NoSubagents(t *testing.T) {
   805→	t.Parallel()
   806→
   807→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   808→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   809→
   810→	// Main transcript: Write to a file, no Task calls
   811→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   812→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/solo.go"),
   813→	)
   814→
   815→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, tmpDir+"/nonexistent")
   816→	if err != nil {
   817→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	if len(files) != 1 {
   821→		t.Errorf("expected 1 file, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   822→	}
   823→	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/solo.go" {
   824→		t.Errorf("expected /repo/solo.go, got %q", files[0])
   825→	}
   826→}
   827→
   828→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {
   829→	t.Parallel()
   830→
   831→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   832→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   833→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   834→
   835→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   836→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   837→	}
   838→
   839→	// Main transcript: ONLY a Task call, no direct file modifications
   840→	// This is the key bug scenario - if we only look at the main transcript,
   841→	// we miss all the subagent's file changes entirely.
   842→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   843→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a1", "toolu_task1"),
   844→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   845→	)

   847→	// Subagent transcript: Write to two files
   848→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",

   850→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/subagent_file2.go"),
   851→	)
   852→
   853→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   854→	if err != nil {

   856→	}
   857→
   858→	if len(files) != 2 {

   861→
   862→	wantFiles := map[string]bool{

   864→		"/repo/subagent_file2.go": true,
   865→	}
   866→	for _, f := range files {

   869→		}
   870→		delete(wantFiles, f)
   871→	}
   872→	for f := range wantFiles {
   873→		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
   874→	}

    12→func TestParseTranscript(t *testing.T) {
    13→	t.Parallel()
    14→
    15→	data := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"hello"}}
    16→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"hi"}]}}
    17→`)
    18→
    19→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
    20→	if err != nil {
    21→		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
    22→	}
    23→
    24→	if len(lines) != 2 {
    25→		t.Errorf("ParseTranscript() got %d lines, want 2", len(lines))
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	if lines[0].Type != transcript.TypeUser || lines[0].UUID != "u1" {
    29→		t.Errorf("First line = %+v, want type=user, uuid=u1", lines[0])
    30→	}
    31→
    32→	if lines[1].Type != transcript.TypeAssistant || lines[1].UUID != "a1" {
    33→		t.Errorf("Second line = %+v, want type=assistant, uuid=a1", lines[1])
    34→	}
    35→}

    86→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a4","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"foo.go"}}]}}

    99→

   114→	}

   117→func TestExtractLastUserPrompt(t *testing.T) {
   118→	t.Parallel()
   119→
   120→	tests := []struct {
   121→		name string
   122→		data string
   123→		want string
   124→	}{
   125→		{
   126→			name: "string content",
   127→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first"}}
   128→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[]}}
   129→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second"}}`,
   130→			want: "second",
   131→		},
   132→		{
   133→			name: "array content with text block",
   134→			data: `{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"hello world"}]}}`,
   135→			want: "hello world",
   136→		},
   137→		{
   138→			name: "empty transcript",
   139→			data: ``,
   140→			want: "",
   141→		},
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	for _, tt := range tests {
   145→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   146→			t.Parallel()
   147→			lines, err := ParseTranscript([]byte(tt.data))
   148→			if err != nil && tt.data != "" {
   149→				t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
   150→			}
   151→			got := ExtractLastUserPrompt(lines)
   152→			if got != tt.want {
   153→				t.Errorf("ExtractLastUserPrompt() = %q, want %q", got, tt.want)
   154→			}
   155→		})
   156→	}
   157→}

   159→func TestTruncateAtUUID(t *testing.T) {
   160→	t.Parallel()
   161→
   162→	data := []byte(`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{}}
   163→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{}}
   164→{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{}}
   165→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{}}
   166→`)
   167→
   168→	lines, err := ParseTranscript(data)
   169→	if err != nil {
   170→		t.Fatalf("ParseTranscript() error = %v", err)
   171→	}
   172→
   173→	tests := []struct {
   174→		name     string
   175→		uuid     string
   176→		wantLen  int
   177→		lastUUID string
   178→	}{
   179→		{"truncate at u1", "u1", 1, "u1"},
   180→		{"truncate at a1", "a1", 2, "a1"},
   181→		{"truncate at u2", "u2", 3, "u2"},
   182→		{"truncate at a2", "a2", 4, "a2"},
   183→		{"empty uuid returns all", "", 4, "a2"},
   184→		{"unknown uuid returns all", "unknown", 4, "a2"},
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	for _, tt := range tests {
   188→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   189→			t.Parallel()
   190→			truncated := TruncateAtUUID(lines, tt.uuid)
   191→			if len(truncated) != tt.wantLen {
   192→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) got %d lines, want %d", tt.uuid, len(truncated), tt.wantLen)
   193→			}
   194→			if len(truncated) > 0 && truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID != tt.lastUUID {
   195→				t.Errorf("TruncateAtUUID(%q) last UUID = %q, want %q", tt.uuid, truncated[len(truncated)-1].UUID, tt.lastUUID)
   196→			}
   197→		})
   198→	}
   199→}

   203→
   204→	data := []byte(`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"tool1"}]}}
   205→{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"tool1"}]}}

   215→	tests := []struct {

   218→		wantFound bool

   235→		})
   236→	}

   241→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_BasicMessages(t *testing.T) {
   242→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   243→		{
   244→			Type: "assistant",
   245→			UUID: "asst-1",
   246→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   247→				"id": "msg_001",
   248→				"usage": map[string]int{
   249→					"input_tokens":                10,
   250→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   251→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   252→					"output_tokens":               20,
   253→				},
   254→			}),
   255→		},
   256→		{
   257→			Type: "assistant",
   258→			UUID: "asst-2",
   259→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   260→				"id": "msg_002",
   261→				"usage": map[string]int{
   262→					"input_tokens":                5,
   263→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 200,
   264→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     0,
   265→					"output_tokens":               30,
   266→				},
   267→			}),
   268→		},
   269→	}
   270→
   271→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   272→
   273→	if usage.APICallCount != 2 {
   274→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 2", usage.APICallCount)
   275→	}
   276→	if usage.InputTokens != 15 {
   277→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 15", usage.InputTokens)
   278→	}
   279→	if usage.CacheCreationTokens != 300 {
   280→		t.Errorf("CacheCreationTokens = %d, want 300", usage.CacheCreationTokens)
   281→	}
   282→	if usage.CacheReadTokens != 50 {
   283→		t.Errorf("CacheReadTokens = %d, want 50", usage.CacheReadTokens)
   284→	}
   285→	if usage.OutputTokens != 50 {
   286→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 50", usage.OutputTokens)
   287→	}
   288→}

   290→func TestCalculateTokenUsage_StreamingDeduplication(t *testing.T) {
   291→	// Simulate streaming: multiple rows with same message ID, increasing output_tokens
   292→	transcript := []TranscriptLine{
   293→		{
   294→			Type: "assistant",
   295→			UUID: "asst-1",
   296→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   297→				"id": "msg_001",
   298→				"usage": map[string]int{
   299→					"input_tokens":                10,
   300→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   301→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   302→					"output_tokens":               1, // First streaming chunk
   303→				},
   304→			}),
   305→		},
   306→		{
   307→			Type: "assistant",
   308→			UUID: "asst-2",
   309→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   310→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   311→				"usage": map[string]int{
   312→					"input_tokens":                10,
   313→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   314→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   315→					"output_tokens":               5, // More output
   316→				},
   317→			}),
   318→		},
   319→		{
   320→			Type: "assistant",
   321→			UUID: "asst-3",
   322→			Message: mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   323→				"id": "msg_001", // Same message ID
   324→				"usage": map[string]int{
   325→					"input_tokens":                10,
   326→					"cache_creation_input_tokens": 100,
   327→					"cache_read_input_tokens":     50,
   328→					"output_tokens":               20, // Final output
   329→				},
   330→			}),
   331→		},
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	usage := CalculateTokenUsage(transcript)
   335→
   336→	// Should deduplicate to 1 API call with the highest output_tokens
   337→	if usage.APICallCount != 1 {
   338→		t.Errorf("APICallCount = %d, want 1 (should deduplicate by message ID)", usage.APICallCount)
   339→	}
   340→	if usage.OutputTokens != 20 {
   341→		t.Errorf("OutputTokens = %d, want 20 (should take highest)", usage.OutputTokens)
   342→	}
   343→	// Input/cache tokens should not be duplicated
   344→	if usage.InputTokens != 10 {
   345→		t.Errorf("InputTokens = %d, want 10", usage.InputTokens)
   346→	}
   347→}

   494→func TestExtractAgentIDFromText(t *testing.T) {
   495→	tests := []struct {
   496→		name     string
   497→		text     string
   498→		expected string
   499→	}{
   500→		{
   501→			name:     "standard format",
   502→			text:     "agentId: ac66d4b (for resuming)",
   503→			expected: "ac66d4b",
   504→		},
   505→		{
   506→			name:     "at end of text",
   507→			text:     "Result text\n\nagentId: abc1234",
   508→			expected: "abc1234",
   509→		},
   510→		{
   511→			name:     "no agent ID",
   512→			text:     "Some text without agent ID",
   513→			expected: "",
   514→		},
   515→		{
   516→			name:     "empty text",
   517→			text:     "",
   518→			expected: "",
   519→		},
   520→		{
   521→			name:     "agent ID with newline after",
   522→			text:     "agentId: xyz9999\nMore text",
   523→			expected: "xyz9999",
   524→		},
   525→	}
   526→
   527→	for _, tt := range tests {
   528→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   529→			got := extractAgentIDFromText(tt.text)
   530→			if got != tt.expected {
   531→				t.Errorf("extractAgentIDFromText(%q) = %q, want %q", tt.text, got, tt.expected)
   532→			}
   533→		})
   534→	}
   535→}
   536→
   537→// mustMarshal is a test helper that marshals a value to JSON or fails the test
   538→func mustMarshal(t *testing.T, v interface{}) []byte {
   539→	t.Helper()
   540→	data, err := json.Marshal(v)
   541→	if err != nil {
   542→		t.Fatalf("failed to marshal: %v", err)
   543→	}
   544→	return data
   545→}
   546→
   547→// TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint verifies token usage
   548→// is calculated per-checkpoint, not from the full conversation.
   549→// This tests the core CalculateTotalTokenUsage function which should:
   550→// - From line 0: count all turns
   551→// - From line N: count only turns from line N onwards
   552→func TestCalculateTotalTokenUsage_PerCheckpoint(t *testing.T) {
   553→	t.Parallel()
   554→
   555→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   556→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   557→
   558→	// Build transcript with 3 turns:
   559→	// Turn 1: user + assistant (100 input, 50 output)
   560→	// Turn 2: user + assistant (200 input, 100 output)
   561→	// Turn 3: user + assistant (300 input, 150 output)
   562→	//
   563→	// Lines:
   564→	// 0: user message 1
   565→	// 1: assistant response 1 (100/50 tokens)
   566→	// 2: user message 2
   567→	// 3: assistant response 2 (200/100 tokens)
   568→	// 4: user message 3
   569→	// 5: assistant response 3 (300/150 tokens)
   570→
   571→	transcriptContent := []byte(
   572→		`{"type":"user","uuid":"u1","message":{"content":"first prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   573→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a1","message":{"id":"m1","usage":{"input_tokens":100,"output_tokens":50}}}` + "\n" +
   574→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u2","message":{"content":"second prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   575→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a2","message":{"id":"m2","usage":{"input_tokens":200,"output_tokens":100}}}` + "\n" +
   576→			`{"type":"user","uuid":"u3","message":{"content":"third prompt"}}` + "\n" +
   577→			`{"type":"assistant","uuid":"a3","message":{"id":"m3","usage":{"input_tokens":300,"output_tokens":150}}}` + "\n",
   578→	)
   579→	if err := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, transcriptContent, 0o600); err != nil {
   580→		t.Fatalf("failed to write transcript: %v", err)
   581→	}
   582→
   583→	// Test 1: From line 0 - all 3 turns = 600 input, 300 output
   584→	usage1, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 0, "")
   585→	if err != nil {
   586→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(0) error: %v", err)
   587→	}
   588→	if usage1.InputTokens != 600 || usage1.OutputTokens != 300 {
   589→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got input=%d output=%d, want input=600 output=300",
   590→			usage1.InputTokens, usage1.OutputTokens)
   591→	}
   592→	if usage1.APICallCount != 3 {
   593→		t.Errorf("From line 0: got APICallCount=%d, want 3", usage1.APICallCount)
   594→	}
   595→
   596→	// Test 2: From line 2 (after turn 1) - turns 2+3 only = 500 input, 250 output
   597→	usage2, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 2, "")
   598→	if err != nil {
   599→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(2) error: %v", err)
   600→	}
   601→	if usage2.InputTokens != 500 || usage2.OutputTokens != 250 {
   602→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got input=%d output=%d, want input=500 output=250",
   603→			usage2.InputTokens, usage2.OutputTokens)
   604→	}
   605→	if usage2.APICallCount != 2 {
   606→		t.Errorf("From line 2: got APICallCount=%d, want 2", usage2.APICallCount)
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Test 3: From line 4 (after turns 1+2) - turn 3 only = 300 input, 150 output
   610→	usage3, err := CalculateTotalTokenUsage(transcriptPath, 4, "")
   611→	if err != nil {
   612→		t.Fatalf("CalculateTotalTokenUsage(4) error: %v", err)
   613→	}
   614→	if usage3.InputTokens != 300 || usage3.OutputTokens != 150 {
   615→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got input=%d output=%d, want input=300 output=150",
   616→			usage3.InputTokens, usage3.OutputTokens)
   617→	}
   618→	if usage3.APICallCount != 1 {
   619→		t.Errorf("From line 4: got APICallCount=%d, want 1", usage3.APICallCount)
   620→	}
   621→}
   622→
   623→// writeJSONLFile is a test helper that writes JSONL transcript lines to a file.
   624→func writeJSONLFile(t *testing.T, path string, lines ...string) {
   625→	t.Helper()
   626→	var buf strings.Builder
   627→	for _, line := range lines {
   628→		buf.WriteString(line)
   629→		buf.WriteByte('\n')
   630→	}
   631→	if err := os.WriteFile(path, []byte(buf.String()), 0o600); err != nil {
   632→		t.Fatalf("failed to write JSONL file %s: %v", path, err)
   633→	}
   634→}
   635→
   636→// makeWriteToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Write tool_use for the given file.
   637→func makeWriteToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
   638→	t.Helper()
   639→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   640→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   641→			{
   642→				"type":  "tool_use",
   643→				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
   644→				"name":  "Write",
   645→				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
   646→			},
   647→		},
   648→	})
   649→	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   650→		"type":    "assistant",
   651→		"uuid":    uuid,
   652→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   653→	})
   654→	return string(line)
   655→}
   656→
   657→// makeEditToolLine returns a JSONL assistant line with an Edit tool_use for the given file.
   658→func makeEditToolLine(t *testing.T, uuid, filePath string) string {
   659→	t.Helper()
   660→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   661→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   662→			{
   663→				"type":  "tool_use",
   664→				"id":    "toolu_" + uuid,
   665→				"name":  "Edit",
   666→				"input": map[string]string{"file_path": filePath},
   667→			},
   668→		},
   669→	})
   670→	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   671→		"type":    "assistant",
   672→		"uuid":    uuid,
   673→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   674→	})
   675→	return string(line)
   676→}
   677→
   678→// makeTaskToolUseLine returns a JSONL assistant line with a Task tool_use (spawning a subagent).
   679→func makeTaskToolUseLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID string) string {
   680→	t.Helper()
   681→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   682→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   683→			{
   684→				"type":  "tool_use",
   685→				"id":    toolUseID,
   686→				"name":  "Task",
   687→				"input": map[string]string{"prompt": "do something"},

   693→		"uuid":    uuid,
   694→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   695→	})
   696→	return string(line)
   697→}
   698→
   699→// makeTaskResultLine returns a JSONL user line with a tool_result containing agentId.
   700→func makeTaskResultLine(t *testing.T, uuid, toolUseID, agentID string) string {
   701→	t.Helper()
   702→	data := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   703→		"content": []map[string]interface{}{
   704→			{
   705→				"type":        "tool_result",
   706→				"tool_use_id": toolUseID,
   707→				"content":     "agentId: " + agentID,
   708→			},

   710→	})
   711→	line := mustMarshal(t, map[string]interface{}{
   712→		"type":    "user",
   713→		"uuid":    uuid,
   714→		"message": json.RawMessage(data),
   715→	})
   716→	return string(line)
   717→}

   719→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_IncludesSubagentFiles(t *testing.T) {
   720→	t.Parallel()
   721→
   722→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   723→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   724→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   725→
   726→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   727→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   728→	}
   729→
   730→	// Main transcript: Write to main.go + Task call spawning subagent "sub1"
   731→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   732→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/main.go"),
   733→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   734→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   735→	)
   736→
   737→	// Subagent transcript: Write to helper.go + Edit to utils.go
   738→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   739→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/helper.go"),
   740→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/utils.go"),
   741→	)
   742→
   743→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   744→	if err != nil {
   745→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   746→	}
   747→
   748→	if len(files) != 3 {
   749→		t.Errorf("expected 3 files, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   750→	}
   751→
   752→	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
   753→		"/repo/main.go":   true,
   754→		"/repo/helper.go": true,
   755→		"/repo/utils.go":  true,
   756→	}
   757→	for _, f := range files {
   758→		if !wantFiles[f] {
   759→			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
   760→		}
   761→		delete(wantFiles, f)
   762→	}
   763→	for f := range wantFiles {
   764→		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
   765→	}
   766→}
   767→
   768→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_DeduplicatesAcrossAgents(t *testing.T) {
   769→	t.Parallel()
   770→
   771→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   772→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   773→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   774→
   775→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   776→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   777→	}
   778→
   779→	// Main transcript: Write to shared.go + Task call
   780→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   781→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   782→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a2", "toolu_task1"),
   783→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   784→	)
   785→
   786→	// Subagent transcript: Also modifies shared.go (same file as main)
   787→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   788→		makeEditToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/shared.go"),
   789→	)
   790→
   791→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   792→	if err != nil {
   793→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	if len(files) != 1 {
   797→		t.Errorf("expected 1 file (deduplicated), got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   798→	}
   799→	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/shared.go" {
   800→		t.Errorf("expected /repo/shared.go, got %q", files[0])
   801→	}
   802→}
   803→
   804→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_NoSubagents(t *testing.T) {
   805→	t.Parallel()
   806→
   807→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   808→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   809→
   810→	// Main transcript: Write to a file, no Task calls
   811→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   812→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "a1", "/repo/solo.go"),
   813→	)
   814→
   815→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, tmpDir+"/nonexistent")
   816→	if err != nil {
   817→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   818→	}
   819→
   820→	if len(files) != 1 {
   821→		t.Errorf("expected 1 file, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   822→	}
   823→	if len(files) > 0 && files[0] != "/repo/solo.go" {
   824→		t.Errorf("expected /repo/solo.go, got %q", files[0])
   825→	}
   826→}
   827→
   828→func TestExtractAllModifiedFiles_SubagentOnlyChanges(t *testing.T) {
   829→	t.Parallel()
   830→
   831→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   832→	transcriptPath := tmpDir + "/transcript.jsonl"
   833→	subagentsDir := tmpDir + "/tasks/toolu_task1"
   834→
   835→	if err := os.MkdirAll(subagentsDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   836→		t.Fatalf("failed to create subagents dir: %v", err)
   837→	}
   838→
   839→	// Main transcript: ONLY a Task call, no direct file modifications
   840→	// This is the key bug scenario - if we only look at the main transcript,
   841→	// we miss all the subagent's file changes entirely.
   842→	writeJSONLFile(t, transcriptPath,
   843→		makeTaskToolUseLine(t, "a1", "toolu_task1"),
   844→		makeTaskResultLine(t, "u1", "toolu_task1", "sub1"),
   845→	)
   846→
   847→	// Subagent transcript: Write to two files
   848→	writeJSONLFile(t, subagentsDir+"/agent-sub1.jsonl",
   849→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa1", "/repo/subagent_file1.go"),
   850→		makeWriteToolLine(t, "sa2", "/repo/subagent_file2.go"),
   851→	)
   852→
   853→	files, err := ExtractAllModifiedFiles(transcriptPath, 0, subagentsDir)
   854→	if err != nil {
   855→		t.Fatalf("ExtractAllModifiedFiles() error: %v", err)
   856→	}
   857→
   858→	if len(files) != 2 {
   859→		t.Errorf("expected 2 files from subagent, got %d: %v", len(files), files)
   860→	}
   861→
   862→	wantFiles := map[string]bool{
   863→		"/repo/subagent_file1.go": true,
   864→		"/repo/subagent_file2.go": true,
   865→	}
   866→	for _, f := range files {
   867→		if !wantFiles[f] {
   868→			t.Errorf("unexpected file %q in result", f)
   869→		}
   870→		delete(wantFiles, f)
   871→	}
   872→	for f := range wantFiles {
   873→		t.Errorf("missing expected file %q", f)
   874→	}
   875→}

   230→	filtered := filterTranscriptAfterUUID(transcript, lastUUID)
   231→	if len(filtered) != 1 {
   232→		t.Fatalf("expected 1 entry after last user, got %d", len(filtered))
   233→	}
   234→	if filtered[0].UUID != "a2" {
   235→		t.Errorf("expected filtered to contain 'a2', got '%s'", filtered[0].UUID)
   236→	}
   237→}

   239→func createTempTranscript(t *testing.T, content string) string {
   240→	t.Helper()
   241→	tmpDir := t.TempDir()
   242→	tmpFile := filepath.Join(tmpDir, "transcript.jsonl")
   243→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   244→		t.Fatalf("failed to create temp file: %v", err)
   245→	}
   246→	return tmpFile
   247→}

   280→func TestFindCheckpointUUID(t *testing.T) {
   281→	transcript := []transcriptLine{
   282→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u1", Message: []byte(`{"content":"First prompt"}`)},
   283→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a1", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_task1","name":"Task","input":{}}]}`)},
   284→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_task1","content":"Task completed"}]}`)},
   285→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done"}]}`)},
   286→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a3", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_task2","name":"Task","input":{}}]}`)},
   287→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u3", Message: []byte(`{"content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"toolu_task2","content":"Second task done"}]}`)},
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	tests := []struct {
   291→		name      string
   292→		toolUseID string
   293→		wantUUID  string
   294→		wantFound bool
   295→	}{
   296→		{
   297→			name:      "find first task result",
   298→			toolUseID: "toolu_task1",
   299→			wantUUID:  "u2",
   300→			wantFound: true,
   301→		},
   302→		{
   303→			name:      "find second task result",
   304→			toolUseID: "toolu_task2",
   305→			wantUUID:  "u3",
   306→			wantFound: true,
   307→		},
   308→		{
   309→			name:      "non-existent tool use ID",
   310→			toolUseID: "toolu_nonexistent",
   311→			wantUUID:  "",
   312→			wantFound: false,
   313→		},
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	for _, tt := range tests {
   317→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   318→			gotUUID, gotFound := FindCheckpointUUID(transcript, tt.toolUseID)
   319→			if gotFound != tt.wantFound {
   320→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID() found = %v, want %v", gotFound, tt.wantFound)
   321→			}
   322→			if gotUUID != tt.wantUUID {
   323→				t.Errorf("FindCheckpointUUID() uuid = %v, want %v", gotUUID, tt.wantUUID)
   324→			}
   325→		})
   326→	}
   327→}

   329→func TestTruncateTranscriptAtUUID(t *testing.T) {
   330→	transcript := []transcriptLine{
   331→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u1", Message: []byte(`{"content":"First"}`)},
   332→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a1", Message: []byte(`{"content":[]}`)},
   333→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u2", Message: []byte(`{"content":"Second"}`)},
   334→		{Type: "assistant", UUID: "a2", Message: []byte(`{"content":[]}`)},
   335→		{Type: "user", UUID: "u3", Message: []byte(`{"content":"Third"}`)},
   336→	}
   337→
   338→	tests := []struct {
   339→		name         string
   340→		uuid         string
   341→		expectedLen  int
   342→		expectedLast string
   343→	}{
   344→		{
   345→			name:         "truncate at u2",
   346→			uuid:         "u2",
   347→			expectedLen:  3,
   348→			expectedLast: "u2",
   349→		},
   350→		{
   351→			name:         "truncate at first",
   352→			uuid:         "u1",
   353→			expectedLen:  1,
   354→			expectedLast: "u1",
   355→		},
   356→		{
   357→			name:         "truncate at last",
   358→			uuid:         "u3",
   359→			expectedLen:  5,
   360→			expectedLast: "u3",
   361→		},
   362→		{
   363→			name:         "uuid not found - return all",
   364→			uuid:         "nonexistent",
   365→			expectedLen:  5,
   366→			expectedLast: "u3",
   367→		},
   368→		{
   369→			name:         "empty uuid - return all",
   370→			uuid:         "",
   371→			expectedLen:  5,
   372→			expectedLast: "u3",
   373→		},
   374→	}
   375→
   376→	for _, tt := range tests {
   377→		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
   378→			result := TruncateTranscriptAtUUID(transcript, tt.uuid)
   379→			if len(result) != tt.expectedLen {
   380→				t.Errorf("TruncateTranscriptAtUUID() len = %d, want %d", len(result), tt.expectedLen)
   381→			}
   382→			if len(result) > 0 && result[len(result)-1].UUID != tt.expectedLast {
   383→				t.Errorf("TruncateTranscriptAtUUID() last UUID = %s, want %s", result[len(result)-1].UUID, tt.expectedLast)
   384→			}
   385→		})
   386→	}
   387→}

   415→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_EntireFile(t *testing.T) {
   416→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"Hello"}}
   417→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Hi"}]}}

   430→	}

   436→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_Offset(t *testing.T) {
   437→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"Line1"}}
   438→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Line2"}]}}
   439→{"type":"user","uuid":"user-2","message":{"content":"Line3"}}
   440→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-2","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Line4"}]}}`
   441→
   442→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)
   443→
   444→	// Parse from line 2 (0-indexed, so skip first 2 lines)
   445→	lines, totalLines, err := parseTranscriptFromLine(tmpFile, 2)
   446→	if err != nil {
   447→		t.Fatalf("unexpected error: %v", err)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	if totalLines != 4 {
   451→		t.Errorf("totalLines = %d, want 4", totalLines)
   452→	}
   453→	if len(lines) != 2 {
   454→		t.Errorf("len(lines) = %d, want 2 (lines after offset)", len(lines))
   455→	}
   456→
   457→	// Verify we got the correct lines (user-2 and asst-2)
   458→	if len(lines) >= 1 && lines[0].UUID != "user-2" {
   459→		t.Errorf("first line UUID = %q, want 'user-2'", lines[0].UUID)
   460→	}
   461→	if len(lines) >= 2 && lines[1].UUID != "asst-2" {
   462→		t.Errorf("second line UUID = %q, want 'asst-2'", lines[1].UUID)
   463→	}
   464→}

   486→func TestParseTranscriptFromLine_SkipsMalformedLines(t *testing.T) {
   487→	content := `{"type":"user","uuid":"user-1","message":{"content":"Hello"}}
   488→invalid json line
   489→{"type":"assistant","uuid":"asst-1","message":{"content":[{"type":"text","text":"Hi"}]}}
   490→{"type":"user","uuid":"user-2","message":{"content":"Bye"}}`

   492→	tmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)

   501→	if totalLines != 4 {

   504→	// But parsed lines excludes malformed
   505→	if len(lines) != 2 {
   506→		t.Errorf("len(lines) = %d, want 2 (valid lines after offset)", len(lines))
   507→	}
   508→}

     1→package transcript
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"bufio"
     5→	"bytes"
     6→	"encoding/json"
     7→	"fmt"
     8→	"io"
     9→	"os"
    10→	"strings"
    11→
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/textutil"
    13→)
    14→
    15→// ParseFromBytes parses transcript content from a byte slice.
    16→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines.
    17→func ParseFromBytes(content []byte) ([]Line, error) {
    18→	var lines []Line
    19→	reader := bufio.NewReader(bytes.NewReader(content))
    20→
    21→	for {
    22→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    23→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    24→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    25→		}
    26→
    27→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    28→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    29→			if err == io.EOF {
    30→				break
    31→			}
    32→			continue
    33→		}
    34→
    35→		var line Line
    36→		if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    37→			lines = append(lines, line)
    38→		}
    39→
    40→		if err == io.EOF {
    41→			break
    42→		}
    43→	}
    44→
    45→	return lines, nil
    46→}
    47→
    48→// ParseFromFileAtLine reads and parses a transcript file starting from a specific line.
    49→// Uses bufio.Reader to handle arbitrarily long lines (no size limit).
    50→// Returns:
    51→//   - lines: parsed transcript lines from startLine onwards (malformed lines skipped)
    52→//   - totalLines: total number of lines in the file (including malformed ones)
    53→//   - error: any error encountered during reading
    54→//
    55→// The startLine parameter is 0-indexed (startLine=0 reads from the beginning).
    56→// This is useful for incremental parsing when you've already processed some lines.
    57→func ParseFromFileAtLine(path string, startLine int) ([]Line, int, error) {
    58→	file, err := os.Open(path) //nolint:gosec // path is a controlled transcript file path
    59→	if err != nil {
    60→		return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to open transcript: %w", err)
    61→	}
    62→	defer func() { _ = file.Close() }()
    63→
    64→	var lines []Line
    65→	reader := bufio.NewReader(file)
    66→
    67→	totalLines := 0
    68→	for {
    69→		lineBytes, err := reader.ReadBytes('\n')
    70→		if err != nil && err != io.EOF {
    71→			return nil, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
    72→		}
    73→
    74→		// Handle empty line or EOF without content
    75→		if len(lineBytes) == 0 {
    76→			if err == io.EOF {
    77→				break
    78→			}
    79→			continue
    80→		}
    81→
    82→		// Count all lines for totalLines, but only parse after startLine
    83→		if totalLines >= startLine {
    84→			var line Line
    85→			if err := json.Unmarshal(lineBytes, &line); err == nil {
    86→				lines = append(lines, line)
    87→			}
    88→		}
    89→		totalLines++
    90→
    91→		if err == io.EOF {
    92→			break
    93→		}
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	return lines, totalLines, nil
    97→}
    98→
    99→// SliceFromLine returns the content starting from line number `startLine` (0-indexed).
   100→// This is used to extract only the checkpoint-specific portion of a cumulative transcript.
   101→// For example, if startLine is 2, lines 0 and 1 are skipped and the result starts at line 2.
   102→// Returns empty slice if startLine exceeds the number of lines.
   103→func SliceFromLine(content []byte, startLine int) []byte {
   104→	if len(content) == 0 || startLine <= 0 {
   105→		return content
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	// Find the byte offset where startLine begins
   109→	lineCount := 0
   110→	offset := 0
   111→	for i, b := range content {
   112→		if b == '\n' {
   113→			lineCount++
   114→			if lineCount == startLine {
   115→				offset = i + 1
   116→				break
   117→			}
   118→		}
   119→	}
   120→
   121→	// If we didn't find enough lines, return empty
   122→	if lineCount < startLine {
   123→		return nil
   124→	}
   125→
   126→	// If offset is beyond content, return empty
   127→	if offset >= len(content) {
   128→		return nil
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	return content[offset:]
   132→}
   133→
   134→// ExtractUserContent extracts user content from a raw message.
   135→// Handles both string and array content formats.
   136→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the result.
   137→// Returns empty string if the message cannot be parsed or contains no text.
   138→func ExtractUserContent(message json.RawMessage) string {
   139→	var msg UserMessage
   140→	if err := json.Unmarshal(message, &msg); err != nil {
   141→		return ""
   142→	}
   143→
   144→	// Handle string content
   145→	if str, ok := msg.Content.(string); ok {
   146→		return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(str)
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	// Handle array content (only if it contains text blocks)
   150→	if arr, ok := msg.Content.([]interface{}); ok {
   151→		var texts []string
   152→		for _, item := range arr {
   153→			if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   154→				if m["type"] == ContentTypeText {
   155→					if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   156→						texts = append(texts, text)
   157→					}
   158→				}
   159→			}
   160→		}
   161→		if len(texts) > 0 {
   162→			return textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   163→		}
   164→	}
   165→
   166→	return ""
   167→}
   168→
```

`{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Hello\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hi there\"}]}}\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Thanks\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif totalLines != 3 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"totalLines = %d, want 3\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 3 lines, got %d\", len(lines))\n\t}\n\n\tif lines[0].Type != \"user\" || lines[0].UUID != \"user-1\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"first line mismatch: got type=%s uuid=%s\",
