`{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Hello\"}}\nthis is not valid json\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hi\"}]}}\n{\"broken json\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Bye\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, _, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(lines) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 3 valid lines, got %d\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_LargeLines(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t// Create a line with content larger than default scanner buffer (64KB)\n\tlargeContent := strings.Repeat(\"x\", 100*1024) // 100KB\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"` + largeContent + `\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, _, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error parsing large line: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(lines) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 line, got %d\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_LineExceedsScannerBuffer(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t// Create a line larger than 10MB. bufio.Reader handles this without any size limit.\n\tlargeContent := strings.Repeat(\"x\", 11*1024*1024) // 11MB\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"` + largeContent + `\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, _, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error parsing line exceeding buffer: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif len(lines) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 line, got %d\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_LineExceedsScannerBufferWithOffset(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\t// Same test with offset - should handle lines > 10MB\n\tlargeContent := strings.Repeat(\"x\", 11*1024*1024) // 11MB\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"` + largeContent + `\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error parsing line exceeding buffer: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif totalLines != 1 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"expected totalLines=1, got %d\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) != 1 {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"expected 1 parsed line, got %d\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_EntireFile(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Hello\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hi\"}]}}\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Bye\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 0)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif totalLines != 3 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"totalLines = %d, want 3\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) != 3 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"len(lines) = %d, want 3\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_Offset(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Line1\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Line2\"}]}}\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Line3\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Line4\"}]}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\t// Parse from line 2 (0-indexed, so skip first 2 lines)\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 2)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif totalLines != 4 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"totalLines = %d, want 4\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"len(lines) = %d, want 2 (lines after offset)\", len(lines))\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify we got the correct lines (user-2 and asst-2)\n\tif len(lines) >= 1 && lines[0].UUID != \"user-2\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"first line UUID = %q, want 'user-2'\", lines[0].UUID)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) >= 2 && lines[1].UUID != \"asst-2\" {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"second line UUID = %q, want 'asst-2'\", lines[1].UUID)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc TestParseFromFileAtLine_OffsetBeyondEnd(t *testing.T) {\n\tt.Parallel()\n\n\tcontent := `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Hello\"}}\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hi\"}]}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\t// Parse from line 10 (beyond end of file)\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 10)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif totalLines != 2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"totalLines = %d, want 2\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\tif len(lines) != 0 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"len(lines) = %d, want 0 (no lines after offset)\", `{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Hello\"}}\ninvalid json line\n{\"type\":\"assistant\",\"uuid\":\"asst-1\",\"message\":{\"content\":[{\"type\":\"text\",\"text\":\"Hi\"}]}}\n{\"type\":\"user\",\"uuid\":\"user-2\",\"message\":{\"content\":\"Bye\"}}`\n\n\ttmpFile := createTempTranscript(t, content)\n\n\t// Parse from line 1 (skip first valid line)\n\tlines, totalLines, err := ParseFromFileAtLine(tmpFile, 1)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"unexpected error: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Total lines counts ALL lines including malformed\n\tif totalLines != 4 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"totalLines = %d, want 4\", totalLines)\n\t}\n\t// But parsed lines excludes malformed\n\tif len(lines) != 2 {\n\t\tt.Errorf(\"len(lines) = %d, want 2 (valid lines after offset)\", len(lines))\n\t}\n}"

## Dead Code Report - Transcript Parsing Consolidation

### Summary of Findings

After the transcript parsing consolidation, I found **significant dead code issues** in `/Users/gtrrz-victor/wks/cli/cli/cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go`. The file uses types and constants that are no longer defined after the deleted functions were removed.

---

### 1. **Missing Type Aliases in `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go`**

**Location:** `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go`  
**Severity:** CRITICAL - Code will not compile

The file uses the following types extensively but **they are not defined in this file**:
- `transcriptLine` (used 16+ times across functions like `ExtractAllPromptResponses`, `extractUserPrompts`, `extractLastUserPrompt`, etc.)
- `userMessage` (used ~6 times)
- `assistantMessage` (used ~5 times)
- `toolInput` (used ~3 times)

**Where they ARE defined:**
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` (lines 18-22) defines them as type aliases:
  ```go
  type (
      userMessage      = transcript.UserMessage
      assistantMessage = transcript.AssistantMessage
      toolInput        = transcript.ToolInput
  )
  ```
- The `transcript` package (`cmd/entire/cli/transcript/types.go`) defines: `UserMessage`, `AssistantMessage`, `ToolInput`, `Line`

**What's missing:**
- No type alias for `transcriptLine` which should map to `transcript.Line` (formerly a local type)
- No type aliases for `userMessage`, `assistantMessage`, `toolInput` in `transcript.go`

**Functions affected:**
- `ExtractAllPromptResponses()` (line 36)
- `extractUserPromptAt()` (line 95)
- `extractAssistantResponses()` (line 105)
- `extractUserPrompts()` (line 127)
- `extractLastUserPrompt()` (line 167)
- `extractLastAssistantMessage()` (line 176)
- `findLastUserUUID()` (line 195)
- `filterTranscriptAfterUUID()` (line 212)
- `extractModifiedFiles()` (line 233)
- `extractKeyActions()` (line 279)
- `FindCheckpointUUID()` (line 340)
- `TruncateTranscriptAtUUID()` (line 365)
- `writeTranscript()` (line 381)
- `GetTranscriptPosition()` (line 413)

---

### 2. **Unused Imports in `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go`**

**Location:** Lines 4-8  
**Status:** LIKELY UNUSED after consolidation

- `"bufio"` - Used only in `GetTranscriptPosition()` (line 428) which calls `bufio.NewReader(file)`
- `"io"` - Used only in `GetTranscriptPosition()` for `io.EOF` constant
- `"os"` - Used in `GetTranscriptPosition()` for `os.Open()` and `os.IsNotExist()`

**Analysis:**
- These imports may still be needed if `GetTranscriptPosition()` is still actively used
- Need to verify if file-reading parsing functions remain or if this is dead code

---

### 3. **Type Duplication - `toolResultBlock` and `userMessageWithToolResults`**

**Location:** 
- `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go` (lines 326-334)
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` (lines 145-153)

**Status:** CODE DUPLICATION

Both files define identical helper types:
```go
type toolResultBlock struct {
    Type      string `json:"type"`
    ToolUseID string `json:"tool_use_id"`
}

type userMessageWithToolResults struct {
    Content []toolResultBlock `json:"content"`
}
```

These types are used only in:
- `FindCheckpointUUID()` in `transcript.go` (line 346)
- `FindCheckpointUUID()` in `claudecode/transcript.go` (line 157)

**Functions using them:**
- `FindCheckpointUUID()` in both files (nearly identical implementations)

---

### 4. **Unused/Dead Helper Functions in `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go`**

**Status:** QUESTIONABLE - May be dead or still used

The following functions use the deleted parsing functions and may be dead:

- `extractUserPrompts()` (lines 127-162) - Only called by `extractLastUserPrompt()`
- `extractLastUserPrompt()` (lines 167-173) - Only called by extracting user prompts for display
- `findLastUserUUID()` (lines 195-209) - Used by `filterTranscriptAfterUUID()`
- `filterTranscriptAfterUUID()` (lines 212-230) - Check if used anywhere
- `extractKeyActions()` (lines 279-316) - Used in `createContextFileMinimal()` in `rewind.go`

---

### 5. **Missing Type Definition for `transcriptLine`**

**Analysis:**
Looking at the consolidated code in `transcript/parse.go`, the type `Line` is now defined in the `transcript` package. All instances of `transcriptLine` should be replaced with `transcript.Line`.

**Current state:**
- `cmd/entire/cli/transcript.go` uses local type `transcriptLine` (undefined)
- `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/transcript.go` defines `TranscriptLine = transcript.Line` (correct)
- The consolidated parser now works with `transcript.Line` directly

---

### 6. **Unused Constants Check**

The constant aliases at the top of `transcript.go` (lines 18-23) are actively used:

> AGENT

Yes, there's clear duplication:

- **`FindCheckpointUUID`**: `cli/transcript.go` (called from `hooks_claudecode_handlers.go`) AND `claudecode/transcript.go` (called from `claude.go`)
- **`TruncateTranscriptAtUUID`** / **`TruncateAtUUID`**: `cli/transcript.go` (called from `rewind.go`) AND `claudecode/transcript.go` (called from `claude.go`)
- **`toolResultBlock` + `userMessageWithToolResults`** types: duplicated in both files

These are functionally identical - both operate on `transcript.Line` slices. The `cli` versions could be removed and callers switched to the `claudecode` versions, or both moved to the shared `transcript` package.
